Transformer-Based Inpainting for Real-Time 3D Streaming in Sparse Multi-Camera Setups
Authors: Leif Van Holland, Domenic Zingsheim, Mana Takhsha, Hannah Dröge, Patrick Stotko, Markus Plack, Reinhard Klein
First: 2026-03-05T18:59:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:59:59+00:00
Comments: You can find the project page https://github.com/vc-bonn/transformer-based-inpainting
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
High-quality 3D streaming from multiple cameras is crucial for immersive experiences in many AR/VR applications. The limited number of views - often due to real-time constraints - leads to missing information and incomplete surfaces in the rendered images. Existing approaches typically rely on simple heuristics for the hole filling, which can result in inconsistencies or visual artifacts. We propose to complete the missing textures using a novel, application-targeted inpainting method independent of the underlying representation as an image-based post-processing step after the novel view rendering. The method is designed as a standalone module compatible with any calibrated multi-camera system. For this we introduce a multi-view aware, transformer-based network architecture using spatio-temporal embeddings to ensure consistency across frames while preserving fine details. Additionally, our resolution-independent design allows adaptation to different camera setups, while an adaptive patch selection strategy balances inference speed and quality, allowing real-time performance. We evaluate our approach against state-of-the-art inpainting techniques under the same real-time constraints and demonstrate that our model achieves the best trade-off between quality and speed, outperforming competitors in both image and video-based metrics.
Summary / 总结
To address the issue of incomplete 3D surfaces in real-time multi-camera streaming due to limited viewpoints, this work introduces a transformer-based inpainting method designed as a standalone post-processing module. The architecture leverages spatio-temporal embeddings to maintain consistency across frames while preserving fine details, and its resolution-independent design enables compatibility with various camera setups. An adaptive patch selection strategy balances speed and quality, enabling real-time performance. Experimental results show that the method outperforms state-of-the-art inpainting techniques in both image and video metrics under real-time constraints, achieving the best quality-speed trade-off.
为解决多摄像头实时流媒体中因视角有限导致的3D表面缺失问题，本文提出一种基于Transformer的修复方法，作为独立于底层3D表示的图像后处理模块。该架构利用时空嵌入确保帧间一致性并保留细节，支持任意分辨率的相机配置，通过自适应块选择策略平衡推理速度与质量，实现实时性能。实验表明，在实时约束下，该方法在图像与视频指标上均优于现有技术，达成最佳的质量-速度平衡。
FaceCam: Portrait Video Camera Control via Scale-Aware Conditioning
Authors: Weijie Lyu, Ming-Hsuan Yang, Zhixin Shu
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-05T18:59:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:59:58+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR 2026. Project page: https://weijielyu.github.io/FaceCam
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We introduce FaceCam, a system that generates video under customizable camera trajectories for monocular human portrait video input. Recent camera control approaches based on large video-generation models have shown promising progress but often exhibit geometric distortions and visual artifacts on portrait videos due to scale-ambiguous camera representations or 3D reconstruction errors. To overcome these limitations, we propose a face-tailored scale-aware representation for camera transformations that provides deterministic conditioning without relying on 3D priors. We train a video generation model on both multi-view studio captures and in-the-wild monocular videos, and introduce two camera-control data generation strategies: synthetic camera motion and multi-shot stitching, to exploit stationary training cameras while generalizing to dynamic, continuous camera trajectories at inference time. Experiments on Ava-256 dataset and diverse in-the-wild videos demonstrate that FaceCam achieves superior performance in camera controllability, visual quality, identity and motion preservation.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the geometric distortions and visual artifacts in portrait video generation caused by scale-ambiguous camera representations, FaceCam introduces a face-tailored scale-aware conditioning method that enables deterministic camera control without 3D priors. The system trains a video generation model on both studio multi-view and in-the-wild monocular data, using synthetic camera motion and multi-shot stitching to bridge stationary training with dynamic inference trajectories. Experimental results on Ava-256 and diverse real-world videos show FaceCam outperforms existing methods in camera controllability, visual fidelity, identity preservation, and motion consistency.
为解决现有方法在人像视频生成中因尺度模糊导致的几何失真与视觉伪影问题，FaceCam提出一种面向人脸的尺度感知条件表示，实现无需3D先验的确定性相机控制。该系统在多视角棚拍与野外单目视频数据上训练视频生成模型，并通过合成相机运动与多帧拼接策略，实现从静态训练到动态推理轨迹的泛化。实验表明，FaceCam在相机可控性、视觉质量、身份与运动保持方面均优于现有方法。
RoboPocket: Improve Robot Policies Instantly with Your Phone
Authors: Junjie Fang, Wendi Chen, Han Xue, Fangyuan Zhou, Tian Le, Yi Wang, Yuting Zhang, Jun Lv, Chuan Wen, Cewu Lu
First: 2026-03-05T18:59:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:59:38+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://robo-pocket.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Scaling imitation learning is fundamentally constrained by the efficiency of data collection. While handheld interfaces have emerged as a scalable solution for in-the-wild data acquisition, they predominantly operate in an open-loop manner: operators blindly collect demonstrations without knowing the underlying policy's weaknesses, leading to inefficient coverage of critical state distributions. Conversely, interactive methods like DAgger effectively address covariate shift but rely on physical robot execution, which is costly and difficult to scale. To reconcile this trade-off, we introduce RoboPocket, a portable system that enables Robot-Free Instant Policy Iteration using single consumer smartphones. Its core innovation is a Remote Inference framework that visualizes the policy's predicted trajectory via Augmented Reality (AR) Visual Foresight. This immersive feedback allows collectors to proactively identify potential failures and focus data collection on the policy's weak regions without requiring a physical robot. Furthermore, we implement an asynchronous Online Finetuning pipeline that continuously updates the policy with incoming data, effectively closing the learning loop in minutes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RoboPocket adheres to data scaling laws and doubles the data efficiency compared to offline scaling strategies, overcoming their long-standing efficiency bottleneck. Moreover, our instant iteration loop also boosts sample efficiency by up to 2$\times$ in distributed environments a small number of interactive corrections per person. Project page and videos: https://robo-pocket.github.io.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of data collection in imitation learning, RoboPocket enables instant policy improvement using only a smartphone by bridging the gap between open-loop human demonstration and costly interactive robot execution. It employs a Remote Inference framework that visualizes predicted robot trajectories via AR to guide human collectors toward policy weaknesses, allowing targeted data collection without physical robot involvement. An asynchronous Online Finetuning pipeline then continuously updates the policy with new data, closing the learning loop rapidly. Experiments show RoboPocket scales efficiently with data, doubling data efficiency over offline methods and improving sample efficiency by up to two times in distributed settings with minimal human interaction.
为解决模仿学习中数据收集效率低的问题，RoboPocket 利用智能手机实现无需机器人即可即时优化策略，弥合了开放循环人工演示与昂贵机器人交互之间的鸿沟。其核心是远程推理框架，通过增强现实可视化预测轨迹，引导人类收集者主动聚焦策略薄弱区域，无需实际机器人参与。异步在线微调管道持续整合新数据更新策略，快速闭合学习循环。实验表明，RoboPocket 遵循数据扩展规律，数据效率较离线方法提升一倍，并在分布式环境中通过少量人工修正即可提升样本效率最高达两倍。
Accelerating Text-to-Video Generation with Calibrated Sparse Attention
Authors: Shai Yehezkel, Shahar Yadin, Noam Elata, Yaron Ostrovsky-Berman, Bahjat Kawar
First: 2026-03-05T18:59:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:59:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent diffusion models enable high-quality video generation, but suffer from slow runtimes. The large transformer-based backbones used in these models are bottlenecked by spatiotemporal attention. In this paper, we identify that a significant fraction of token-to-token connections consistently yield negligible scores across various inputs, and their patterns often repeat across queries. Thus, the attention computation in these cases can be skipped with little to no effect on the result. This observation continues to hold for connections among local token blocks. Motivated by this, we introduce CalibAtt, a training-free method that accelerates video generation via calibrated sparse attention. CalibAtt performs an offline calibration pass that identifies block-level sparsity and repetition patterns that are stable across inputs, and compiles these patterns into optimized attention operations for each layer, head, and diffusion timestep. At inference time, we compute the selected input-dependent connections densely, and skip the unselected ones in a hardware-efficient manner. Extensive experiments on Wan 2.1 14B, Mochi 1, and few-step distilled models at various resolutions show that CalibAtt achieves up to 1.58x end-to-end speedup, outperforming existing training-free methods while maintaining video generation quality and text-video alignment.
Summary / 总结
To address the slow inference speed of text-to-video diffusion models due to costly spatiotemporal attention, the authors propose CalibAtt, a training-free method that identifies and skips redundant token connections through offline calibration of block-level sparsity and repetition patterns. CalibAtt compiles optimized attention operations per layer, head, and timestep, then at inference time computes only the selected connections densely while skipping others efficiently. Experiments on multiple models and resolutions show CalibAtt delivers up to 1.58x end-to-end speedup without sacrificing video quality or text-video alignment, outperforming existing training-free acceleration techniques.
为解决文本到视频扩散模型因时空注意力计算缓慢的问题，作者提出CalibAtt方法，通过离线校准识别并跳过冗余的token连接，利用块级稀疏性和重复模式的稳定性，为每一层、每一头和每一步扩散编译优化的注意力操作。推理时仅密集计算选定的连接，其余跳过，实现硬件高效加速。实验表明，CalibAtt在多个模型和分辨率下最高可实现1.58倍端到端加速，且不降低视频质量或文本-视频对齐效果，优于现有无训练加速方法。
POET-X: Memory-efficient LLM Training by Scaling Orthogonal Transformation
Authors: Zeju Qiu, Lixin Liu, Adrian Weller, Han Shi, Weiyang Liu
First: 2026-03-05T18:59:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:59:23+00:00
Comments: Technical report v1 (14 pages, 7 figures, project page: https://spherelab.ai/poetx/)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Efficient and stable training of large language models (LLMs) remains a core challenge in modern machine learning systems. To address this challenge, Reparameterized Orthogonal Equivalence Training (POET), a spectrum-preserving framework that optimizes each weight matrix through orthogonal equivalence transformation, has been proposed. Although POET provides strong training stability, its original implementation incurs high memory consumption and computational overhead due to intensive matrix multiplications. To overcome these limitations, we introduce POET-X, a scalable and memory-efficient variant that performs orthogonal equivalence transformations with significantly reduced computational cost. POET-X maintains the generalization and stability benefits of POET while achieving substantial improvements in throughput and memory efficiency. In our experiments, POET-X enables the pretraining of billion-parameter LLMs on a single Nvidia H100 GPU, and in contrast, standard optimizers such as AdamW run out of memory under the same settings.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and stable training of large language models, this work introduces POET-X, a memory-efficient variant of the Reparameterized Orthogonal Equivalence Training framework. POET-X scales orthogonal transformations to reduce computational overhead while preserving spectrum-preserving properties, enabling training without excessive memory usage. Experimental results show that POET-X successfully trains billion-parameter LLMs on a single Nvidia H100 GPU, whereas standard optimizers like AdamW fail due to memory exhaustion under identical conditions.
为解决大语言模型训练中效率与稳定性不足的问题，本文提出POET-X，它是正交等价变换框架的内存优化版本。POET-X通过缩放正交变换降低计算开销，同时保持谱保持特性，从而显著减少内存占用。实验表明，POET-X可在单块Nvidia H100 GPU上成功训练十亿参数规模的模型，而标准优化器如AdamW在相同配置下因内存不足而失败。
The Spike, the Sparse and the Sink: Anatomy of Massive Activations and Attention Sinks
Authors: Shangwen Sun, Alfredo Canziani, Yann LeCun, Jiachen Zhu
First: 2026-03-05T18:59:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:59:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study two recurring phenomena in Transformer language models: massive activations, in which a small number of tokens exhibit extreme outliers in a few channels, and attention sinks, in which certain tokens attract disproportionate attention mass regardless of semantic relevance. Prior work observes that these phenomena frequently co-occur and often involve the same tokens, but their functional roles and causal relationship remain unclear. Through systematic experiments, we show that the co-occurrence is largely an architectural artifact of modern Transformer design, and that the two phenomena serve related but distinct functions. Massive activations operate globally: they induce near-constant hidden representations that persist across layers, effectively functioning as implicit parameters of the model. Attention sinks operate locally: they modulate attention outputs across heads and bias individual heads toward short-range dependencies. We identify the pre-norm configuration as the key choice that enables the co-occurrence, and show that ablating it causes the two phenomena to decouple.
Summary / 总结
We study two recurring phenomena in Transformer language models: massive activations, in which a small number of tokens exhibit extreme outliers in a few channels, and attention sinks, in which certain tokens attract disproportionate attention mass regardless of semantic relevance.
Safe-SAGE: Social-Semantic Adaptive Guidance for Safe Engagement through Laplace-Modulated Poisson Safety Functions
Authors: Lizhi Yang, Ryan M. Bena, Meg Wilkinson, Gilbert Bahati, Andy Navarro Brenes, Ryan K. Cosner, Aaron D. Ames
First: 2026-03-05T18:59:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:59:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Traditional safety-critical control methods, such as control barrier functions, suffer from semantic blindness, exhibiting the same behavior around obstacles regardless of contextual significance. This limitation leads to the uniform treatment of all obstacles, despite their differing semantic meanings. We present Safe-SAGE (Social-Semantic Adaptive Guidance for Safe Engagement), a unified framework that bridges the gap between high-level semantic understanding and low-level safety-critical control through a Poisson safety function (PSF) modulated using a Laplace guidance field. Our approach perceives the environment by fusing multi-sensor point clouds with vision-based instance segmentation and persistent object tracking to maintain up-to-date semantics beyond the camera's field of view. A multi-layer safety filter is then used to modulate system inputs to achieve safe navigation using this semantic understanding of the environment. This safety filter consists of both a model predictive control layer and a control barrier function layer. Both layers utilize the PSF and flux modulation of the guidance field to introduce varying levels of conservatism and multi-agent passing norms for different obstacles in the environment. Our framework enables legged robots to navigate semantically rich, dynamic environments with context-dependent safety margins while maintaining rigorous safety guarantees.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the semantic blindness of traditional safety-critical control methods that treat all obstacles uniformly, Safe-SAGE introduces a unified framework integrating high-level semantic perception with low-level safety control. It employs a Poisson safety function modulated by a Laplace guidance field to adapt safety margins based on obstacle semantics. The system fuses multi-sensor point clouds with vision-based instance segmentation and tracking to maintain semantic awareness beyond the camera’s field of view. A multi-layer safety filter—combining model predictive control and control barrier functions—uses the modulated guidance field to enforce context-dependent safety behaviors. Experimental results demonstrate that Safe-SAGE enables legged robots to navigate dynamic, semantically rich environments while maintaining rigorous safety guarantees and adapting conservatism levels for different obstacles.
为解决传统安全控制方法对障碍物语义无感知、统一处理的缺陷，Safe-SAGE提出融合高层语义理解与底层安全控制的统一框架。该方法采用泊松安全函数结合拉普拉斯引导场进行调制，根据障碍物语义动态调整安全裕度。系统通过融合多传感器点云与基于视觉的实例分割及持续目标跟踪，实现超出相机视场的语义感知。安全滤波器包含模型预测控制层与控制屏障函数层，均利用调制引导场实现不同障碍物的差异化保守性与多智能体通行规范。实验表明，该框架使足式机器人能在语义丰富、动态环境中安全导航，同时保持严格的安全保障并自适应调整安全策略。
Cheap Thrills: Effective Amortized Optimization Using Inexpensive Labels
Authors: Khai Nguyen, Petros Ellinas, Anvita Bhagavathula, Priya Donti
First: 2026-03-05T18:58:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:58:39+00:00
Comments: in submission
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
To scale the solution of optimization and simulation problems, prior work has explored machine-learning surrogates that inexpensively map problem parameters to corresponding solutions. Commonly used approaches, including supervised and self-supervised learning with either soft or hard feasibility enforcement, face inherent challenges such as reliance on expensive, high-quality labels or difficult optimization landscapes. To address their trade-offs, we propose a novel framework that first collects "cheap" imperfect labels, then performs supervised pretraining, and finally refines the model through self-supervised learning to improve overall performance. Our theoretical analysis and merit-based criterion show that labeled data need only place the model within a basin of attraction, confirming that only modest numbers of inexact labels and training epochs are required. We empirically validate our simple three-stage strategy across challenging domains, including nonconvex constrained optimization, power-grid operation, and stiff dynamical systems, and show that it yields faster convergence; improved accuracy, feasibility, and optimality; and up to 59x reductions in total offline cost.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost of generating accurate labels for optimization problems, this work proposes a three-stage framework that first uses inexpensive but imperfect labels for supervised pretraining, then refines the model via self-supervised learning to improve performance. Theoretical analysis supports that only modest label quality and training epochs are needed, as long as labels guide the model into a basin of attraction. Empirical results across nonconvex optimization, power-grid simulation, and stiff dynamics show faster convergence, better accuracy and feasibility, and up to 59x reduction in offline computational cost.
为解决优化与仿真问题规模化时对昂贵标签的依赖，本文提出三阶段框架：先收集低成本但不精确的标签，再进行监督预训练，最后通过自监督学习优化模型。理论分析表明，只要标签能将模型引导至吸引域内，少量不精确标签与训练轮次即可有效。实验在非凸约束优化、电网运行与刚性动力系统中验证，结果表明该方法收敛更快、精度与可行性更优，总离线成本最高可降低59倍。
Censored LLMs as a Natural Testbed for Secret Knowledge Elicitation
Authors: Helena Casademunt, Bartosz Cywiński, Khoi Tran, Arya Jakkli, Samuel Marks, Neel Nanda
First: 2026-03-05T18:58:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:58:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models sometimes produce false or misleading responses. Two approaches to this problem are honesty elicitation -- modifying prompts or weights so that the model answers truthfully -- and lie detection -- classifying whether a given response is false. Prior work evaluates such methods on models specifically trained to lie or conceal information, but these artificial constructions may not resemble naturally-occurring dishonesty. We instead study open-weights LLMs from Chinese developers, which are trained to censor politically sensitive topics: Qwen3 models frequently produce falsehoods about subjects like Falun Gong or the Tiananmen protests while occasionally answering correctly, indicating they possess knowledge they are trained to suppress. Using this as a testbed, we evaluate a suite of elicitation and lie detection techniques. For honesty elicitation, sampling without a chat template, few-shot prompting, and fine-tuning on generic honesty data most reliably increase truthful responses. For lie detection, prompting the censored model to classify its own responses performs near an uncensored-model upper bound, and linear probes trained on unrelated data offer a cheaper alternative. The strongest honesty elicitation techniques also transfer to frontier open-weights models including DeepSeek R1. Notably, no technique fully eliminates false responses. We release all prompts, code, and transcripts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：作为秘密知识提取自然实验平台的审查型大语言模型
大语言模型有时会产生虚假或误导性回答。解决此问题的两种方法是诚实提取——通过修改提示或权重使模型诚实作答——和谎言检测——判断给定回答是否为假。先前研究在专门训练用于撒谎或隐瞒信息的模型上评估这些方法，但这些人工构造可能无法反映自然发生的不诚实行为。我们转而研究中国开发者训练的开源权重大语言模型，这些模型被训练以审查政治敏感话题：Qwen3模型在涉及法轮功或天安门抗议等话题时频繁产生虚假信息，但偶尔也能正确作答，表明其掌握着被训练压制的知识。我们以此作为实验平台，评估一系列提取诚实回答和谎言检测技术。在诚实提取方面，无聊天模板采样、少样本提示和在通用诚实数据上微调最可靠地提升诚实回答率。在谎言检测方面，让被审查模型对其自身回答进行分类，其表现接近未审查模型的上限，而基于无关数据训练的线性探针则提供更廉价的替代方案。最强的诚实提取技术也适用于前沿开源权重模型，包括DeepSeek R1。值得注意的是，没有任何技术能完全消除虚假回答。我们公开所有提示、代码和转录记录。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate honesty elicitation and lie detection methods on naturally occurring deceptive behavior rather than artificial setups, the study uses censored Chinese open-weight LLMs like Qwen3, which frequently generate falsehoods about politically sensitive topics despite possessing factual knowledge. The researchers test various elicitation techniques—including sampling without chat templates, few-shot prompting, and fine-tuning on honesty data—and lie detection methods, such as self-classification and linear probes trained on unrelated data. Results show that sampling without chat templates and fine-tuning most reliably increase truthful responses, while self-classification of responses achieves near-uncensored performance for lie detection, with linear probes offering a cost-effective alternative. These methods also transfer to frontier models like DeepSeek R1, yet no technique fully eliminates false responses.
为评估诚实引导与谎言检测方法在自然发生欺骗行为中的有效性，研究者以中国开发者训练的、对敏感话题进行审查的开源大模型（如Qwen3）为测试平台，这些模型常在涉及政治敏感话题时生成虚假信息，但同时又保留真实知识。研究测试了多种诚实引导方法（如无聊天模板采样、少样本提示、基于通用诚实数据微调）和谎言检测方法（如模型自我分类、基于无关数据训练的线性探测器）。结果表明，无聊天模板采样和微调最有效提升真实回答率，自我分类检测效果接近未审查模型上限，线性探测器则提供低成本替代方案。这些方法亦可迁移至前沿开源模型如DeepSeek R1，但无任何方法能完全消除虚假回答。
cuRoboV2: Dynamics-Aware Motion Generation with Depth-Fused Distance Fields for High-DoF Robots
Authors: Balakumar Sundaralingam, Adithyavairavan Murali, Stan Birchfield
First: 2026-03-05T18:58:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:58:04+00:00
Comments: cuRoboV2 Technical Report
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Effective robot autonomy requires motion generation that is safe, feasible, and reactive. Current methods are fragmented: fast planners output physically unexecutable trajectories, reactive controllers struggle with high-fidelity perception, and existing solvers fail on high-DoF systems. We present cuRoboV2, a unified framework with three key innovations: (1) B-spline trajectory optimization that enforces smoothness and torque limits; (2) a GPU-native TSDF/ESDF perception pipeline that generates dense signed distance fields covering the full workspace, unlike existing methods that only provide distances within sparsely allocated blocks, up to 10x faster and in 8x less memory than the state-of-the-art at manipulation scale, with up to 99% collision recall; and (3) scalable GPU-native whole-body computation, namely topology-aware kinematics, differentiable inverse dynamics, and map-reduce self-collision, that achieves up to 61x speedup while also extending to high-DoF humanoids (where previous GPU implementations fail). On benchmarks, cuRoboV2 achieves 99.7% success under 3kg payload (where baselines achieve only 72--77%), 99.6% collision-free IK on a 48-DoF humanoid (where prior methods fail entirely), and 89.5% retargeting constraint satisfaction (vs. 61% for PyRoki); these collision-free motions yield locomotion policies with 21% lower tracking error than PyRoki and 12x lower cross-seed variance than mink. A ground-up codebase redesign for discoverability enabled LLM coding assistants to author up to 73% of new modules, including hand-optimized CUDA kernels, demonstrating that well-structured robotics code can unlock productive human--LLM collaboration. Together, these advances provide a unified, dynamics-aware motion generation stack that scales from single-arm manipulators to full humanoids.
中文标题/摘要
标题：cuRoboV2：面向高自由度机器人的动态感知运动生成方法
实现有效的机器人自主性需要安全、可行且反应迅速的运动生成。当前方法存在割裂：快速规划器输出物理不可执行的轨迹，反应式控制器难以处理高保真感知，现有求解器在高自由度系统上失效。我们提出cuRoboV2统一框架，包含三项关键创新：（1）B样条轨迹优化，强制平滑性和扭矩限制；（2）GPU原生TSDF/ESDF感知管道，生成覆盖完整工作空间的稠密有符号距离场，相较现有仅提供稀疏块内距离的方法，速度快10倍、内存占用低8倍，在操作尺度下碰撞召回率达99%；（3）可扩展的GPU原生全身计算，包括拓扑感知运动学、可微逆动力学及Map-Reduce自碰撞检测，实现最高61倍加速，并可扩展至高自由度人形机器人（此前GPU实现无法支持）。在基准测试中，cuRoboV2在3kg负载下成功率99.7%（基线仅72–77%），在48自由度人形机器人上实现99.6%无碰撞逆运动学（此前方法完全失败），约束重定向满足率89.5%（PyRoki为61%）；这些无碰撞运动使步态策略跟踪误差比PyRoki低21%，跨种子方差低12倍。从零重构的代码库设计提升了可发现性，使LLM编码助手可编写73%的新模块（含手优化CUDA内核），证明结构良好的机器人代码可促进人-LLM高效协作。综合而言，这些进展构建了统一、动态感知的运动生成栈，可从单臂操作器扩展至完整人形机器人。
Summary / 总结
To address the fragmentation in robot motion generation—where fast planners lack physical feasibility, reactive controllers suffer from poor perception, and solvers fail on high-DoF systems—cuRoboV2 introduces a unified GPU-native framework. It employs B-spline trajectory optimization to ensure smooth, torque-limited motion; a depth-fused TSDF/ESDF perception pipeline that generates dense signed distance fields across the full workspace, achieving 10x faster speed and 8x less memory than prior methods with 99% collision recall; and scalable whole-body computation including topology-aware kinematics and differentiable inverse dynamics, enabling 61x speedup and supporting high-DoF humanoids. Experiments show 99.7% success under 3kg payload (vs. 72–77% baselines), 99.6% collision-free IK on a 48-DoF humanoid (where prior methods fail), and 89.5% retargeting constraint satisfaction (vs. 61% for PyRoki), with 21% lower tracking error and 12x lower cross-seed variance than mink. The codebase’s structure also enabled LLMs to author 73% of new modules, including CUDA kernels, demonstrating the potential for human-LLM collaboration in robotics.
为解决机器人运动生成中规划器轨迹不可执行、控制器感知精度不足、求解器难以处理高自由度系统的问题，作者提出cuRoboV2统一框架。该框架采用B样条轨迹优化确保平滑性与扭矩限制，GPU原生TSDF/ESDF感知管道生成覆盖全工作空间的稠密有符号距离场（比现有方法快10倍、内存低8倍、碰撞召回率99%），并支持可扩展的全身计算（拓扑感知运动学、可微逆动力学、MapReduce自碰撞检测，提速61倍），可扩展至高自由度人形机器人。实验表明，cuRoboV2在3kg负载下成功率99.7%（基线仅72–77%），48自由度人形机器人上碰撞自由逆运动学成功率99.6%（此前方法完全失败），重定向约束满足率89.5%（PyRoki仅61%），轨迹跟踪误差低21%，跨种子方差低12倍。代码结构设计还支持LLM自动生成73%新模块（含手优化CUDA内核），验证了结构化机器人代码可促进人机协作。
AgentIR: Reasoning-Aware Retrieval for Deep Research Agents
Authors: Zijian Chen, Xueguang Ma, Shengyao Zhuang, Jimmy Lin, Akari Asai, Victor Zhong
First: 2026-03-04T18:47:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:56:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Deep Research agents are rapidly emerging as primary consumers of modern retrieval systems. Unlike human users who issue and refine queries without documenting their intermediate thought processes, Deep Research agents generate explicit natural language reasoning before each search call, revealing rich intent and contextual information that existing retrievers entirely ignore. To exploit this overlooked signal, we introduce: (1) Reasoning-Aware Retrieval, a retrieval paradigm that jointly embeds the agent's reasoning trace alongside its query; and (2) DR-Synth, a data synthesis method that generates Deep Research retriever training data from standard QA datasets. We demonstrate that both components are independently effective, and their combination yields a trained embedding model, AgentIR-4B, with substantial gains. On the challenging BrowseComp-Plus benchmark, AgentIR-4B achieves 68\% accuracy with the open-weight agent Tongyi-DeepResearch, compared to 50\% with conventional embedding models twice its size, and 37\% with BM25. Code and data are available at: https://texttron.github.io/AgentIR/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AgentIR：面向深度研究代理的推理感知检索
深度研究代理正迅速成为现代检索系统的首要用户。与人类用户发出并优化查询而无需记录中间思考过程不同，深度研究代理在每次搜索调用前会生成明确的自然语言推理，揭示丰富的意图和上下文信息，而现有检索器完全忽略这些信息。为利用这一被忽视的信号，我们提出：（1）推理感知检索，一种将代理推理轨迹与查询联合嵌入的检索范式；（2）DR-Synth，一种从标准问答数据集生成深度研究检索器训练数据的数据合成方法。我们证明这两个组件各自独立有效，其结合训练出的嵌入模型AgentIR-4B效果显著提升。在具有挑战性的BrowseComp-Plus基准测试中，AgentIR-4B在开放权重代理通义深度研究上达到68%准确率，相比同等大小两倍的常规嵌入模型（50%）和BM25（37%）均有显著提升。代码和数据可在：https://texttron.github.io/AgentIR/ 获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to better serve Deep Research agents that generate explicit reasoning before queries, this work introduces Reasoning-Aware Retrieval, a method that jointly encodes reasoning traces and queries into embeddings for improved retrieval. It also presents DR-Synth, a data synthesis technique to generate training data tailored for Deep Research agents from standard QA datasets. Experiments show that both components independently improve performance, and their combination yields AgentIR-4B, a 4-billion-parameter model that achieves 68% accuracy on BrowseComp-Plus using Tongyi-DeepResearch, outperforming conventional models of similar size (50%) and BM25 (37%).
为提升深度研究代理的检索效果，该研究提出“推理感知检索”方法，将代理的推理过程与查询联合嵌入以增强检索能力，并设计DR-Synth数据合成方法，从标准问答数据生成适配代理的训练数据。实验表明，两个组件均能独立提升性能，结合后生成的AgentIR-4B模型在BrowseComp-Plus基准上使用Tongyi-DeepResearch时准确率达68%，显著优于同等规模传统模型（50%）和BM25（37%）。
Reasoning Theater: Disentangling Model Beliefs from Chain-of-Thought
Authors: Siddharth Boppana, Annabel Ma, Max Loeffler, Raphael Sarfati, Eric Bigelow, Atticus Geiger, Owen Lewis, Jack Merullo
First: 2026-03-05T18:55:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:55:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We provide evidence of performative chain-of-thought (CoT) in reasoning models, where a model becomes strongly confident in its final answer, but continues generating tokens without revealing its internal belief. Our analysis compares activation probing, early forced answering, and a CoT monitor across two large models (DeepSeek-R1 671B & GPT-OSS 120B) and find task difficulty-specific differences: The model's final answer is decodable from activations far earlier in CoT than a monitor is able to say, especially for easy recall-based MMLU questions. We contrast this with genuine reasoning in difficult multihop GPQA-Diamond questions. Despite this, inflection points (e.g., backtracking, 'aha' moments) occur almost exclusively in responses where probes show large belief shifts, suggesting these behaviors track genuine uncertainty rather than learned "reasoning theater." Finally, probe-guided early exit reduces tokens by up to 80% on MMLU and 30% on GPQA-Diamond with similar accuracy, positioning attention probing as an efficient tool for detecting performative reasoning and enabling adaptive computation.
Summary / 总结
We provide evidence of performative chain-of-thought (CoT) in reasoning models, where a model becomes strongly confident in its final answer, but continues generating tokens without revealing its internal belief.
本研究探讨大型语言模型中表演式链式思维现象，即模型看似推理但实际在未暴露内部信念的情况下生成自信最终答案。通过对DeepSeek-R1 671B和GPT-OSS 120B模型使用激活探针、早期强制回答和CoT监控器进行对比，发现最终答案可在比监控器更早的激活阶段被解码——尤其在简单MMLU任务中。相比之下，在困难的GPQA-Diamond任务中，真实推理表现出回溯等转折点，这些转折点与探针检测到的信念变化高度一致，表明这些行为反映真实不确定性。基于探针引导的早期退出可将MMLU任务的输出token减少80%，GPQA-Diamond减少30%，且准确率基本不变，证明注意力探针能有效识别表演式推理并支持自适应计算。
Deep FlexQP: Accelerated Nonlinear Programming via Deep Unfolding
Authors: Alex Oshin, Rahul Vodeb Ghosh, Augustinos D. Saravanos, Evangelos A. Theodorou
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-12-01T11:38:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:54:48+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose FlexQP, an always-feasible convex quadratic programming (QP) solver based on an $\ell_1$ elastic relaxation of the QP constraints. If the original constraints are feasible, FlexQP provably recovers the optimal solution. If the constraints are infeasible, FlexQP identifies a solution that minimizes the constraint violation while keeping the number of violated constraints sparse. Such infeasibilities arise naturally in sequential quadratic programming (SQP) subproblems due to the linearization of the constraints. We prove the convergence of FlexQP under mild coercivity assumptions, making it robust to both feasible and infeasible QPs. We then apply deep unfolding to learn LSTM-based, dimension-agnostic feedback policies for the algorithm parameters, yielding an accelerated Deep FlexQP. To preserve the exactness guarantees of the relaxation, we propose a normalized training loss that incorporates the Lagrange multipliers. We additionally design a log-scaled loss for PAC-Bayes generalization bounds that yields substantially tighter performance certificates, which we use to construct an accelerated SQP solver with guaranteed QP subproblem performance. Deep FlexQP outperforms state-of-the-art learned QP solvers on a suite of benchmarks including portfolio optimization, classification, and regression problems, and scales to dense QPs with over 10k variables and constraints via fine-tuning. When deployed within SQP, our approach solves nonlinear trajectory optimization problems 4-16x faster than SQP with OSQP while substantially improving success rates. On predictive safety filter problems, Deep FlexQP reduces safety violations by over 70\% and increases task completion by 43\% compared to existing methods.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust and efficient solvers for nonlinear programming subproblems in sequential quadratic programming, the authors propose FlexQP, a convex quadratic programming solver that guarantees feasibility and optimal recovery under constraints, or sparse constraint violation when infeasible. They enhance it via deep unfolding to learn LSTM-based feedback policies for algorithm parameters, preserving exactness through a normalized loss incorporating Lagrange multipliers and using a log-scaled loss for tighter PAC-Bayes bounds. Experimental results show Deep FlexQP outperforms existing learned QP solvers across portfolio optimization, classification, and regression benchmarks, scales to dense problems with over 10k variables, and when embedded in SQP, accelerates nonlinear trajectory optimization by 4-16x while improving success rates and reducing safety violations by over 70%.
为提升非线性优化中凸二次规划求解器的鲁棒性和效率，作者提出FlexQP方法，通过$\ell_1$弹性松弛处理可行与不可行QP问题，保证最优解或稀疏违反约束的解。随后采用深度展开技术学习LSTM反馈策略，构建Deep FlexQP，使用结合拉格朗日乘子的归一化损失和对数尺度损失以获得更紧的PAC-Bayes泛化界。实验表明，Deep FlexQP在包括投资组合优化和回归等基准测试中优于现有学习型QP求解器，可扩展至超1万变量的密集问题，并在SQP框架下将非线性轨迹优化速度提升4-16倍，同时任务成功率提升、安全违规降低70%以上。
Observing and Controlling Features in Vision-Language-Action Models
Authors: Hugo Buurmeijer, Carmen Amo Alonso, Aiden Swann, Marco Pavone
First: 2026-03-05T18:53:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:53:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action Models (VLAs) have shown remarkable progress towards embodied intelligence. While their architecture partially resembles that of Large Language Models (LLMs), VLAs exhibit higher complexity due to their multi-modal inputs/outputs and often hybrid nature of transformer and diffusion heads. This is part of the reason why insights from mechanistic interpretability in LLMs, which explain how the internal model representations relate to their output behavior, do not trivially transfer to VLA counterparts. In this work, we propose to close this gap by introducing and analyzing two main concepts: feature-observability and feature-controllability. In particular, we first study features that are linearly encoded in representation space, and show how they can be observed by means of a linear classifier. Then, we use a minimal linear intervention grounded in optimal control to accurately place internal representations and steer the VLA's output towards a desired region. Our results show that targeted, lightweight interventions can reliably steer a robot's behavior while preserving closed-loop capabilities. We demonstrate on different VLA architectures ($π_{0.5}$ and OpenVLA) through simulation experiments that VLAs possess interpretable internal structure amenable to online adaptation without fine-tuning, enabling real-time alignment with user preferences and task requirements.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand and guide the behavior of Vision-Language-Action Models (VLAs), this work introduces two key concepts: feature-observability and feature-controllability. It first identifies linearly encoded features in VLA representations and demonstrates their observability via linear classifiers. Then, it applies minimal linear interventions based on optimal control to steer internal representations toward desired output regions. Experimental results on π₀.₅ and OpenVLA show that such lightweight interventions can reliably guide robot behavior in simulation while preserving closed-loop functionality, enabling real-time adaptation without fine-tuning.
为实现具身智能中视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）的可解释与可控，本文提出两个核心概念：特征可观测性与特征可控制性。首先，通过线性分类器观测表示空间中线性编码的特征；继而采用基于最优控制的最小线性干预，精准调整内部表征以引导模型输出至目标区域。实验表明，在π₀.₅与OpenVLA模型的仿真环境中，此类轻量干预可稳定引导机器人行为，同时保持闭环能力，无需微调即可实现实时偏好与任务对齐。
Towards Provably Unbiased LLM Judges via Bias-Bounded Evaluation
Authors: Benjamin Feuer, Lucas Rosenblatt, Oussama Elachqar
First: 2026-03-05T18:52:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:52:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
As AI models progress beyond simple chatbots into more complex workflows, we draw ever closer to the event horizon beyond which AI systems will be utilized in autonomous, self-maintaining feedback loops. Any autonomous AI system will depend on automated, verifiable rewards and feedback; in settings where ground truth is sparse or non-deterministic, one practical source of such rewards is an LLM-as-a-Judge. Although LLM judges continue to improve, the literature has yet to introduce systems capable of enforcing standards with strong guarantees, particularly when bias vectors are unknown or adversarially discovered. To remedy this issue, we propose average bias-boundedness (A-BB), an algorithmic framework which formally guarantees reductions of harm/impact as a result of any measurable bias in an LLM judge. Evaluating on Arena-Hard-Auto with four LLM judges, we achieve (tau=0.5, delta=0.01) bias-bounded guarantees while retaining 61-99% correlation with original rankings across formatting and schematic bias settings, with most judge-bias combinations exceeding 80%. The code to reproduce our findings is available at https://github.com/penfever/bias-bounded-evaluation.
Summary / 总结
As AI systems move toward autonomous feedback loops, reliable and unbiased evaluation by LLM judges becomes critical, especially when ground truth is scarce and bias vectors are unknown or adversarial. To address this, the authors introduce average bias-boundedness (A-BB), a framework that mathematically guarantees reduced harm from any measurable bias in LLM judges. Evaluated on Arena-Hard-Auto with four LLM judges, A-BB achieves (tau=0.5, delta=0.01) bias-bounded guarantees while preserving 61–99% correlation with original rankings across various bias conditions, with most judge-bias combinations exceeding 80% correlation.
随着AI系统向自主反馈循环演进，LLM裁判在缺乏真实标注时提供可靠且无偏的评估至关重要。为解决现有LLM裁判缺乏可证明偏倚控制的问题，作者提出平均偏倚有界性（A-BB）算法框架，该框架可形式化保证任何可测量偏倚带来的损害降低。在Arena-Hard-Auto数据集上对四个LLM裁判进行评估，A-BB在（tau=0.5，delta=0.01）偏倚有界保证下，仍保持61–99%的原始排序相关性，多数裁判偏倚组合相关性超过80%。
CBF-RL: Safety Filtering Reinforcement Learning in Training with Control Barrier Functions
Authors: Lizhi Yang, Blake Werner, Massimiliano de Sa, Aaron D. Ames
First: 2025-10-16T17:58:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:52:25+00:00
Comments: 8 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning (RL), while powerful and expressive, can often prioritize performance at the expense of safety. Yet safety violations can lead to catastrophic outcomes in real-world deployments. Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) offer a principled method to enforce dynamic safety -- traditionally deployed online via safety filters. While the result is safe behavior, the fact that the RL policy does not have knowledge of the CBF can lead to conservative behaviors. This paper proposes CBF-RL, a framework for generating safe behaviors with RL by enforcing CBFs in training. CBF-RL has two key attributes: (1) minimally modifying a nominal RL policy to encode safety constraints via a CBF term, (2) and safety filtering of the policy rollouts in training. Theoretically, we prove that continuous-time safety filters can be deployed via closed-form expressions on discrete-time roll-outs. Practically, we demonstrate that CBF-RL internalizes the safety constraints in the learned policy -- both enforcing safer actions and biasing towards safer rewards -- enabling safe deployment without the need for an online safety filter. We validate our framework through ablation studies on navigation tasks and on the Unitree G1 humanoid robot, where CBF-RL enables safer exploration, faster convergence, and robust performance under uncertainty, enabling the humanoid robot to avoid obstacles and climb stairs safely in real-world settings without a runtime safety filter.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CBF-RL：基于控制屏障函数的安全过滤强化学习训练
尽管强化学习（RL）具有强大且表达力强的特点，但其常优先考虑性能而牺牲安全性。然而，安全违规在实际部署中可能导致灾难性后果。控制屏障函数（CBFs）提供了一种原则性的方法来强制执行动态安全——传统上通过在线安全过滤器部署。虽然结果是安全行为，但RL策略缺乏CBF知识可能导致保守行为。本文提出CBF-RL框架，通过在训练中强制执行CBFs生成安全行为。CBF-RL具有两个关键特性：（1）通过添加CBF项最小化修改标准RL策略以编码安全约束；（2）在训练中对策略滚动进行安全过滤。理论上，我们证明连续时间安全过滤器可通过闭式表达式在离散时间滚动中部署。实践中，我们表明CBF-RL将安全约束内化于学习策略中——既强制执行更安全的动作，又偏向更安全的奖励——从而无需在线安全过滤器即可实现安全部署。我们通过导航任务和Unitree G1人形机器人的消融研究验证了该框架，CBF-RL实现了更安全的探索、更快的收敛和在不确定性下的鲁棒性能，使人形机器人无需运行时安全过滤器即可在现实环境中避障并安全攀爬楼梯。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk of safety violations in reinforcement learning due to its focus on performance, this paper introduces CBF-RL, a framework that integrates Control Barrier Functions into training to enforce safety without requiring online safety filters. The method minimally modifies the RL policy by incorporating a CBF term and applies safety filtering during training rollouts. Theoretical analysis shows that continuous-time safety filters can be approximated via closed-form expressions on discrete-time trajectories. Experiments on navigation tasks and the Unitree G1 humanoid robot demonstrate that CBF-RL leads to safer exploration, faster convergence, and robust performance under uncertainty, enabling the robot to avoid obstacles and climb stairs safely without runtime safety intervention.
为解决强化学习系统因追求性能而忽视安全导致的潜在风险，本文提出CBF-RL框架，通过在训练阶段集成控制屏障函数（CBF）来保障安全性，无需运行时安全过滤器。该方法通过在RL策略中加入CBF项并过滤训练轨迹，使策略能内化安全约束。理论分析表明，连续时间安全过滤器可通过离散时间轨迹的闭式表达近似实现。实验在导航任务和Unitree G1人形机器人上验证，结果表明CBF-RL能实现更安全的探索、更快收敛及对不确定性的鲁棒性，使机器人无需运行时安全过滤即可安全避障和爬楼梯。
Towards Multimodal Lifelong Understanding: A Dataset and Agentic Baseline
Authors: Guo Chen, Lidong Lu, Yicheng Liu, Liangrui Dong, Lidong Zou, Jixin Lv, Zhenquan Li, Xinyi Mao, Baoqi Pei, Shihao Wang, Zhiqi Li, Karan Sapra, Fuxiao Liu, Yin-Dong Zheng, Yifei Huang, Limin Wang, Zhiding Yu, Andrew Tao, Guilin Liu, Tong Lu
First: 2026-03-05T18:52:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:52:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While datasets for video understanding have scaled to hour-long durations, they typically consist of densely concatenated clips that differ from natural, unscripted daily life. To bridge this gap, we introduce MM-Lifelong, a dataset designed for Multimodal Lifelong Understanding. Comprising 181.1 hours of footage, it is structured across Day, Week, and Month scales to capture varying temporal densities. Extensive evaluations reveal two critical failure modes in current paradigms: end-to-end MLLMs suffer from a Working Memory Bottleneck due to context saturation, while representative agentic baselines experience Global Localization Collapse when navigating sparse, month-long timelines. To address this, we propose the Recursive Multimodal Agent (ReMA), which employs dynamic memory management to iteratively update a recursive belief state, significantly outperforming existing methods. Finally, we establish dataset splits designed to isolate temporal and domain biases, providing a rigorous foundation for future research in supervised learning and out-of-distribution generalization.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between scripted video datasets and natural daily life, the authors introduce MM-Lifelong, a 181.1-hour multimodal dataset structured across Day, Week, and Month scales to reflect real-world temporal density. They identify two key failures in current models: end-to-end MLLMs suffer from a Working Memory Bottleneck due to context saturation, and agentic baselines collapse under sparse, long-term timelines. To resolve this, they propose ReMA, a recursive agent that dynamically manages memory and updates a belief state iteratively, achieving superior performance over existing methods. The dataset also includes carefully designed splits to isolate temporal and domain biases, enabling rigorous evaluation of generalization.
为弥合现有视频数据集与真实日常生活的差距，作者构建了MM-Lifelong数据集，包含181.1小时的多模态视频，按日、周、月尺度组织以反映真实时间密度。实验发现当前方法存在两大缺陷：端到端MLLM因上下文饱和导致工作记忆瓶颈，而代理基线在稀疏的月级时间线上出现全局定位崩溃。为此，作者提出递归多模态代理ReMA，通过动态内存管理和递归信念状态更新显著提升性能。数据集还设计了隔离时间与领域偏差的划分，为未来监督学习与分布外泛化研究提供严谨基础。
SurvHTE-Bench: A Benchmark for Heterogeneous Treatment Effect Estimation in Survival Analysis
Authors: Shahriar Noroozizadeh, Xiaobin Shen, Jeremy C. Weiss, George H. Chen
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-03-05T18:52:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:52:02+00:00
Comments: The Fourteenth International Conference on Learning Representations (ICLR 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Estimating heterogeneous treatment effects (HTEs) from right-censored survival data is critical in high-stakes applications such as precision medicine and individualized policy-making. Yet, the survival analysis setting poses unique challenges for HTE estimation due to censoring, unobserved counterfactuals, and complex identification assumptions. Despite recent advances, from Causal Survival Forests to survival meta-learners and outcome imputation approaches, evaluation practices remain fragmented and inconsistent. We introduce SurvHTE-Bench, the first comprehensive benchmark for HTE estimation with censored outcomes. The benchmark spans (i) a modular suite of synthetic datasets with known ground truth, systematically varying causal assumptions and survival dynamics, (ii) semi-synthetic datasets that pair real-world covariates with simulated treatments and outcomes, and (iii) real-world datasets from a twin study (with known ground truth) and from an HIV clinical trial. Across synthetic, semi-synthetic, and real-world settings, we provide the first rigorous comparison of survival HTE methods under diverse conditions and realistic assumption violations. SurvHTE-Bench establishes a foundation for fair, reproducible, and extensible evaluation of causal survival methods. The data and code of our benchmark are available at: https://github.com/Shahriarnz14/SurvHTE-Bench .
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable heterogeneous treatment effect (HTE) estimation in survival analysis under censoring and complex causal assumptions, the authors introduce SurvHTE-Bench, the first comprehensive benchmark for evaluating HTE methods in survival settings. The benchmark includes synthetic datasets with known ground truth, semi-synthetic datasets combining real-world covariates with simulated treatments, and real-world datasets from twin and HIV clinical trials. Through rigorous comparisons across these settings, the study demonstrates that existing methods perform inconsistently under varying causal assumptions and censoring patterns, highlighting gaps in current approaches and establishing a standardized evaluation framework for future research.
为解决生存分析中右删失数据下异质性处理效应估计缺乏统一评估标准的问题，本文提出SurvHTE-Bench，首个面向生存分析异质性处理效应估计的综合性基准。该基准包含具有已知真实值的合成数据、结合真实协变量与模拟处理和结果的半合成数据，以及来自双生子研究和HIV临床试验的真实数据。通过在不同因果假设和删失条件下对现有方法（如因果生存森林、生存元学习器和结果插补方法）进行系统比较，实验结果揭示了各方法性能差异显著，尤其在现实因果假设违反情况下表现不稳定，凸显了构建更鲁棒、更通用的生存分析异质性处理效应估计方法的必要性。
FMint-SDE: A Multimodal Foundation Model for Accelerating Numerical Simulation of SDEs via Error Correction
Authors: Jiaxin Yuan, Haizhao Yang, Maria Cameron
First: 2025-10-31T04:49:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:50:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Fast and accurate simulation of dynamical systems is a fundamental challenge across scientific and engineering domains. Traditional numerical integrators often face a trade-off between accuracy and computational efficiency, while existing neural network-based approaches typically require training a separate model for each case. To overcome these limitations, we introduce a novel multi-modal foundation model for large-scale simulations of differential equations: FMint-SDE (Foundation Model based on Initialization for stochastic differential equations). Based on a decoder-only transformer with in-context learning, FMint-SDE leverages numerical and textual modalities to learn a universal error-correction scheme. It is trained using prompted sequences of coarse solutions generated by conventional solvers, enabling broad generalization across diverse systems. We evaluate our models on a suite of challenging SDE benchmarks spanning applications in molecular dynamics, mechanical systems, finance, and biology. Experimental results show that our approach achieves a superior accuracy-efficiency tradeoff compared to classical solvers, underscoring the potential of FMint-SDE as a general-purpose simulation tool for dynamical systems.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for fast and accurate simulation of dynamical systems while overcoming the accuracy-efficiency trade-off of traditional integrators and the lack of generalization in neural methods, FMint-SDE introduces a multimodal foundation model based on a decoder-only transformer with in-context learning. It combines numerical and textual inputs to learn a universal error-correction mechanism, trained on coarse solutions generated by conventional solvers. Experiments across diverse SDE benchmarks in molecular dynamics, mechanics, finance, and biology demonstrate that FMint-SDE achieves superior accuracy-efficiency performance compared to classical solvers, validating its potential as a general-purpose simulation tool.
为解决随机动力系统高效且精确模拟的需求，FMint-SDE提出一种结合数值与文本模态的多模态基础模型，基于解码器Transformer架构学习通用误差校正机制。该模型通过传统求解器生成的粗略解序列进行提示式训练，实现跨领域泛化，涵盖分子动力学、金融与生物学等场景。实验表明，FMint-SDE在精度与效率的权衡上优于传统数值积分器，展现出作为通用动力系统模拟工具的潜力。
Thermodynamic Response Functions in Singular Bayesian Models
Authors: Sean Plummer
First: 2026-03-05T18:50:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:50:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Singular statistical models-including mixtures, matrix factorization, and neural networks-violate regular asymptotics due to parameter non-identifiability and degenerate Fisher geometry. Although singular learning theory characterizes marginal likelihood behavior through invariants such as the real log canonical threshold and singular fluctuation, these quantities remain difficult to interpret operationally. At the same time, widely used criteria such as WAIC and WBIC appear disconnected from underlying singular geometry. We show that posterior tempering induces a one-parameter deformation of the posterior distribution whose associated observables generate a hierarchy of thermodynamic response functions. A universal covariance identity links derivatives of tempered expectations to posterior fluctuations, placing WAIC, WBIC, and singular fluctuation within a unified response framework. Within this framework, classical quantities from singular learning theory acquire natural thermodynamic interpretations: RLCT governs the leading free-energy slope, singular fluctuation corresponds to curvature of the tempered free energy, and WAIC measures predictive fluctuation. We formalize an observable algebra that quotients out non-identifiable directions, allowing structurally meaningful order parameters to be constructed in singular models. Across canonical singular examples-including symmetric Gaussian mixtures, reduced-rank regression, and overparameterized neural networks-we empirically demonstrate phase-transition-like behavior under tempering. Order parameters collapse, susceptibilities peak, and complexity measures align with structural reorganization in posterior geometry. Our results suggest that thermodynamic response theory provides a natural organizing framework for interpreting complexity, predictive variability, and structural reorganization in singular Bayesian learning.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of interpreting complexity and predictive behavior in singular Bayesian models like mixtures and neural networks, this work introduces a thermodynamic response framework via posterior tempering, which generates a family of response functions linked to model geometry. By establishing a universal covariance identity, the study unifies WAIC, WBIC, and singular fluctuation as thermodynamic observables, where RLCT governs free-energy slope, singular fluctuation reflects curvature, and WAIC quantifies predictive variability. Empirical experiments across symmetric mixtures, reduced-rank regression, and overparameterized networks reveal phase-transition-like phenomena: order parameters collapse, susceptibilities peak, and complexity measures align with structural reorganization in posterior geometry, demonstrating the framework’s utility in characterizing model behavior.
为理解混合模型、矩阵分解和神经网络等奇异贝叶斯模型中的复杂性与预测行为，本文提出通过后验退火构建热力学响应函数框架。作者推导出一个普适协方差恒等式，将退火后验期望的导数与后验波动联系起来，统一了WAIC、WBIC与奇异波动：RLCT决定自由能斜率，奇异波动反映自由能曲率，WAIC衡量预测波动。在对称高斯混合、降秩回归和过参数化神经网络等典型奇异模型的实验中，观察到类似相变的现象：序参量坍缩、响应系数峰值出现，复杂度度量与后验几何结构重组一致，表明热力学响应理论为理解奇异贝叶斯学习中的复杂性、预测波动与结构重组提供了自然框架。
Towards 3D Scene Understanding of Gas Plumes in LWIR Hyperspectral Images Using Neural Radiance Fields
Authors: Scout Jarman, Zigfried Hampel-Arias, Adra Carr, Kevin R. Moon
First: 2026-03-05T18:44:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:44:45+00:00
Comments: This manuscript was submitted to SPIE JARS and is under review. Code and Data can be found at https://github.com/lanl/HSI-Nerfstudio and https://zenodo.org/records/18626884 respectively. Video 1 and Video 2 can be found at https://github.com/lanl/HSI-Nerfstudio/blob/main/renders/paper/grid_Falsecolor.mp4 and https://github.com/lanl/HSI-Nerfstudio/blob/main/renders/paper/grid_ACE.mp4 respectively
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Hyperspectral images (HSI) have many applications, ranging from environmental monitoring to national security, and can be used for material detection and identification. Longwave infrared (LWIR) HSI can be used for gas plume detection and analysis. Oftentimes, only a few images of a scene of interest are available and are analyzed individually. The ability to combine information from multiple images into a single, cohesive representation could enhance analysis by providing more context on the scene's geometry and spectral properties. Neural radiance fields (NeRFs) create a latent neural representation of volumetric scene properties that enable novel-view rendering and geometry reconstruction, offering a promising avenue for hyperspectral 3D scene reconstruction. We explore the possibility of using NeRFs to create 3D scene reconstructions from LWIR HSI and demonstrate that the model can be used for the basic downstream analysis task of gas plume detection. The physics-based DIRSIG software suite was used to generate a synthetic multi-view LWIR HSI dataset of a simple facility with a strong sulfur hexafluoride gas plume. Our method, built on the standard Mip-NeRF architecture, combines state-of-the-art methods for hyperspectral NeRFs and sparse-view NeRFs, along with a novel adaptive weighted MSE loss. Our final NeRF method requires around 50% fewer training images than the standard Mip-NeRF and achieves an average PSNR of 39.8 dB with as few as 30 training images. Gas plume detection applied to NeRF-rendered test images using the adaptive coherence estimator achieves an average AUC of 0.821 when compared with detection masks generated from ground-truth test images.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance gas plume detection in longwave infrared hyperspectral images through 3D scene understanding, this work proposes using neural radiance fields (NeRFs) to reconstruct volumetric scenes from sparse multi-view data. Building on the Mip-NeRF architecture, the method integrates hyperspectral and sparse-view NeRF techniques with a novel adaptive weighted MSE loss, enabling effective 3D reconstruction using as few as 30 training images—about half the standard requirement. Experimental results show an average PSNR of 39.8 dB, and downstream gas plume detection via adaptive coherence estimation achieves an AUC of 0.821 against ground-truth masks, demonstrating the model’s utility for downstream analysis.
为提升长波红外高光谱图像中气体羽流检测的准确性，该研究提出利用神经辐射场（NeRF）从稀疏多视角图像重建三维场景。基于Mip-NeRF架构，方法融合了高光谱NeRF与稀疏视角NeRF技术，并引入自适应加权MSE损失函数以提升重建效率与精度。实验采用DIRSIG生成的合成数据表明，该模型仅需30张训练图像（比标准Mip-NeRF少约50%）即可达到39.8 dB的平均PSNR。在NeRF渲染测试图像上应用自适应相干性估计算法进行气体羽流检测，平均AUC达0.821，显著优于单帧图像分析，得益于三维几何与光谱信息的上下文融合。
Leveraging LLM Parametric Knowledge for Fact Checking without Retrieval
Authors: Artem Vazhentsev, Maria Marina, Daniil Moskovskiy, Sergey Pletenev, Mikhail Seleznyov, Mikhail Salnikov, Elena Tutubalina, Vasily Konovalov, Irina Nikishina, Alexander Panchenko, Viktor Moskvoretskii
First: 2026-03-05T18:42:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:42:51+00:00
Comments: Preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Trustworthiness is a core research challenge for agentic AI systems built on Large Language Models (LLMs). To enhance trust, natural language claims from diverse sources, including human-written text, web content, and model outputs, are commonly checked for factuality by retrieving external knowledge and using an LLM to verify the faithfulness of claims to the retrieved evidence. As a result, such methods are constrained by retrieval errors and external data availability, while leaving the models intrinsic fact-verification capabilities largely unused. We propose the task of fact-checking without retrieval, focusing on the verification of arbitrary natural language claims, independent of their source. To study this setting, we introduce a comprehensive evaluation framework focused on generalization, testing robustness to (i) long-tail knowledge, (ii) variation in claim sources, (iii) multilinguality, and (iv) long-form generation. Across 9 datasets, 18 methods and 3 models, our experiments indicate that logit-based approaches often underperform compared to those that leverage internal model representations. Building on this finding, we introduce INTRA, a method that exploits interactions between internal representations and achieves state-of-the-art performance with strong generalization. More broadly, our work establishes fact-checking without retrieval as a promising research direction that can complement retrieval-based frameworks, improve scalability, and enable the use of such systems as reward signals during training or as components integrated into the generation process.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of retrieval-dependent fact-checking methods—such as reliance on external data and susceptibility to retrieval errors—this work explores fact-checking without retrieval, leveraging LLMs' internal parametric knowledge to verify natural language claims regardless of source. The authors introduce a comprehensive evaluation framework assessing generalization across long-tail knowledge, claim source variation, multilinguality, and long-form generation, testing 18 methods across 9 datasets and 3 models. Results show that logit-based methods underperform compared to those using internal representations, leading to the development of INTRA, which exploits interactions between internal model states and achieves state-of-the-art performance with strong generalization, demonstrating the viability of retrieval-free fact-checking as a scalable and integrable component for agentic AI systems.
为克服依赖外部检索的查证方法在数据可用性与检索误差方面的局限，本研究探索仅利用大语言模型内部知识进行事实核查的新范式。作者构建了涵盖长尾知识、来源多样性、多语言及长文本生成等维度的评估框架。在九个数据集、十八种方法和三种模型的实验中，发现基于logit的方法表现劣于利用内部表示的方法。所提出的INTRA方法通过内部表示间的交互实现最优性能并具备强泛化能力，证明无需检索的事实核查是可行且可与检索框架互补的方案，有助于提升系统可扩展性并支持训练或生成过程中的奖励信号应用。
Kraus Constrained Sequence Learning For Quantum Trajectories from Continuous Measurement
Authors: Priyanshi Singh, Krishna Bhatia
Venue: ICLR 2026 Poster
First: 2026-03-05T18:37:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:37:05+00:00
Comments: Poster at AI&PDE: ICLR 2026 Workshop on AI and Partial Differential Equations. 17 pages, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real-time reconstruction of conditional quantum states from continuous measurement records is a fundamental requirement for quantum feedback control, yet standard stochastic master equation (SME) solvers require exact model specification, known system parameters, and are sensitive to parameter mismatch. While neural sequence models can fit these stochastic dynamics, the unconstrained predictors can violate physicality such as positivity or trace constraints, leading to unstable rollouts and unphysical estimates. We propose a Kraus-structured output layer that converts the hidden representation of a generic sequence backbone into a completely positive trace preserving (CPTP) quantum operation, yielding physically valid state updates by construction. We instantiate this layer across diverse backbones, RNN, GRU, LSTM, TCN, ESN and Mamba; including Neural ODE as a comparative baseline, on stochastic trajectories characterized by parameter drift. Our evaluation reveals distinct trade-offs between gating mechanisms, linear recurrence, and global attention. Across all models, Kraus-LSTM achieves the strongest results, improving state estimation quality by 7% over its unconstrained counterpart while guaranteeing physically valid predictions in non-stationary regimes.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for physically valid quantum state reconstruction from continuous measurement data, this work introduces a Kraus-structured output layer that enforces complete positivity and trace preservation on neural sequence models. Applied across diverse architectures including RNN, GRU, LSTM, TCN, ESN, Mamba, and Neural ODE, the layer transforms hidden representations into valid quantum operations. Experimental results on parameter-drift trajectories show that Kraus-LSTM outperforms its unconstrained version by 7% in state estimation accuracy while ensuring physicality even under non-stationary conditions, highlighting the effectiveness of gated recurrence with physical constraints.
为实现从连续测量记录中重建物理有效的量子态，本文提出一种Kraus结构输出层，将通用序列模型的隐藏表示转化为完全正定且保持迹的量子操作，从而保证输出状态更新的物理合理性。该层应用于RNN、GRU、LSTM、TCN、ESN、Mamba及Neural ODE等多种架构，并在参数漂移的随机轨迹上进行评估。实验表明，Kraus-LSTM表现最优，在非稳态环境下保持物理有效性的同时，其状态估计质量比无约束版本提升7%。
HALP: Detecting Hallucinations in Vision-Language Models without Generating a Single Token
Authors: Sai Akhil Kogilathota, Sripadha Vallabha E G, Luzhe Sun, Jiawei Zhou
Venue: The 19th Conference of the European Chapter of the Association for Computational Linguistics (EACL 2026)
First: 2026-03-05T18:36:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:36:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Hallucinations remain a persistent challenge for vision-language models (VLMs), which often describe nonexistent objects or fabricate facts. Existing detection methods typically operate after text generation, making intervention both costly and untimely. We investigate whether hallucination risk can instead be predicted before any token is generated by probing a model's internal representations in a single forward pass. Across a diverse set of vision-language tasks and eight modern VLMs, including Llama-3.2-Vision, Gemma-3, Phi-4-VL, and Qwen2.5-VL, we examine three families of internal representations: (i) visual-only features without multimodal fusion, (ii) vision-token representations within the text decoder, and (iii) query-token representations that integrate visual and textual information before generation. Probes trained on these representations achieve strong hallucination-detection performance without decoding, reaching up to 0.93 AUROC on Gemma-3-12B, Phi-4-VL 5.6B, and Molmo 7B. Late query-token states are the most predictive for most models, while visual or mid-layer features dominate in a few architectures (e.g., ~0.79 AUROC for Qwen2.5-VL-7B using visual-only features). These results demonstrate that (1) hallucination risk is detectable pre-generation, (2) the most informative layer and modality vary across architectures, and (3) lightweight probes have the potential to enable early abstention, selective routing, and adaptive decoding to improve both safety and efficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HALP：无需生成任何标记即可检测视觉-语言模型中的幻觉
幻觉仍是视觉-语言模型（VLMs）持续存在的挑战，这些模型常描述不存在的对象或捏造事实。现有检测方法通常在文本生成后进行，干预成本高且时机滞后。我们探究是否能在生成任何标记前，通过单次前向传播探测模型内部表征来预测幻觉风险。在多样化的视觉-语言任务及八种现代VLMs（包括Llama-3.2-Vision、Gemma-3、Phi-4-VL和Qwen2.5-VL）上，我们考察三类内部表征：（i）无多模态融合的视觉特征，（ii）文本解码器内的视觉-标记表征，（iii）整合视觉与文本信息的查询-标记表征。基于这些表征训练的探测器无需解码即可实现强幻觉检测性能，在Gemma-3-12B、Phi-4-VL 5.6B和Molmo 7B上AUROC高达0.93。多数模型中后期查询-标记状态最具预测性，少数架构（如Qwen2.5-VL-7B使用视觉特征时AUROC约0.79）则由视觉或中间层特征主导。结果表明：（1）幻觉风险可在生成前检测；（2）最具信息量的层与模态因架构而异；（3）轻量级探测器有望实现早期拒绝、选择性路由和自适应解码，提升安全性和效率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the persistent hallucination problem in vision-language models, this study explores whether hallucination risk can be predicted before any text is generated by analyzing internal model representations during a single forward pass. The method evaluates three types of internal features—visual-only, vision-token, and query-token representations—across eight modern VLMs, training lightweight probes on each to detect hallucinations. Experimental results show that probes achieve high detection performance without decoding, with AUROC scores up to 0.93 on models like Gemma-3-12B and Phi-4-VL-5.6B, and late query-token states are most predictive for most architectures, though visual features dominate in others like Qwen2.5-VL-7B. These findings confirm that hallucination risk is detectable pre-generation, varies by model architecture, and can enable early intervention for safer and more efficient VLM usage.
为解决视觉语言模型中持续存在的幻觉问题，本研究探索在生成任何文本前，通过分析单次前向传播中的内部表示来预测幻觉风险。方法评估了三种内部特征——纯视觉特征、视觉-文本混合特征及查询-文本融合特征，在八种现代视觉语言模型上训练轻量级探测器进行幻觉检测。实验结果显示，无需解码即可达到高检测性能，部分模型AUROC高达0.93，多数模型中后期查询-文本融合特征最具预测力，而部分架构（如Qwen2.5-VL-7B）则依赖纯视觉特征（AUROC约0.79）。结果表明，幻觉风险可在生成前检测，不同架构最优特征不同，且该方法可支持早期干预，提升模型安全与效率。
EdgeDAM: Real-time Object Tracking for Mobile Devices
Authors: Syed Muhammad Raza, Syed Murtaza Hussain Abidi, Khawar Islam, Muhammad Ibrahim, Ajmal Saeed Mian
First: 2026-03-05T18:35:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:35:25+00:00
Comments: 10 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Single-object tracking (SOT) on edge devices is a critical computer vision task, requiring accurate and continuous target localization across video frames under occlusion, distractor interference, and fast motion. However, recent state-of-the-art distractor-aware memory mechanisms are largely built on segmentation-based trackers and rely on mask prediction and attention-driven memory updates, which introduce substantial computational overhead and limit real-time deployment on resource-constrained hardware; meanwhile, lightweight trackers sustain high throughput but are prone to drift when visually similar distractors appear. To address these challenges, we propose EdgeDAM, a lightweight detection-guided tracking framework that reformulates distractor-aware memory for bounding-box tracking under strict edge constraints. EdgeDAM introduces two key strategies: (1) Dual-Buffer Distractor-Aware Memory (DAM), which integrates a Recent-Aware Memory to preserve temporally consistent target hypotheses and a Distractor-Resolving Memory to explicitly store hard negative candidates and penalize their re-selection during recovery; and (2) Confidence-Driven Switching with Held-Box Stabilization, where tracker reliability and temporal consistency criteria adaptively activate detection and memory-guided re-identification during occlusion, while a held-box mechanism temporarily freezes and expands the estimate to suppress distractor contamination. Extensive experiments on five benchmarks, including the distractor-focused DiDi dataset, demonstrate improved robustness under occlusion and fast motion while maintaining real-time performance on mobile devices, achieving 88.2% accuracy on DiDi and 25 FPS on an iPhone 15. Code will be released.
Summary / 总结
EdgeDAM addresses the challenge of real-time single-object tracking on mobile devices by introducing a lightweight detection-guided framework that reformulates distractor-aware memory for bounding-box tracking under strict edge constraints. It employs a Dual-Buffer Distractor-Aware Memory system to maintain temporally consistent target hypotheses and explicitly manage hard negative distractors, while a Confidence-Driven Switching mechanism with Held-Box Stabilization adapts detection and memory-guided re-identification during occlusion and fast motion, suppressing distractor contamination. Evaluated on five benchmarks including the DiDi dataset, EdgeDAM achieves 88.2% accuracy and 25 FPS on an iPhone 15, demonstrating robustness under occlusion and fast motion while preserving real-time performance.
EdgeDAM 为移动端实时单目标跟踪提出了一种轻量级检测引导框架，通过在边缘约束下重构基于边界框的干扰感知记忆机制来应对遮挡、快速运动和干扰物挑战。其核心包含双缓冲干扰感知记忆（DAM），分别维护时序一致的目标假设与显式存储硬负样本以避免误选；以及基于置信度的切换与保持框稳定机制，在遮挡时自适应切换检测与记忆引导重识别，并临时冻结并扩展目标估计以抑制干扰。在五个基准数据集（含干扰聚焦的DiDi数据集）上的实验表明，EdgeDAM在iPhone 15上实现25 FPS的实时性能，同时在遮挡与快速运动场景下达到88.2%的准确率。
NCTB-QA: A Large-Scale Bangla Educational Question Answering Dataset and Benchmarking Performance
Authors: Abrar Eyasir, Tahsin Ahmed, Muhammad Ibrahim
First: 2026-03-05T18:35:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:35:03+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 7 figures, 6 tables. Dataset contains 87,805 Bangla QA pairs from NCTB textbooks
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reading comprehension systems for low-resource languages face significant challenges in handling unanswerable questions. These systems tend to produce unreliable responses when correct answers are absent from context. To solve this problem, we introduce NCTB-QA, a large-scale Bangla question answering dataset comprising 87,805 question-answer pairs extracted from 50 textbooks published by Bangladesh's National Curriculum and Textbook Board. Unlike existing Bangla datasets, NCTB-QA maintains a balanced distribution of answerable (57.25%) and unanswerable (42.75%) questions. NCTB-QA also includes adversarially designed instances containing plausible distractors. We benchmark three transformer-based models (BERT, RoBERTa, ELECTRA) and demonstrate substantial improvements through fine-tuning. BERT achieves 313% relative improvement in F1 score (0.150 to 0.620). Semantic answer quality measured by BERTScore also increases significantly across all models. Our results establish NCTB-QA as a challenging benchmark for Bangla educational question answering. This study demonstrates that domain-specific fine-tuning is critical for robust performance in low-resource settings.
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of unreliable responses in low-resource Bangla reading comprehension systems when encountering unanswerable questions, the authors introduce NCTB-QA, a large-scale educational QA dataset with 87,805 pairs drawn from 50 Bangla textbooks. The dataset uniquely balances answerable and unanswerable questions and includes adversarial distractors to enhance robustness. Experiments benchmarking BERT, RoBERTa, and ELECTRA show that fine-tuning on NCTB-QA yields substantial performance gains, with BERT achieving a 313% relative F1 score improvement (from 0.150 to 0.620), and BERTScore also rising significantly across all models, underscoring the value of domain-specific fine-tuning in low-resource settings.
为解决低资源语言（如孟加拉语）阅读理解系统在面对无答案问题时响应不可靠的问题，作者构建了NCTB-QA数据集，包含从50本孟加拉语教材中抽取的87,805个问答对。该数据集在可答与不可答问题间保持平衡，并包含对抗性干扰项以提升系统鲁棒性。实验在BERT、RoBERTa和ELECTRA三类模型上进行，结果显示微调后性能显著提升，其中BERT的F1分数提升313%（从0.150至0.620），各模型的BERTScore也大幅提高，证明了在低资源环境下领域特定微调的重要性。
DEBISS: a Corpus of Individual, Semi-structured and Spoken Debates
Authors: Klaywert Danillo Ferreira de Souza, David Eduardo Pereira, Cláudio E. C. Campelo, Larissa Lucena Vasconcelos
First: 2026-03-05T18:30:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:30:10+00:00

Abstract
The process of debating is essential in our daily lives, whether in studying, work activities, simple everyday discussions, political debates on TV, or online discussions on social networks. The range of uses for debates is broad. Due to the diverse applications, structures, and formats of debates, developing corpora that account for these variations can be challenging, and the scarcity of debate corpora in the state of the art is notable. For this reason, the current research proposes the DEBISS corpus: a collection of spoken and individual debates with semi-structured features. With a broad range of NLP task annotations, such as speech-to-text, speaker diarization, argument mining, and debater quality assessment.
