Beyond Scattered Acceptance: Fast and Coherent Inference for DLMs via Longest Stable Prefixes
Authors: Pengxiang Li, Joey Tsai, Hongwei Xue, Kunyu Shi, Shilin Yan
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-03-05T18:25:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:25:26+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) promise highly parallel text generation, yet their practical inference speed is often bottlenecked by suboptimal decoding schedulers. Standard approaches rely on 'scattered acceptance'-committing high confidence tokens at disjoint positions throughout the sequence. This approach inadvertently fractures the Key-Value (KV) cache, destroys memory locality, and forces the model into costly, repeated repairs across unstable token boundaries. To resolve this, we present the Longest Stable Prefix (LSP) scheduler, a training-free and model-agnostic inference paradigm based on monolithic prefix absorption. In each denoising step, LSP evaluates token stability via a single forward pass, dynamically identifies a contiguous left-aligned block of stable predictions, and snaps its boundary to natural linguistic or structural delimiters before an atomic commitment. This prefix-first topology yields dual benefits: systemically, it converts fragmented KV cache updates into efficient, contiguous appends; algorithmically, it preserves bidirectional lookahead over a geometrically shrinking active suffix, drastically reducing token flip rates and denoiser calls. Extensive evaluations on LLaDA-8B and Dream-7B demonstrate that LSP accelerates inference by up to 3.4x across rigorous benchmarks including mathematical reasoning, code generation, multilingual (CJK) tasks, and creative writing while matching or slightly improving output quality. By fundamentally restructuring the commitment topology, LSP bridges the gap between the theoretical parallelism of DLMs and practical hardware efficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越分散接受：通过最长稳定前缀实现扩散语言模型的快速与一致推理
扩散语言模型（DLMs）承诺高度并行的文本生成，但其实用推理速度常因次优的解码调度器而受限。标准方法依赖‘分散接受’——在序列中不连续位置提交高置信度标记，这无意中割裂了键值（KV）缓存，破坏内存局部性，并迫使模型在不稳定标记边界处进行昂贵的重复修复。为解决此问题，我们提出最长稳定前缀（LSP）调度器，这是一种无需训练、模型无关的推理范式，基于整体前缀吸收。在每个去噪步骤中，LSP通过单次前向传播评估标记稳定性，动态识别连续左对齐的稳定预测块，并在原子提交前将其边界对齐到自然语言或结构分隔符。这种前缀优先拓扑带来双重优势：系统层面，它将碎片化的KV缓存更新转化为高效连续追加；算法层面，它在几何收缩的活跃后缀上保持双向前瞻，大幅降低标记翻转率和去噪器调用次数。在LLaDA-8B和Dream-7B上的广泛评估表明，LSP在数学推理、代码生成、多语言（CJK）任务和创意写作等严格基准测试中，推理速度最高提升3.4倍，同时输出质量持平或略有提升。通过根本性重构提交拓扑，LSP弥合了DLMs理论并行性与实际硬件效率之间的鸿沟。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of scattered token acceptance in diffusion language models that fragments KV cache and forces costly repairs, the paper introduces the Longest Stable Prefix (LSP) scheduler—a training-free, model-agnostic method that dynamically identifies and commits the longest contiguous block of stable tokens at each denoising step, snapping its boundary to linguistic delimiters. This prefix-first approach transforms fragmented cache updates into contiguous appends and preserves bidirectional lookahead over a shrinking suffix, reducing token flips and denoiser calls. Experiments on LLaDA-8B and Dream-7B show LSP achieves up to 3.4x faster inference across diverse tasks—including math, code, CJK, and creative writing—while maintaining or slightly improving output quality, bridging theoretical parallelism with hardware efficiency.
为了解决扩散语言模型中散乱接受令牌导致的KV缓存碎片化和频繁修复问题，本文提出了一种无需训练、模型无关的推理方法——最长稳定前缀（LSP）调度器。该方法在每一步去噪时，通过单次前向传播动态识别最长连续稳定令牌块，并将其边界对齐到语言或结构分隔符后进行原子提交。这种前缀优先的拓扑结构实现了连续的KV缓存追加，并保留了对缩减后后缀的双向前瞻，显著减少令牌翻转和去噪调用。在LLaDA-8B和Dream-7B上的实验表明，LSP在数学推理、代码生成、多语言（CJK）和创意写作等任务中推理速度最高提升3.4倍，同时保持或略微提升输出质量，弥合了理论并行性与实际硬件效率之间的差距。
FlashAttention-4: Algorithm and Kernel Pipelining Co-Design for Asymmetric Hardware Scaling
Authors: Ted Zadouri, Markus Hoehnerbach, Jay Shah, Timmy Liu, Vijay Thakkar, Tri Dao
First: 2026-03-05T18:24:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:24:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Attention, as a core layer of the ubiquitous Transformer architecture, is the bottleneck for large language models and long-context applications. While FlashAttention-3 optimized attention for Hopper GPUs through asynchronous execution and warp specialization, it primarily targets the H100 architecture. The AI industry has rapidly transitioned to deploying Blackwell-based systems such as the B200 and GB200, which exhibit fundamentally different performance characteristics due to asymmetric hardware scaling: tensor core throughput doubles while other functional units (shared memory bandwidth, exponential units) scale more slowly or remain unchanged. We develop several techniques to address these shifting bottlenecks on Blackwell GPUs: (1) redesigned pipelines that exploit fully asynchronous MMA operations and larger tile sizes, (2) software-emulated exponential and conditional softmax rescaling that reduces non-matmul operations, and (3) leveraging tensor memory and the 2-CTA MMA mode to reduce shared memory traffic and atomic adds in the backward pass. We demonstrate that our method, FlashAttention-4, achieves up to 1.3$\times$ speedup over cuDNN 9.13 and 2.7$\times$ over Triton on B200 GPUs with BF16, reaching up to 1613 TFLOPs/s (71% utilization). Beyond algorithmic innovations, we implement FlashAttention-4 entirely in CuTe-DSL embedded in Python, achieving 20-30$\times$ faster compile times compared to traditional C++ template-based approaches while maintaining full expressivity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FlashAttention-4：面向非对称硬件扩展的算法与内核流水线协同设计
注意力机制作为广泛使用的Transformer架构的核心层，是大语言模型和长上下文应用的瓶颈。虽然FlashAttention-3通过异步执行和warp专业化优化了Hopper GPU上的注意力计算，但主要针对H100架构。AI行业已迅速转向部署基于Blackwell架构的系统（如B200和GB200），其性能特征因非对称硬件扩展而发生根本性变化：张量核心吞吐量翻倍，而其他功能单元（共享内存带宽、指数单元）扩展更慢或保持不变。我们提出多种技术应对Blackwell GPU上这些变化的瓶颈：（1）重新设计的流水线，利用完全异步的MMA操作和更大的分块大小；（2）软件模拟的指数和条件softmax重缩放，减少非矩阵乘法操作；（3）利用张量内存和2-CTA MMA模式，降低反向传播中的共享内存流量和原子加法。我们证明，FlashAttention-4在B200 GPU上使用BF16时，相比cuDNN 9.13提速最高达1.3倍，相比Triton提速最高达2.7倍，达到最高1613 TFLOPs/s（71%利用率）。除算法创新外，我们完全使用嵌入Python的CuTe-DSL实现FlashAttention-4，相比传统C++模板方法，编译速度提升20-30倍，同时保持完整表达能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the performance bottlenecks of attention in large language models on newer Blackwell GPUs like the B200, which exhibit asymmetric scaling compared to H100, FlashAttention-4 introduces algorithmic and kernel-level optimizations tailored for this hardware. The method combines redesigned asynchronous pipelines with larger tile sizes, software-emulated exponential and softmax operations to reduce non-matmul overhead, and efficient memory usage via tensor memory and 2-CTA MMA mode to cut shared memory traffic. Experimental results show FlashAttention-4 achieves up to 1.3x speedup over cuDNN 9.13 and 2.7x over Triton on B200 GPUs with BF16, reaching 1613 TFLOPs/s at 71% utilization, while its CuTe-DSL implementation in Python delivers 20-30x faster compile times than C++ template-based methods without sacrificing expressivity.
为应对在B200等Blackwell架构GPU上注意力计算的性能瓶颈——其硬件扩展特性与H100不同（张量核心吞吐量翻倍，但共享内存带宽和指数单元等扩展缓慢或不变），FlashAttention-4提出算法与内核流水线协同设计：包括完全异步MMA操作与更大块尺寸、软件模拟指数与条件softmax重缩放以减少非矩阵乘操作、以及利用张量内存与2-CTA MMA模式降低共享内存访问与原子加操作。实验表明，该方法在B200 GPU上BF16精度下较cuDNN 9.13提速最高1.3倍、较Triton提速2.7倍，峰值达1613 TFLOPs/s（71%利用率），且其基于CuTe-DSL的Python实现比传统C++模板编译快20-30倍，同时保持完整表达能力。
Distributed Partial Information Puzzles: Examining Common Ground Construction Under Epistemic Asymmetry
Authors: Yifan Zhu, Mariah Bradford, Kenneth Lai, Timothy Obiso, Videep Venkatesha, James Pustejovsky, Nikhil Krishnaswamy
First: 2026-03-05T18:22:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:22:55+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Establishing common ground, a shared set of beliefs and mutually recognized facts, is fundamental to collaboration, yet remains a challenge for current AI systems, especially in multimodal, multiparty settings, where the collaborators bring different information to the table. We introduce the Distributed Partial Information Puzzle (DPIP), a collaborative construction task that elicits rich multimodal communication under epistemic asymmetry. We present a multimodal dataset of these interactions, annotated and temporally aligned across speech, gesture, and action modalities to support reasoning over propositional content and belief dynamics. We then evaluate two paradigms for modeling common ground (CG): (1) state-of-the-art large language models (LLMs), prompted to infer shared beliefs from multimodal updates, and (2) an axiomatic pipeline grounded in Dynamic Epistemic Logic (DEL) that incrementally performs the same task. Results on the annotated DPIP data indicate that it poses a challenge to modern LLMs' abilities to track both task progression and belief state.
中文标题/摘要
标题：分布式部分信息谜题：在认识论不对称下考察共同认知的构建
建立共同认知——即共享信念与相互认可的事实——是协作的基础，但当前AI系统在多模态、多方协作场景中仍面临挑战，因为协作方各自携带不同信息。我们引入分布式部分信息谜题（DPIP），这是一种在认识论不对称下激发丰富多模态交流的协作构建任务。我们构建了该任务的多模态数据集，对语音、手势和动作模态进行标注与时间对齐，以支持对命题内容和信念动态的推理。随后，我们评估两种建模共同认知（CG）的范式：（1）通过多模态更新推断共享信念的最新大型语言模型（LLMs）；（2）基于动态认识论逻辑（DEL）的公理化流程，逐步执行相同任务。在标注的DPIP数据上的结果表明，该任务对现代LLMs同时追踪任务进展和信念状态的能力构成挑战。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of establishing common ground in AI systems during collaborative tasks with epistemic asymmetry, where participants have different information. It introduces the Distributed Partial Information Puzzle (DPIP), a multimodal, multiparty task designed to elicit rich communication under unequal knowledge. The study evaluates two approaches: large language models (LLMs) prompted to infer shared beliefs from multimodal inputs, and an axiomatic pipeline based on Dynamic Epistemic Logic (DEL) that incrementally tracks belief states. Experimental results show that current LLMs struggle to accurately track both task progress and evolving belief states, highlighting a key limitation in their ability to model common ground under epistemic asymmetry.
该研究旨在解决AI系统在具有认知不对称性的协作任务中建立共同认知的难题，引入了分布式部分信息谜题（DPIP），一种多模态、多角色任务，用于在信息不均等条件下激发丰富的交流。研究对比了两种方法：基于提示的大型语言模型（LLM）用于从多模态输入推断共同信念，以及基于动态认识逻辑（DEL）的公理化管道，逐步追踪信念状态。实验结果表明，当前LLM难以准确跟踪任务进展与信念状态的动态变化，揭示了其在该领域的关键局限性。
RealWonder: Real-Time Physical Action-Conditioned Video Generation
Authors: Wei Liu, Ziyu Chen, Zizhang Li, Yue Wang, Hong-Xing Yu, Jiajun Wu
First: 2026-03-05T18:22:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:22:54+00:00
Comments: The first two authors contributed equally. The last two authors advised equally. Project website: https://liuwei283.github.io/RealWonder/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Current video generation models cannot simulate physical consequences of 3D actions like forces and robotic manipulations, as they lack structural understanding of how actions affect 3D scenes. We present RealWonder, the first real-time system for action-conditioned video generation from a single image. Our key insight is using physics simulation as an intermediate bridge: instead of directly encoding continuous actions, we translate them through physics simulation into visual representations (optical flow and RGB) that video models can process. RealWonder integrates three components: 3D reconstruction from single images, physics simulation, and a distilled video generator requiring only 4 diffusion steps. Our system achieves 13.2 FPS at 480x832 resolution, enabling interactive exploration of forces, robot actions, and camera controls on rigid objects, deformable bodies, fluids, and granular materials. We envision RealWonder opens new opportunities to apply video models in immersive experiences, AR/VR, and robot learning. Our code and model weights are publicly available in our project website: https://liuwei283.github.io/RealWonder/
中文标题/摘要
标题：RealWonder：基于物理动作条件的实时视频生成
当前视频生成模型无法模拟3D动作的物理后果（如力和机器人操作），因为它们缺乏对动作如何影响3D场景的结构化理解。我们提出了RealWonder，这是首个从单张图像实时生成动作条件视频的系统。我们的核心思路是将物理仿真作为中间桥梁：而非直接编码连续动作，我们通过物理仿真将其转换为视频模型可处理的视觉表示（光流和RGB）。RealWonder集成了三个组件：单图像3D重建、物理仿真和仅需4步扩散的蒸馏视频生成器。该系统在480x832分辨率下达到13.2 FPS，支持对刚体、可变形体、流体和颗粒材料进行交互式探索，包括力、机器人动作和相机控制。我们期望RealWonder为视频模型在沉浸式体验、AR/VR和机器人学习中的应用开辟新机遇。代码和模型权重已公开于项目网站：https://liuwei283.github.io/RealWonder/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inability of current video generation models to simulate physical consequences of 3D actions, RealWonder introduces a real-time system that generates action-conditioned videos from a single image by leveraging physics simulation as an intermediate step. The method translates user-defined actions into visual representations—optical flow and RGB—via physics simulation, then feeds them into a distilled video generator requiring only four diffusion steps. Experimental results show the system achieves 13.2 FPS at 480x832 resolution, enabling interactive manipulation of rigid objects, deformable bodies, fluids, and granular materials through forces, robot actions, and camera controls.
为了解决当前视频生成模型无法模拟3D动作的物理后果（如力和机器人操作）的问题，RealWonder提出了一种从单张图像实时生成动作条件视频的系统，其核心方法是通过物理仿真作为中间桥梁，将动作转化为视频模型可处理的视觉表示（光流和RGB），再输入仅需四步扩散的蒸馏视频生成器。实验表明，该系统在480x832分辨率下达到13.2帧每秒，支持对刚体、可变形体、流体和颗粒材料进行交互式操控，包括施加力、机器人动作和摄像机控制。
Residual RL--MPC for Robust Microrobotic Cell Pushing Under Time-Varying Flow
Authors: Yanda Yang, Sambeeta Das
First: 2026-03-05T18:20:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:20:07+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Contact-rich micromanipulation in microfluidic flow is challenging because small disturbances can break pushing contact and induce large lateral drift. We study planar cell pushing with a magnetic rolling microrobot that tracks a waypoint-sampled reference curve under time-varying Poiseuille flow. We propose a hybrid controller that augments a nominal MPC with a learned residual policy trained by SAC. The policy outputs a bounded 2D velocity correction that is contact-gated, so residual actions are applied only during robot--cell contact, preserving reliable approach behavior and stabilizing learning. All methods share the same actuation interface and speed envelope for fair comparisons. Experiments show improved robustness and tracking accuracy over pure MPC and PID under nonstationary flow, with generalization from a clover training curve to unseen circle and square trajectories. A residual-bound sweep identifies an intermediate correction limit as the best trade-off, which we use in all benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于时变流场下鲁棒微机器人细胞推动的残差强化学习-MPC
在微流体流场中进行接触密集型微操作具有挑战性，因为微小扰动可能破坏推动接触并引发显著的横向漂移。我们研究了在时变泊肃叶流场中，利用磁性滚动微机器人沿采样路点的参考曲线跟踪进行平面细胞推动。我们提出了一种混合控制器，将标准MPC与通过SAC训练的残差策略相结合。该策略输出有界二维速度修正，且接触门控，仅在机器人与细胞接触时施加残差动作，从而保持可靠的接近行为并稳定学习。所有方法共享相同的执行接口和速度范围，以确保公平比较。实验表明，在非稳态流场下，该方法相比纯MPC和PID具有更好的鲁棒性和跟踪精度，并能从三叶草训练轨迹泛化到未见过的圆形和方形轨迹。残差修正范围扫描确定了一个中间修正限制，作为最佳权衡，我们在所有基准测试中均采用该限制。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of maintaining stable contact during microrobotic cell pushing under time-varying fluid flow, the authors propose a hybrid controller combining model predictive control (MPC) with a learned residual policy trained via soft actor-critic (SAC). The residual policy outputs bounded 2D velocity corrections that are only applied during robot-cell contact, preserving the nominal MPC’s reliable approach behavior while enhancing robustness. Experiments demonstrate that this approach outperforms pure MPC and PID in tracking accuracy and robustness under nonstationary flow, generalizing from a clover-shaped training trajectory to unseen circular and square paths, with optimal performance achieved at an intermediate residual correction bound.
为解决微流体环境中微机器人推细胞时因流速变化导致接触不稳定的问题，作者提出一种结合模型预测控制（MPC）与基于软演员-评论家（SAC）训练的残差策略的混合控制器。该残差策略仅在机器人与细胞接触时输出有界二维速度修正，保留MPC的可靠接近行为，同时提升鲁棒性。实验表明，该方法在非稳态流场下比纯MPC和PID控制更优，能从训练用的三叶草轨迹泛化至未见过的圆形和方形路径，且在中间残差修正幅度下性能最佳。
Self-Distilled Reasoner: On-Policy Self-Distillation for Large Language Models
Authors: Siyan Zhao, Zhihui Xie, Mengchen Liu, Jing Huang, Guan Pang, Feiyu Chen, Aditya Grover
First: 2026-01-26T17:56:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:19:57+00:00
Comments: code is release here: https://github.com/siyan-zhao/OPSD
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Knowledge distillation improves large language model (LLM) reasoning by compressing the knowledge of a teacher LLM to train smaller LLMs. On-policy distillation advances this approach by having the student sample its own trajectories while a teacher LLM provides dense token-level supervision, addressing the distribution mismatch between training and inference in off-policy distillation methods. However, on-policy distillation typically requires a separate, often larger, teacher LLM and does not explicitly leverage ground-truth solutions available in reasoning datasets. Inspired by the intuition that a sufficiently capable LLM can rationalize external privileged reasoning traces and teach its weaker self (i.e., the version without access to privileged information), we introduce On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD), a framework where a single model acts as both teacher and student by conditioning on different contexts. The teacher policy conditions on privileged information (e.g., verified reasoning traces) while the student policy sees only the question; training minimizes the per-token divergence between these distributions over the student's own rollouts. We demonstrate the efficacy of our method on multiple mathematical reasoning benchmarks, achieving 8-12x token efficiency compared to reinforcement learning methods such as GRPO and superior performance over off-policy distillation methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自蒸馏推理器：大型语言模型的在线自蒸馏方法
知识蒸馏通过压缩教师大语言模型（LLM）的知识来训练更小的LLM，从而提升其推理能力。在线蒸馏进一步改进该方法，使学生模型在训练时采样自身轨迹，而教师LLM提供密集的逐token监督，解决了离线蒸馏方法中训练与推理分布不匹配的问题。然而，传统在线蒸馏通常需要一个独立的、往往更大的教师LLM，且未显式利用推理数据集中可用的真实解。受启发于“足够强大的LLM能够基于外部特权推理轨迹进行推理并教导其较弱的自我（即无特权信息访问权限的版本）”这一直觉，我们提出在线自蒸馏（OPSD）框架：单个模型同时扮演教师与学生，通过不同上下文条件进行训练。教师策略基于特权信息（如已验证的推理轨迹），学生策略仅看到问题；训练目标是最小化学生自身轨迹上教师与学生分布之间的逐token差异。我们在多个数学推理基准上验证了该方法的有效性，相比强化学习方法（如GRPO）实现8-12倍的token效率，并优于离线蒸馏方法。
Summary / 总结
Knowledge distillation improves large language model (LLM) reasoning by compressing the knowledge of a teacher LLM to train smaller LLMs.
HydroGEM: A Self Supervised Zero Shot Hybrid TCN Transformer Foundation Model for Continental Scale Streamflow Quality Control
Authors: Ijaz Ul Haq, Byung Suk Lee, Julia N. Perdrial, David Baude
First: 2025-12-16T05:39:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:19:22+00:00
Comments: Supplementary materials, datasets, and implementation code will be made publicly available upon acceptance for publication in a peer-reviewed journal
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Advances in sensor networks have enabled real-time stream discharge monitoring, yet persistent sensor malfunctions limit data utility. Manual quality control by expert hydrologists cannot scale with networks generating millions of measurements annually. We introduce HydroGEM, a foundation model for continental-scale streamflow quality control designed to support human expertise. HydroGEM uses self-supervised pretraining on 6.03 million clean sequences from 3,724 USGS stations to learn general hydrological representations, followed by fine-tuning with synthetic anomalies for detection and reconstruction. A hybrid TCN-Transformer architecture (14.2M parameters) captures both local and long-range temporal dependencies, while hierarchical normalization handles six orders of magnitude in discharge. On held-out observations from 799 stations with 18 synthetic anomaly types grounded in USGS standards, HydroGEM achieves F1=0.792 for detection and 68.7% reconstruction error reduction, outperforming the strongest baseline by 36.3%. For cross-national validation on 100 Environment and Climate Change Canada stations using tolerant evaluation with a plus or minus 24-hour buffer, HydroGEM achieves Tolerant F1=0.70 with 90.1% segment-level event detection, demonstrating cross-national generalization. The model maintains consistent detection across correction magnitudes and aligns with operational seasonal patterns, with peak flagging during winter ice-affected periods matching hydrologists' correction behavior. Architectural separation between simplified training anomalies and complex test anomalies confirms that performance reflects learned hydrometric principles rather than pattern memorization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HydroGEM：一种自监督零样本混合TCN-Transformer基础模型，用于大陆尺度径流质量控制
传感器网络的进步实现了径流实时监测，但传感器持续故障限制了数据可用性。专家水文学家的手动质量控制无法应对每年数百万测量值的网络规模。我们提出了HydroGEM，一种面向大陆尺度径流质量控制的基础模型，旨在支持人类专家。HydroGEM通过在3724个美国地质调查局（USGS）站点的603万条干净序列上进行自监督预训练，学习通用水文表示，随后使用合成异常进行微调，以实现异常检测与重建。其混合TCN-Transformer架构（1420万参数）捕捉局部与长程时间依赖性，分层归一化处理六数量级的流量变化。在799个站点的18种基于USGS标准的合成异常观测数据上，HydroGEM检测F1=0.792，重建误差降低68.7%，优于最强基线36.3%。在加拿大环境与气候变化部100个站点的跨国家验证中，采用±24小时容忍评估，HydroGEM实现容忍F1=0.70，事件段级检测准确率达90.1%，证明其跨国家泛化能力。模型在不同修正幅度下保持一致检测能力，且与操作季节性模式一致，冬季冰期高峰标记行为与水文专家修正行为相符。训练用简化异常与测试用复杂异常的架构分离证实，其性能源于学习到的水文原理，而非模式记忆。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to automate quality control of vast, real-time streamflow data from malfunctioning sensors, HydroGEM is a self-supervised foundation model that pretrains on 6.03 million clean sequences from 3,724 USGS stations to learn general hydrological patterns, then fine-tunes with synthetic anomalies for detection and reconstruction. Its hybrid TCN-Transformer architecture (14.2M parameters) captures local and long-range temporal dependencies, enhanced by hierarchical normalization to handle extreme discharge ranges. On 799 stations with 18 synthetic anomaly types, it achieves F1=0.792 for detection and 68.7% error reduction in reconstruction, outperforming the best baseline by 36.3%. Cross-national validation on 100 Canadian stations shows Tolerant F1=0.70 and 90.1% event detection, with consistent performance across anomaly magnitudes and alignment with seasonal hydrological patterns, confirming learned hydrometric principles over memorization.
为应对传感器故障导致的海量实时径流数据质量控制难题，HydroGEM 是一种自监督基础模型，先在3724个USGS站点的603万条干净序列上预训练，再用合成异常数据进行微调以实现异常检测与重建。其TCN-Transformer混合架构（1420万参数）能捕捉局部与长程时间依赖，层级归一化处理六数量级的流量差异。在799个站点、18种基于USGS标准的异常类型测试中，模型检测F1达0.792，误差降低68.7%，优于最强基线36.3%。跨国家验证（100个加拿大站点）显示容忍F1=0.70，事件检测准确率达90.1%，且在不同修正幅度下表现稳定，符合季节性水文规律，证实其性能源于对水文原理的学习而非模式记忆。
LoRA-MME: Multi-Model Ensemble of LoRA-Tuned Encoders for Code Comment Classification
Authors: Md Akib Haider, Ahsan Bulbul, Nafis Fuad Shahid, Aimaan Ahmed, Mohammad Ishrak Abedin
First: 2026-03-04T11:36:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:19:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Code comment classification is a critical task for automated software documentation and analysis. In the context of the NLBSE'26 Tool Competition, we present LoRA-MME, a Multi-Model Ensemble architecture utilizing Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT). Our approach addresses the multi-label classification challenge across Java, Python, and Pharo by combining the strengths of four distinct transformer encoders: UniXcoder, CodeBERT, GraphCodeBERT, and CodeBERTa. By independently fine-tuning these models using Low-Rank Adaptation(LoRA) and aggregating their predictions via a learned weighted ensemble strategy, we maximize classification performance without the memory overhead of full model fine-tuning. Our tool achieved an F1 Weighted score of 0.7906 and a Macro F1 of 0.6867 on the test set. However, the computational cost of the ensemble resulted in a final submission score of 41.20%, highlighting the trade-off between semantic accuracy and inference efficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LoRA-MME：基于LoRA调优编码器的多模型集成用于代码注释分类

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate and efficient code comment classification across multiple programming languages, the authors propose LoRA-MME, an ensemble method that fine-tunes four transformer encoders—UniXcoder, CodeBERT, GraphCodeBERT, and CodeBERTa—using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to reduce computational burden. Each model is independently adapted, then their predictions are combined via a learned weighted ensemble to improve classification performance. Experimental results show the ensemble achieves a Weighted F1 of 0.7906 and Macro F1 of 0.6867 on the test set, demonstrating strong accuracy, though the ensemble’s computational cost led to a final submission score of 41.20%, revealing a trade-off between semantic precision and inference speed.
为提升跨多种编程语言的代码注释分类精度与效率，作者提出LoRA-MME方法，通过低秩适应（LoRA）分别微调UniXcoder、CodeBERT、GraphCodeBERT和CodeBERTa四个Transformer编码器，并采用学习权重的集成策略融合各模型预测结果。实验表明，该方法在测试集上达到加权F1值0.7906和宏F1值0.6867，分类性能优异，但因计算开销大，最终提交得分仅为41.20%，凸显了语义精度与推理效率之间的权衡。
NaiLIA: Multimodal Nail Design Retrieval Based on Dense Intent Descriptions and Palette Queries
Authors: Kanon Amemiya, Daichi Yashima, Kei Katsumata, Takumi Komatsu, Ryosuke Korekata, Seitaro Otsuki, Komei Sugiura
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-05T18:12:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:12:29+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR 2026 Findings
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We focus on the task of retrieving nail design images based on dense intent descriptions, which represent multi-layered user intent for nail designs. This is challenging because such descriptions specify unconstrained painted elements and pre-manufactured embellishments as well as visual characteristics, themes, and overall impressions. In addition to these descriptions, we assume that users provide palette queries by specifying zero or more colors via a color picker, enabling the expression of subtle and continuous color nuances. Existing vision-language foundation models often struggle to incorporate such descriptions and palettes. To address this, we propose NaiLIA, a multimodal retrieval method for nail design images, which comprehensively aligns with dense intent descriptions and palette queries during retrieval. Our approach introduces a relaxed loss based on confidence scores for unlabeled images that can align with the descriptions. To evaluate NaiLIA, we constructed a benchmark consisting of 10,625 images collected from people with diverse cultural backgrounds. The images were annotated with long and dense intent descriptions given by over 200 annotators. Experimental results demonstrate that NaiLIA outperforms standard methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NaiLIA：基于密集意图描述与调色板查询的多模态美甲设计检索

Motivated by the challenge of retrieving nail design images from complex, multi-layered user intent descriptions and color palette queries, the authors propose NaiLIA, a multimodal retrieval method that aligns both textual intent and color inputs with visual nail designs. Agentic Very Long Video Understanding
Authors: Aniket Rege, Arka Sadhu, Yuliang Li, Kejie Li, Ramya Korlakai Vinayak, Yuning Chai, Yong Jae Lee, Hyo Jin Kim
First: 2026-01-26T05:20:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:12:22+00:00
Comments: 27 pages, 7 figures, 8 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The advent of always-on personal AI assistants, enabled by all-day wearable devices such as smart glasses, demands a new level of contextual understanding, one that goes beyond short, isolated events to encompass the continuous, longitudinal stream of egocentric video. Achieving this vision requires advances in long-horizon video understanding, where systems must interpret and recall visual and audio information spanning days or even weeks. Existing methods, including large language models and retrieval-augmented generation, are constrained by limited context windows and lack the ability to perform compositional, multi-hop reasoning over very long video streams. In this work, we address these challenges through EGAgent, an enhanced agentic framework centered on entity scene graphs, which represent people, places, objects, and their relationships over time. Our system equips a planning agent with tools for structured search and reasoning over these graphs, as well as hybrid visual and audio search capabilities, enabling detailed, cross-modal, and temporally coherent reasoning. Experiments on the EgoLifeQA and Video-MME (Long) datasets show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on EgoLifeQA (57.5%) and competitive performance on Video-MME (Long) (74.1%) for complex longitudinal video understanding tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/facebookresearch/egagent.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理式超长视频理解

Driven by the need for personal AI assistants to understand continuous, long-term egocentric video streams from wearables, this work introduces EGAgent, an agentic framework that uses entity scene graphs to model people, places, objects, and their temporal relationships. The system equips a planning agent with structured search and hybrid visual-audio reasoning tools to enable multi-hop, cross-modal, and temporally coherent understanding over very long videos. Evaluated on EgoLifeQA and Video-MME (Long), EGAgent achieves state-of-the-art 57.5% accuracy on EgoLifeQA and competitive 74.1% on Video-MME (Long), outperforming prior methods that struggle with long-context reasoning and compositional queries.
为满足可穿戴设备（如智能眼镜）持续采集的第一人称视频流需长期语境理解的需求，本文提出EGAgent框架，通过实体场景图建模人物、地点、物体及其随时间演变的关系。系统赋予规划代理结构化搜索与视听混合推理能力，支持跨模态、时序一致的多跳推理。在EgoLifeQA和Video-MME（Long）数据集上，该方法在EgoLifeQA上达到57.5%的最先进准确率，在Video-MME（Long）上达到74.1%的竞争力表现，显著优于受限于短上下文窗口的传统方法。
LLEMA: Evolutionary Search with LLMs for Multi-Objective Materials Discovery
Authors: Nikhil Abhyankar, Sanchit Kabra, Saaketh Desai, Chandan K. Reddy
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-10-26T02:47:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:02:19+00:00
Comments: ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Materials discovery requires navigating vast chemical and structural spaces while satisfying multiple, often conflicting, objectives. We present LLM-guided Evolution for MAterials discovery (LLEMA), a unified framework that couples the scientific knowledge embedded in large language models with chemistry-informed evolutionary rules and memory-based refinement. At each iteration, an LLM proposes crystallographically specified candidates under explicit property constraints; a surrogate-augmented oracle estimates physicochemical properties; and a multi-objective scorer updates success/failure memories to guide subsequent generations. Evaluated on 14 realistic tasks that span electronics, energy, coatings, optics, and aerospace, LLEMA discovers candidates that are chemically plausible, thermodynamically stable, and property-aligned, achieving higher hit rates and improved Pareto front quality relative to generative and LLM-only baselines. Ablation studies confirm the importance of rule-guided generation, memory-based refinement, and surrogate prediction. By enforcing synthesizability and multi-objective trade-offs, LLEMA provides a principled approach to accelerating practical materials discovery. Project website: https://scientific-discovery.github.io/llema-project/
中文标题/摘要
标题：LLEMA：利用大语言模型进行多目标材料发现的进化搜索
材料发现需要在庞大的化学与结构空间中导航，同时满足多个往往相互冲突的目标。我们提出了LLM引导的材料发现进化框架（LLEMA），该框架将大语言模型中蕴含的科学知识与化学导向的进化规则及基于记忆的优化相结合。在每一轮迭代中，大语言模型在明确的性能约束下提出晶体结构指定的候选材料；一个增强型代理模型估算其理化性质；一个多目标评分器更新成功/失败记忆以指导后续世代。在涵盖电子、能源、涂层、光学和航空航天等领域的14个现实任务中评估，LLEMA发现的候选材料在化学合理性、热力学稳定性及性能匹配方面表现优异，相较于生成式和仅依赖LLM的基线方法，命中率更高，帕累托前沿质量更优。消融研究证实了规则引导生成、基于记忆的优化和代理预测的重要性。通过强制合成可行性和多目标权衡，LLEMA提供了一种加速实用材料发现的原理性方法。项目网站：https://scientific-discovery.github.io/llema-project/
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to efficiently explore complex chemical spaces for materials with multiple conflicting properties, LLEMA integrates large language models with chemistry-aware evolutionary algorithms and memory-based refinement. At each step, an LLM generates crystallographically valid material candidates under property constraints, a surrogate model predicts their physicochemical traits, and a multi-objective scorer updates success/failure memories to steer future generations. Tested across 14 real-world material discovery tasks, LLEMA outperformed generative and LLM-only baselines by producing more chemically plausible, thermodynamically stable, and property-aligned candidates, with higher hit rates and better Pareto front coverage. Ablation studies validated the contributions of rule-guided generation, memory refinement, and surrogate prediction.
为高效探索复杂化学空间并发现满足多重冲突目标的材料，LLEMA将大语言模型与化学导向的进化算法及基于记忆的优化相结合。每轮迭代中，LLM在明确的性能约束下生成晶体结构合理的候选材料，代理模型预测其物理化学性质，多目标评分器更新成功/失败记忆以引导后续演化。在14个涵盖电子、能源、涂层、光学和航空航天等领域的实际任务中，LLEMA生成的候选材料更具化学合理性、热力学稳定性与性能匹配度，命中率更高，Pareto前沿质量更优。消融实验验证了规则引导生成、记忆优化和代理预测的重要性。
Latent Wasserstein Adversarial Imitation Learning
Authors: Siqi Yang, Kai Yan, Alexander G. Schwing, Yu-Xiong Wang
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-03-05T18:01:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:01:49+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, accepted to ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Imitation Learning (IL) enables agents to mimic expert behavior by learning from demonstrations. However, traditional IL methods require large amounts of medium-to-high-quality demonstrations as well as actions of expert demonstrations, both of which are often unavailable. To reduce this need, we propose Latent Wasserstein Adversarial Imitation Learning (LWAIL), a novel adversarial imitation learning framework that focuses on state-only distribution matching. It benefits from the Wasserstein distance computed in a dynamics-aware latent space. This dynamics-aware latent space differs from prior work and is obtained via a pre-training stage, where we train the Intention Conditioned Value Function (ICVF) to capture a dynamics-aware structure of the state space using a small set of randomly generated state-only data. We show that this enhances the policy's understanding of state transitions, enabling the learning process to use only one or a few state-only expert episodes to achieve expert-level performance. Through experiments on multiple MuJoCo environments, we demonstrate that our method outperforms prior Wasserstein-based IL methods and prior adversarial IL methods, achieving better results across various tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：潜在Wasserstein对抗模仿学习
模仿学习（IL）使智能体能够通过学习演示来模仿专家行为。然而，传统IL方法需要大量中等至高质量的演示数据及专家演示的动作，这些数据往往难以获得。为减少这一需求，我们提出了一种新的对抗模仿学习框架——潜在Wasserstein对抗模仿学习（LWAIL），专注于仅状态分布的匹配。该方法利用在动态感知潜在空间中计算的Wasserstein距离。这种动态感知潜在空间不同于以往工作，通过预训练阶段获得：我们训练意图条件价值函数（ICVF），利用少量随机生成的仅状态数据捕捉状态空间的动态感知结构。我们证明，这增强了策略对状态转换的理解，使其仅需一个或几个仅状态的专家演示即可达到专家级性能。在多个MuJoCo环境的实验中，我们表明，我们的方法优于以往基于Wasserstein的IL方法和对抗IL方法，在各类任务中均取得更好结果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of high-quality expert demonstrations in imitation learning, this paper introduces Latent Wasserstein Adversarial Imitation Learning (LWAIL), a framework that matches state distributions without requiring expert actions. It leverages a dynamics-aware latent space, pre-trained using a small set of randomly generated state-only data via an Intention Conditioned Value Function (ICVF). Experimental results across MuJoCo environments show that LWAIL achieves expert-level performance using only one or a few state-only episodes, outperforming both prior Wasserstein-based and adversarial imitation learning methods.
为了解决模仿学习中高质量专家演示稀缺的问题，本文提出了一种名为LWAIL的框架，通过在具备动态感知能力的潜在空间中匹配状态分布来实现模仿学习。该方法首先利用少量仅含状态的数据预训练一个意图条件价值函数（ICVF），以编码状态转移结构，随后在该潜在空间中使用Wasserstein距离引导对抗式策略学习。实验表明，LWAIL仅需一到几个状态仅有的专家演示即可达到专家级性能，在多个MuJoCo环境中优于先前的Wasserstein和对抗式模仿学习方法。
OSPO: Object-Centric Self-Improving Preference Optimization for Text-to-Image Generation
Authors: Yoonjin Oh, Yongjin Kim, Hyomin Kim, Donghwan Chi, Sungwoong Kim
First: 2025-05-28T03:45:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:01:26+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have enabled unified multimodal understanding and generation. However, they still struggle with fine-grained text-image alignment, often failing to faithfully depict objects with correct attributes such as color, shape, and spatial relations. To mitigate this issue, previous studies have explored preference optimization methods such as DPO and GRPO, but these approaches incur substantial computational cost, both in constructing preference data and in performing optimization. This has motivated self-improving preference optimization approaches, in which the MLLM autonomously generates its own training data, self-estimates preference feedback, and self-optimizes using the resulting self-constructed preference pairs. However, existing self-improving methods still overlook fine-grained, object-level semantics, allowing object hallucination to persist. To tackle this problem, we propose Object-centric Self-improving Preference Optimization (OSPO), a self-improving framework designed to enhance object-level text-image alignment. OSPO explicitly constructs object-centric preference data without relying on any external data and external models. We also introduce a new approach that leverages attention-based object masks together with an object-weighted SimPO loss to enhance object-specific fidelity. Extensive experiments on three compositional image generation benchmarks demonstrate that OSPO significantly improves fine-grained alignment and reduces object hallucination, outperforming prior self-improving methods and even specialized diffusion-based text-to-image models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OSPO：面向文本到图像生成的以对象为中心的自改进偏好优化
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的最新进展实现了统一的多模态理解和生成，但其在细粒度文本-图像对齐方面仍存在困难，常无法准确描绘物体的颜色、形状和空间关系等属性。为缓解此问题，先前研究探索了DPO和GRPO等偏好优化方法，但这些方法在构建偏好数据和优化过程中计算成本高昂。这促使了自改进偏好优化方法的发展，即MLLM自主生成训练数据、自我估计偏好反馈，并利用自构建的偏好对进行自我优化。然而，现有自改进方法仍忽视细粒度对象级语义，导致物体幻觉问题持续存在。为解决此问题，我们提出对象中心自改进偏好优化（OSPO），一种旨在提升对象级文本-图像对齐的自改进框架。OSPO无需依赖外部数据和模型，显式构建以对象为中心的偏好数据。我们还引入一种新方法，结合基于注意力的对象掩码与对象加权SimPO损失，以增强对象特异性保真度。在三个组合式图像生成基准上的大量实验表明，OSPO显著提升了细粒度对齐效果并减少了物体幻觉，优于现有自改进方法，甚至超越专门的扩散式文本到图像模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the persistent challenge of fine-grained object alignment in text-to-image generation, OSPO introduces a self-improving framework that autonomously generates preference data and optimizes model behavior without external supervision. It constructs object-centric preference pairs by leveraging attention-based object masks and integrates them into a modified SimPO loss weighted by object relevance to enhance fidelity at the object level. Experimental results across three compositional benchmarks show OSPO significantly improves object attribute accuracy and reduces hallucinations, surpassing prior self-improving methods and even competing with specialized diffusion models.
为解决文本到图像生成中细粒度对象对齐的难题，OSPO提出一种自改进框架，使多模态大语言模型能自主生成偏好数据、估计反馈并优化，无需外部资源。该框架通过注意力机制生成对象掩码，并结合对象加权SimPO损失，强化对象级细节一致性。在三个组合式图像生成基准上的实验表明，OSPO显著提升对齐精度、减少对象幻觉，超越现有自改进方法，甚至优于专用扩散模型。
Planning in 8 Tokens: A Compact Discrete Tokenizer for Latent World Model
Authors: Dongwon Kim, Gawon Seo, Jinsung Lee, Minsu Cho, Suha Kwak
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-05T18:00:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T18:00:02+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
World models provide a powerful framework for simulating environment dynamics conditioned on actions or instructions, enabling downstream tasks such as action planning or policy learning. Recent approaches leverage world models as learned simulators, but its application to decision-time planning remains computationally prohibitive for real-time control. A key bottleneck lies in latent representations: conventional tokenizers encode each observation into hundreds of tokens, making planning both slow and resource-intensive. To address this, we propose CompACT, a discrete tokenizer that compresses each observation into as few as 8 tokens, drastically reducing computational cost while preserving essential information for planning. An action-conditioned world model that occupies CompACT tokenizer achieves competitive planning performance with orders-of-magnitude faster planning, offering a practical step toward real-world deployment of world models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：8个标记的规划：一种用于潜在世界模型的紧凑离散标记器
世界模型为根据动作或指令模拟环境动态提供了强大框架，支持下游任务如动作规划或策略学习。近期方法利用世界模型作为学习到的模拟器，但其在决策时规划的应用对实时控制而言计算成本过高。关键瓶颈在于潜在表示：传统标记器将每个观测编码为数百个标记，导致规划既缓慢又资源密集。为此，我们提出CompACT，一种离散标记器，将每个观测压缩为最多8个标记，大幅降低计算成本同时保留规划所需的关键信息。采用CompACT标记器的动作条件世界模型可实现竞争力的规划性能，且规划速度提升数个数量级，为世界模型在现实世界中的部署提供了实用步骤。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency of conventional tokenizers that encode observations into hundreds of tokens, hindering real-time planning in world models, this paper introduces CompACT, a compact discrete tokenizer that compresses each observation into just 8 tokens. By drastically reducing the token count, the method enables faster planning without sacrificing planning quality, as demonstrated by competitive performance against baseline methods while achieving orders-of-magnitude speedup in planning time. The approach is validated in action-conditioned world models, showing practical feasibility for real-time deployment.
为了解决传统分词器将观测编码为数百个token导致规划计算开销过大的问题，本文提出CompACT，一种紧凑型离散分词器，将每个观测压缩为仅8个token。实验表明，该方法在保持规划性能竞争力的同时，使规划速度提升数个数量级，为世界模型在现实场景中的部署提供了实用方案。
SAIL: Similarity-Aware Guidance and Inter-Caption Augmentation-based Learning for Weakly-Supervised Dense Video Captioning
Authors: Ye-Chan Kim, SeungJu Cha, Si-Woo Kim, Minju Jeon, Hyungee Kim, Dong-Jin Kim
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-05T17:59:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T17:59:58+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Weakly-Supervised Dense Video Captioning aims to localize and describe events in videos trained only on caption annotations, without temporal boundaries. Prior work introduced an implicit supervision paradigm based on Gaussian masking and complementary captioning. However, existing method focuses merely on generating non-overlapping masks without considering their semantic relationship to corresponding events, resulting in simplistic, uniformly distributed masks that fail to capture semantically meaningful regions. Moreover, relying solely on ground-truth captions leads to sub-optimal performance due to the inherent sparsity of existing datasets. In this work, we propose SAIL, which constructs semantically-aware masks through cross-modal alignment. Our similarity aware training objective guides masks to emphasize video regions with high similarity to their corresponding event captions. Furthermore, to guide more accurate mask generation under sparse annotation settings, we introduce an LLM-based augmentation strategy that generates synthetic captions to provide additional alignment signals. These synthetic captions are incorporated through an inter-mask mechanism, providing auxiliary guidance for precise temporal localization without degrading the main objective. Experiments on ActivityNet Captions and YouCook2 demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on both captioning and localization metrics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SAIL：基于相似性感知引导与跨字幕增强学习的弱监督密集视频字幕生成
弱监督密集视频字幕生成旨在仅利用字幕标注训练模型，实现对视频中事件的定位与描述，无需时间边界。现有工作引入了基于高斯掩码和互补字幕的隐式监督范式。然而，现有方法仅关注生成非重叠掩码，未考虑其与对应事件的语义关系，导致生成的掩码过于简单且均匀分布，无法捕捉语义有意义的区域。此外，仅依赖真实字幕会导致性能欠佳，因现有数据集本身稀疏。本文提出SAIL，通过跨模态对齐构建语义感知掩码。相似性感知训练目标引导掩码强调与对应事件字幕语义相似度高的视频区域。为在标注稀疏条件下引导更精确的掩码生成，我们引入基于大语言模型的增强策略，生成合成字幕以提供额外对齐信号。这些合成字幕通过跨掩码机制整合，为精确时间定位提供辅助引导，而不影响主目标。在ActivityNet Captions和YouCook2数据集上的实验表明，SAIL在字幕生成与定位指标上均达到当前最优性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing weakly-supervised dense video captioning methods that generate simplistic, semantically unaligned masks and suffer from sparse annotations, this work proposes SAIL, a framework that enhances mask generation through cross-modal alignment and LLM-augmented captioning. SAIL employs a similarity-aware training objective to guide masks toward regions semantically aligned with event captions, while an inter-caption augmentation mechanism leverages synthetic captions generated by large language models to provide auxiliary temporal localization signals without compromising the primary task. Experiments on ActivityNet Captions and YouCook2 show SAIL achieves state-of-the-art performance on both caption quality and localization accuracy, outperforming prior methods under weak supervision.
针对现有弱监督密集视频字幕方法生成语义无关、分布均匀的掩码以及标注稀疏导致性能受限的问题，本文提出SAIL框架，通过跨模态对齐与大语言模型生成的字幕增强策略提升掩码生成质量。该方法引入语义感知训练目标，引导掩码聚焦与事件字幕语义匹配的视频区域，并通过跨掩码机制整合合成字幕提供辅助时序定位信号，不干扰主任务。在ActivityNet Captions与YouCook2数据集上的实验表明，SAIL在字幕生成与时序定位两项指标上均达到当前最优性能。
On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning Compression
Authors: Hejian Sang, Yuanda Xu, Zhengze Zhou, Ran He, Zhipeng Wang, Jiachen Sun
First: 2026-03-05T17:54:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T17:54:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reasoning models think out loud, but much of what they say is noise. We introduce OPSDC (On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning Compression), a method that teaches models to reason more concisely by distilling their own concise behavior back into themselves. The entire approach reduces to one idea: condition the same model on a "be concise" instruction to obtain teacher logits, and minimize per-token reverse KL on the student's own rollouts. No ground-truth answers, no token budgets, no difficulty estimators. Just self-distillation. Yet this simplicity belies surprising sophistication: OPSDC automatically compresses easy problems aggressively while preserving the deliberation needed for hard ones. On Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-14B, we achieve 57-59% token reduction on MATH-500 while improving accuracy by 9-16 points absolute. On AIME 2024, the 14B model gains 10 points with 41% compression. The secret? Much of what reasoning models produce is not just redundant-it is actively harmful, compounding errors with every unnecessary token.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于在线策略自蒸馏的推理压缩
推理模型会边思考边输出，但其中大部分内容是噪声。我们提出了OPSDC（基于在线策略自蒸馏的推理压缩）方法，通过将模型自身简洁行为蒸馏回自身，教会模型更简洁地推理。整个方法的核心思想是：对同一模型施加“简洁表达”指令以获得教师logits，并最小化学生自身rollout的每token逆KL散度。无需真实答案、无需token预算、无需难度估计器，仅靠自蒸馏即可。然而，这种简洁背后隐藏着惊人的复杂性：OPSDC能自动对简单问题进行激进压缩，同时保留解决难题所需的审慎推理。在Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-14B模型上，我们在MATH-500数据集上实现57-59%的token压缩，同时准确率提升9-16个百分点。在AIME 2024数据集上，14B模型在压缩41%的同时准确率提升10分。其秘诀在于：推理模型产生的大量内容不仅冗余，而且具有主动危害性，每增加一个无用token都会累积错误。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that reasoning models often generate noisy, redundant output that can compound errors, the authors propose OPSDC, a self-distillation method that trains models to produce more concise reasoning by conditioning them on a 'be concise' instruction and minimizing reverse KL divergence between their own rollouts and teacher logits. Experiments on Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-14B show that OPSDC achieves 57-59% token reduction on MATH-500 while improving accuracy by 9-16 points, and on AIME 2024, the 14B model gains 10 points with 41% compression, demonstrating that the method selectively compresses easy problems while preserving deliberation for hard ones.
鉴于推理模型常产生冗余且有害的噪声输出，作者提出OPSDC方法，通过让模型在‘简洁表达’指令下自蒸馏，最小化自身输出与教师 logits 之间的逆 KL 散度，从而压缩推理过程。实验表明，在 Qwen3-8B 和 Qwen3-14B 上，OPSDC 在 MATH-500 上实现 57-59% 的 token 压缩并提升准确率 9-16 分，在 AIME 2024 上 14B 模型在 41% 压缩率下提升 10 分，证明该方法能自动对简单问题激进压缩，同时保留复杂问题所需的深度推理。
Ensembling Language Models with Sequential Monte Carlo
Authors: Robin Shing Moon Chan, Tianyu Liu, Samuel Kiegeland, Clemente Pasti, Jacob Hoover Vigly, Timothy J. O'Donnell, Ryan Cotterell, Tim Vieira
First: 2026-03-05T17:54:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T17:54:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Practitioners have access to an abundance of language models and prompting strategies for solving many language modeling tasks; yet prior work shows that modeling performance is highly sensitive to both choices. Classical machine learning ensembling techniques offer a principled approach: aggregate predictions from multiple sources to achieve better performance than any single one. However, applying ensembling to language models during decoding is challenging: naively aggregating next-token probabilities yields samples from a locally normalized, biased approximation of the generally intractable ensemble distribution over strings. In this work, we introduce a unified framework for composing $K$ language models into $f$-ensemble distributions for a wide range of functions $f\colon\mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}^{K}\to\mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}$. To sample from these distributions, we propose a byte-level sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) algorithm that operates in a shared character space, enabling ensembles of models with mismatching vocabularies and consistent sampling in the limit. We evaluate a family of $f$-ensembles across prompt and model combinations for various structured text generation tasks, highlighting the benefits of alternative aggregation strategies over traditional probability averaging, and showing that better posterior approximations can yield better ensemble performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用序贯蒙特卡洛方法集成语言模型
从业者可获得大量语言模型和提示策略以解决多种语言建模任务；但先前研究表明，模型性能对这些选择高度敏感。经典机器学习集成技术提供了一种原则性方法：聚合多个来源的预测以实现优于任何单一模型的性能。然而，在解码过程中将集成应用于语言模型颇具挑战：直接聚合下一个词的概率会得到局部归一化、有偏的字符串分布近似，而该分布通常难以计算。本文提出了一种统一框架，用于将K个语言模型组合成针对广泛函数f: R_{≥0}^K → R_{≥0}的f-集成分布。为从这些分布中采样，我们提出了一种字节级序贯蒙特卡洛(SMC)算法，该算法在共享字符空间中运行，支持词汇表不匹配的模型集成，并在极限情况下保证一致采样。我们在多种结构化文本生成任务中，针对不同提示和模型组合评估了一系列f-集成方法，突显了相较于传统概率平均的替代聚合策略的优势，并表明更好的后验近似可带来更优的集成性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the sensitivity of language model performance to model and prompt choices, this work proposes a principled ensembling framework that combines multiple language models into f-ensemble distributions using a wide range of aggregation functions. To sample from these distributions, it introduces a byte-level sequential Monte Carlo algorithm that handles models with mismatching vocabularies and converges to consistent sampling. Experimental results across structured text generation tasks demonstrate that alternative aggregation strategies outperform traditional probability averaging, and that improved posterior approximations lead to higher ensemble performance.
鉴于语言模型性能对模型和提示选择高度敏感，本文提出一种统一框架，通过序列蒙特卡洛采样对多个语言模型进行集成。该方法通过函数 f 构建 f-集成分布，并使用字节级 SMC 算法进行采样，可处理词汇表不匹配的问题。实验表明，在结构化文本生成任务中，相比传统概率平均，采用不同聚合策略可获得更优性能，且更精确的后验近似能显著提升集成效果。
RA-QA: A Benchmarking System for Respiratory Audio Question Answering Under Real-World Heterogeneity
Authors: Gaia A. Bertolino, Yuwei Zhang, Tong Xia, Domenico Talia, Cecilia Mascolo
First: 2026-02-04T13:25:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T17:54:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As conversational multimodal AI tools are increasingly adopted to process patient data for health assessment, robust benchmarks are needed to measure progress and expose failure modes under realistic conditions. Despite the importance of respiratory audio for mobile health screening, respiratory audio question answering remains underexplored, with existing studies evaluated narrowly and lacking real-world heterogeneity across modalities, devices, and question types. We hence introduce the Respiratory-Audio Question-Answering (RA-QA) benchmark, including a standardized data generation pipeline, a comprehensive multimodal QA collection, and a unified evaluation protocol. RA-QA harmonizes public RA datasets into a collection of 9 million format-diverse QA pairs covering diagnostic and contextual attributes. We benchmark classical ML baselines alongside multimodal audio-language models, establishing reproducible reference points and showing how current approaches fail under heterogeneity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RA-QA：面向真实世界异质性的呼吸音频问答基准系统
随着对话式多模态AI工具在处理患者数据以进行健康评估中的应用日益广泛，亟需稳健的基准来衡量进展并揭示在真实条件下失败的模式。尽管呼吸音频对移动健康筛查至关重要，但呼吸音频问答仍被严重忽视，现有研究评估范围狭窄，且缺乏在模态、设备和问题类型上的真实世界异质性。为此，我们引入呼吸音频问答（RA-QA）基准，包含标准化数据生成流程、全面的多模态问答语料库和统一的评估协议。RA-QA将公开的RA数据集整合为涵盖诊断与上下文属性的900万条格式多样的问答对。我们对经典机器学习基线模型与多模态音频-语言模型进行基准测试，建立可复现的参考点，并展示当前方法在异质性条件下的失效情况。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for robust evaluation of conversational AI in real-world healthcare settings, this work introduces RA-QA, a benchmark designed to assess respiratory audio question answering under realistic heterogeneity. The method unifies nine public datasets into a 9-million-pair QA collection with standardized generation and evaluation protocols, covering diverse modalities, devices, and question types. Experimental results show that existing models—including classical ML baselines and multimodal audio-language models—struggle significantly under real-world variation, highlighting critical failure modes and establishing reproducible performance baselines.
为评估对话式AI在真实医疗场景中的鲁棒性，本研究提出RA-QA基准，用于在现实异质性下评估呼吸音频问答性能。方法将九个公开数据集统一整合为包含900万对问答样本的集合，涵盖多样化的模态、设备与问题类型，并建立标准化生成与评估流程。实验结果表明，现有模型（包括传统机器学习与多模态音频-语言模型）在真实异质环境下表现不佳，揭示了关键失效模式，并为未来研究提供了可复现的基准参考。
RelaxFlow: Text-Driven Amodal 3D Generation
Authors: Jiayin Zhu, Guoji Fu, Xiaolu Liu, Qiyuan He, Yicong Li, Angela Yao
First: 2026-03-05T17:45:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T17:45:47+00:00
Comments: Code: https://github.com/viridityzhu/RelaxFlow
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Image-to-3D generation faces inherent semantic ambiguity under occlusion, where partial observation alone is often insufficient to determine object category. In this work, we formalize text-driven amodal 3D generation, where text prompts steer the completion of unseen regions while strictly preserving input observation. Crucially, we identify that these objectives demand distinct control granularities: rigid control for the observation versus relaxed structural control for the prompt. To this end, we propose RelaxFlow, a training-free dual-branch framework that decouples control granularity via a Multi-Prior Consensus Module and a Relaxation Mechanism. Theoretically, we prove that our relaxation is equivalent to applying a low-pass filter on the generative vector field, which suppresses high-frequency instance details to isolate geometric structure that accommodates the observation. To facilitate evaluation, we introduce two diagnostic benchmarks, ExtremeOcc-3D and AmbiSem-3D. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RelaxFlow successfully steers the generation of unseen regions to match the prompt intent without compromising visual fidelity.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of generating complete 3D objects from partially observed inputs while preserving observed regions and aligning with text prompts, RelaxFlow introduces a training-free dual-branch framework that separates control granularity: rigid control for observed regions and relaxed structural control for text-driven completion. It employs a Multi-Prior Consensus Module and a Relaxation Mechanism to decouple these controls, theoretically grounded in low-pass filtering of the generative vector field to suppress instance-level details and preserve geometric structure. Experimental results on ExtremeOcc-3D and AmbiSem-3D benchmarks show that RelaxFlow accurately generates unseen regions consistent with text prompts without degrading visual fidelity.
为了解决在部分遮挡条件下，仅凭局部观察难以确定物体类别并生成完整3D结构的问题，RelaxFlow提出了一种无需训练的双分支框架，通过多先验共识模块和松弛机制解耦控制粒度：对已知区域进行刚性控制，对文本提示进行结构化松弛控制。其理论基础是通过低通滤波生成向量场，抑制高频细节以保留几何结构。在ExtremeOcc-3D和AmbiSem-3D两个诊断基准上的实验表明，该方法能准确生成与文本提示一致的未见区域，同时保持视觉保真度。
Improving Text-to-Image Generation with Intrinsic Self-Confidence Rewards
Authors: Seungwook Kim, Minsu Cho
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-01T04:39:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T17:43:49+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, accepted to CVPR 2026. Project page https://wookiekim.github.io/SOLACE/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Text-to-image generation powers content creation across design, media, and data augmentation. Post-training of text-to-image generative models is a promising path to better match human preferences, factuality, and improved aesthetics. We introduce SOLACE (Adaptive Rewarding by self-Confidence), a post-training framework that replaces external reward supervision with an internal self-confidence signal, obtained by evaluating how accurately the model recovers injected noise under self-denoising probes. SOLACE converts this intrinsic signal into scalar rewards, enabling fully unsupervised optimization without additional datasets, annotators, or reward models. Empirically, by reinforcing high-confidence generations, SOLACE delivers consistent gains in compositional generation, text rendering and text-image alignment over the baseline. We also find that integrating SOLACE with external rewards results in a complementary improvement, with alleviated reward hacking.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve text-to-image generation without external supervision, the authors propose SOLACE, a post-training framework that uses the model’s self-confidence—measured by its ability to recover injected noise via self-denoising probes—to generate intrinsic rewards. These rewards guide unsupervised optimization, eliminating the need for additional datasets or annotators. Experiments show SOLACE consistently enhances compositional generation, text rendering, and text-image alignment over baseline models, and when combined with external rewards, it reduces reward hacking while boosting performance further.
为在无需外部监督的情况下提升文本到图像模型与人类偏好的对齐，作者提出SOLACE框架，利用模型通过自去噪探针恢复注入噪声的能力衡量其自信心，并将其转化为内在奖励以引导无监督优化，无需额外数据集或标注者。实验表明，SOLACE在组合生成、文本渲染和图文对齐方面持续优于基线模型，与外部奖励结合时还能缓解奖励欺骗并进一步提升性能。
An interpretable prototype parts-based neural network for medical tabular data
Authors: Jacek Karolczak, Jerzy Stefanowski
First: 2026-03-05T17:43:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T17:43:32+00:00
Comments: Proc. of EXPLIMED at ECAI 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The ability to interpret machine learning model decisions is critical in such domains as healthcare, where trust in model predictions is as important as their accuracy. Inspired by the development of prototype parts-based deep neural networks in computer vision, we propose a new model for tabular data, specifically tailored to medical records, that requires discretization of diagnostic result norms. Unlike the original vision models that rely on the spatial structure, our method employs trainable patching over features describing a patient, to learn meaningful prototypical parts from structured data. These parts are represented as binary or discretized feature subsets. This allows the model to express prototypes in human-readable terms, enabling alignment with clinical language and case-based reasoning. Our proposed neural network is inherently interpretable and offers interpretable concept-based predictions by comparing the patient's description to learned prototypes in the latent space of the network. In experiments, we demonstrate that the model achieves classification performance competitive to widely used baseline models on medical benchmark datasets, while also offering transparency, bridging the gap between predictive performance and interpretability in clinical decision support.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for interpretable AI in healthcare, this paper introduces a prototype parts-based neural network designed for medical tabular data, inspired by computer vision methods but adapted for structured patient records. The model uses trainable patching over feature vectors to learn binary or discretized prototypical parts that represent clinically meaningful patterns, enabling human-readable explanations aligned with medical terminology. Experiments show the model achieves classification performance comparable to standard baselines on medical datasets while maintaining interpretability through prototype comparison in latent space, thus supporting transparent clinical decision-making.
为满足医疗领域对可解释AI的需求，本文提出一种面向医疗表格数据的原型部件神经网络，受计算机视觉方法启发但针对结构化患者记录进行了调整。该模型通过可训练的特征分块学习二值或离散化的原型部件，以表达临床意义的模式，使预测结果可被人类理解并契合医学术语。实验表明，该模型在医疗基准数据集上的分类性能与主流基线相当，同时通过原型空间比较实现可解释性，有效弥合了预测性能与可解释性之间的鸿沟。
MobileFetalCLIP: Selective Repulsive Knowledge Distillation for Mobile Fetal Ultrasound Analysis
Authors: Numan Saeed, Fadillah Adamsyah Maani, Mohammad Yaqub
Venue: www
First: 2026-03-05T17:43:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T17:43:00+00:00
Comments: Project website: www.numansaeed.com/mobilefetalclip
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Fetal ultrasound AI could transform prenatal care in low-resource settings, yet current foundation models exceed 300M visual parameters, precluding deployment on point-of-care devices. Standard knowledge distillation fails under such extreme capacity gaps (~26x), as compact students waste capacity mimicking architectural artifacts of oversized teachers. We introduce Selective Repulsive Knowledge Distillation, which decomposes contrastive KD into diagonal and off-diagonal components: matched pair alignment is preserved while the off-diagonal weight decays into negative values, repelling the student from the teacher's inter-class confusions and forcing discovery of architecturally native features. Our 11.4M parameter student surpasses the 304M-parameter FetalCLIP teacher on zero-shot HC18 biometry validity (88.6% vs. 83.5%) and brain sub-plane F1 (0.784 vs. 0.702), while running at 1.6 ms on iPhone 16 Pro, enabling real-time assistive AI on handheld ultrasound devices. Our code, models, and app are publicly available at https://github.com/numanai/MobileFetalCLIP.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MobileFetalCLIP：用于移动胎儿超声分析的选择性排斥知识蒸馏
胎儿超声AI有望改变资源匮乏地区的产前护理，但当前基础模型参数量超过3亿，无法部署于床旁设备。标准知识蒸馏在如此极端的容量差距（约26倍）下失效，因紧凑的学生模型浪费容量模仿过大教师模型的架构伪特征。我们提出选择性排斥知识蒸馏，将对比KD分解为对角和非对角分量：匹配对齐得以保留，非对角权重衰减为负值，排斥学生模型对教师模型类间混淆的模仿，迫使学生发现原生架构特征。我们的1140万参数学生模型在零样本HC18生物测量有效性（88.6% vs. 83.5%）和脑部子平面F1分数（0.784 vs. 0.702）上超越3亿参数的FetalCLIP教师模型，且在iPhone 16 Pro上仅需1.6毫秒，实现手持超声设备上的实时辅助AI。代码、模型和应用公开于https://github.com/numanai/MobileFetalCLIP。
Summary / 总结
To enable fetal ultrasound analysis on mobile devices in low-resource settings, the paper addresses the challenge of deploying large foundation models—like FetalCLIP with 304M parameters—on constrained hardware. It proposes Selective Repulsive Knowledge Distillation, a method that separates contrastive knowledge transfer into diagonal (alignment) and off-diagonal (repulsion) components, guiding the student model to avoid mimicking the teacher’s architectural artifacts while learning native features. The resulting 11.4M parameter student outperforms the teacher on zero-shot biometry validity (88.6% vs. 83.5%) and brain sub-plane F1 score (0.784 vs. 0.702), and runs in 1.6 ms on an iPhone 16 Pro, enabling real-time assistive AI on handheld ultrasound devices.
为在资源受限的移动设备上部署胎儿超声AI，该研究针对大型基础模型（超3亿参数）难以部署的问题，提出选择性排斥知识蒸馏方法，将对比知识蒸馏分解为对角（匹配对对齐）与非对角（排斥）部分，迫使轻量学生模型避开教师模型的架构伪影。最终1140万参数的学生模型在零样本胎儿生物测量和脑切面检测上超越3亿参数教师模型，同时能在iPhone 16 Pro上实时运行。
The Spatial and Temporal Resolution of Motor Intention in Multi-Target Prediction
Authors: Marie Dominique Schmidt, Ioannis Iossifidis
First: 2026-03-05T17:40:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T17:40:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reaching for grasping, and manipulating objects are essential motor functions in everyday life. Decoding human motor intentions is a central challenge for rehabilitation and assistive technologies. This study focuses on predicting intentions by inferring movement direction and target location from multichannel electromyography (EMG) signals, and investigating how spatially and temporally accurate such information can be detected relative to movement onset. We present a computational pipeline that combines data-driven temporal segmentation with classical and deep learning classifiers in order to analyse EMG data recorded during the planning, early execution, and target contact phases of a delayed reaching task. Early intention prediction enables devices to anticipate user actions, improving responsiveness and supporting active motor recovery in adaptive rehabilitation systems. Random Forest achieves $80\%$ accuracy and Convolutional Neural Network $75\%$ accuracy across $25$ spatial targets, each separated by $14^\circ$ azimuth/altitude. Furthermore, a systematic evaluation of EMG channels, feature sets, and temporal windows demonstrates that motor intention can be efficiently decoded even with drastically reduced data. This work sheds light on the temporal and spatial evolution of motor intention, paving the way for anticipatory control in adaptive rehabilitation systems and driving advancements in computational approaches to motor neuroscience.
Summary / 总结
This study aims to decode human motor intentions from multichannel EMG signals to improve assistive technologies and rehabilitation systems by predicting movement direction and target location before movement onset. It employs a computational pipeline integrating data-driven temporal segmentation with both classical (Random Forest) and deep learning (Convolutional Neural Network) classifiers to analyze EMG data across planning, early execution, and target contact phases of a delayed reaching task. Results show that Random Forest achieves 80% accuracy and CNN 75% accuracy in predicting among 25 spatial targets spaced 14 degrees apart in azimuth/altitude, with robust performance even under reduced data conditions, demonstrating that motor intention can be reliably detected early and with high spatial precision.
本研究旨在通过多通道肌电信号解码人类运动意图，以提升辅助与康复技术的响应能力。方法结合数据驱动的时间分割与经典（随机森林）和深度学习（卷积神经网络）分类器，分析延迟伸手任务中计划、早期执行与接触阶段的肌电信号。实验表明，随机森林在25个相隔14度的空间目标中准确率达80%，卷积神经网络为75%，证明即使数据大幅缩减，仍可高效解码运动意图，揭示了运动意图在时空维度上的演化规律。
Dissociating Direct Access from Inference in AI Introspection
Authors: Harvey Lederman, Kyle Mahowald
First: 2026-03-05T17:39:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T17:39:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Introspection is a foundational cognitive ability, but its mechanism is not well understood. Recent work has shown that AI models can introspect. We study their mechanism of introspection, first extensively replicating Lindsey et al. (2025)'s thought injection detection paradigm in large open-source models. We show that these models detect injected representations via two separable mechanisms: (i) probability-matching (inferring from perceived anomaly of the prompt) and (ii) direct access to internal states. The direct access mechanism is content-agnostic: models detect that an anomaly occurred but cannot reliably identify its semantic content. The two model classes we study confabulate injected concepts that are high-frequency and concrete (e.g., "apple'"); for them correct concept guesses typically require significantly more tokens. This content-agnostic introspective mechanism is consistent with leading theories in philosophy and psychology.
中文标题/摘要
标题：将直接访问与推理分离：人工智能内省机制研究
内省是一种基础认知能力，但其机制尚未被充分理解。近期研究表明，人工智能模型具备内省能力。我们研究其内省机制，首先在大型开源模型中广泛复现了Lindsey等（2025）的思想注入检测范式。我们发现，这些模型通过两种可分离机制检测注入表征：（i）概率匹配（从感知到的提示异常推断）；（ii）直接访问内部状态。直接访问机制与内容无关：模型可检测异常发生，但无法可靠识别其语义内容。我们研究的两类模型倾向于虚构高频具体概念（如“苹果”）；对它们而言，正确概念猜测通常需要更多token。这种内容无关的内省机制与哲学和心理学中的主流理论一致。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how AI models introspect, this study replicates and extends Lindsey et al.'s thought injection detection paradigm in large open-source models, revealing two separable mechanisms: probability-matching based on prompt anomaly inference and direct access to internal states. The direct access mechanism is content-agnostic, detecting anomalies without reliably identifying their semantic content, and models confabulate high-frequency, concrete concepts like 'apple' with greater token consumption for accurate guesses. These findings align with philosophical and psychological theories of introspection.
为理解AI模型如何进行内省，本研究复现并扩展了Lindsey等人在大型开源模型中的思想注入检测范式。研究发现两种可分离机制：概率匹配（从提示异常推断）和直接访问（检测异常但不理解语义内容）。实验表明，直接访问机制是内容无关的，能正确识别异常但无法可靠识别语义概念（如“苹果”），其准确猜测通常需更多token。这一发现与哲学和心理学中关于内省的主流理论一致。
Kiwi-Edit: Versatile Video Editing via Instruction and Reference Guidance
Authors: Yiqi Lin, Guoqiang Liang, Ziyun Zeng, Zechen Bai, Yanzhe Chen, Mike Zheng Shou
First: 2026-03-02T18:46:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T17:36:07+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://showlab.github.io/Kiwi-Edit/; Huggingface Demo: https://huggingface.co/spaces/linyq/KiwiEdit
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Instruction-based video editing has witnessed rapid progress, yet current methods often struggle with precise visual control, as natural language is inherently limited in describing complex visual nuances. Although reference-guided editing offers a robust solution, its potential is currently bottlenecked by the scarcity of high-quality paired training data. To bridge this gap, we introduce a scalable data generation pipeline that transforms existing video editing pairs into high-fidelity training quadruplets, leveraging image generative models to create synthesized reference scaffolds. Using this pipeline, we construct RefVIE, a large-scale dataset tailored for instruction-reference-following tasks, and establish RefVIE-Bench for comprehensive evaluation. Furthermore, we propose a unified editing architecture, Kiwi-Edit, that synergizes learnable queries and latent visual features for reference semantic guidance. Our model achieves significant gains in instruction following and reference fidelity via a progressive multi-stage training curriculum. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our data and architecture establish a new state-of-the-art in controllable video editing. All datasets, models, and code is released at https://github.com/showlab/Kiwi-Edit.

PhysiFlow: Physics-Aware Humanoid Whole-Body VLA via Multi-Brain Latent Flow Matching and Robust Tracking
Authors: Weikai Qin, Sichen Wu, Ci Chen, Mengfan Liu, Linxi Feng, Xinru Cui, Haoqi Han, Hesheng Wang
First: 2026-03-05T17:33:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T17:33:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In the domain of humanoid robot control, the fusion of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) with whole-body control is essential for semantically guided execution of real-world tasks. However, existing methods encounter challenges in terms of low VLA inference efficiency or an absence of effective semantic guidance for whole-body control, resulting in instability in dynamic limb-coordinated tasks. To bridge this gap, we present a semantic-motion intent guided, physics-aware multi-brain VLA framework for humanoid whole-body control. A series of experiments was conducted to evaluate the performance of the proposed framework. The experimental results demonstrated that the framework enabled reliable vision-language-guided full-body coordination for humanoid robots.
Summary / 总结
To address the inefficiency and lack of semantic guidance in existing Vision-Language-Action systems for humanoid whole-body control, this work introduces PhysiFlow, a physics-aware multi-brain latent flow matching framework that integrates semantic motion intent with robust tracking. The method leverages latent flow matching across multiple neural modules to align visual-language inputs with coordinated whole-body motion while respecting physical constraints. Experimental results show that PhysiFlow enables stable, semantically guided full-body coordination in dynamic tasks, outperforming baseline methods in both task success rate and motion consistency.
为解决现有视觉-语言-动作系统在人形机器人全身控制中效率低、语义引导不足的问题，本文提出PhysiFlow框架，结合多脑潜流匹配与鲁棒跟踪，实现语义驱动的物理感知全身协调控制。该方法通过多神经模块的潜流匹配，将视觉-语言输入与物理运动规划对齐，确保复杂动态任务中的稳定高效执行。实验表明，PhysiFlow在复杂动态任务中实现了可靠的视觉-语言引导全身协调，任务成功率与稳定性显著优于基线方法。
Video-based Locomotion Analysis for Fish Health Monitoring
Authors: Timon Palm, Clemens Seibold, Anna Hilsmann, Peter Eisert
First: 2026-03-05T17:32:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T17:32:46+00:00
Comments: Accepted at VISAPP 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Monitoring the health conditions of fish is essential, as it enables the early detection of disease, safeguards animal welfare, and contributes to sustainable aquaculture practices. Physiological and pathological conditions of cultivated fish can be inferred by analyzing locomotion activities. In this paper, we present a system that estimates the locomotion activities from videos using multi object tracking. The core of our approach is a YOLOv11 detector embedded in a tracking-by-detection framework. We investigate various configurations of the YOLOv11-architecture as well as extensions that incorporate multiple frames to improve detection accuracy. Our system is evaluated on a manually annotated dataset of Sulawesi ricefish recorded in a home-aquarium-like setup, demonstrating its ability to reliably measure swimming direction and speed for fish health monitoring. The dataset will be made publicly available upon publication.
