Differentially Private and Scalable Estimation of the Network Principal Component
Authors: Alireza Khayatian, Anil Vullikanti, Aritra Konar
First: 2025-05-06T14:10:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T17:31:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Computing the principal component (PC) of the adjacency matrix of an undirected graph has several applications ranging from identifying key vertices for influence maximization and controlling diffusion processes, to discovering densely interconnected vertex subsets. However, many networked datasets are sensitive, which necessitates private computation of the PC for use in the aforementioned applications. Differential privacy has emerged as the gold standard in privacy-preserving data analysis, but existing DP algorithms for private PC suffer from low accuracy due to large noise injection or high complexity. Motivated by the large gap between the local and global sensitivities of the PC on real-graphs, we consider instance-specific mechanisms for privately computing the PC under edge-DP. These mechanisms guarantee privacy for all datasets, but provide good utility on ``well-behaved'' datasets by injecting smaller amounts of noise. More specifically, we consider the Propose-Test-Release (PTR) framework. Although computationally expensive in general, we design a novel approach for implementing a PTR variant in the same time as computation of a non-private PC, while offering good utility. Our framework tests in a differentially-private manner whether a given graph is ``well-behaved'' or not, and then tests whether its private to release a noisy PC with small noise. As a consequence, this also leads to the first DP algorithm for the Densest-$k$-subgraph problem, a key graph mining primitive. We run our method on diverse real-world networks, with the largest having 3 million vertices, and compare its utility to a pre-existing baseline based on the private power method (PPM). Although PTR requires a slightly larger privacy budget, on average, it achieves a 180-fold improvement in runtime over PPM.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for privacy-preserving computation of network principal components in sensitive graph datasets, this work proposes instance-specific differentially private mechanisms under edge-DP that adapt noise levels based on graph structure. Leveraging the Propose-Test-Release (PTR) framework, it efficiently tests whether a graph is 'well-behaved' and releases a low-noise PC if so, otherwise injecting more noise. The method achieves comparable or better utility than prior approaches while drastically reducing runtime—180-fold faster on large graphs—by aligning noise with local sensitivity. It also yields the first differentially private algorithm for the Densest-k-subgraph problem.
为在保护隐私的同时提升网络主成分计算的实用性，该研究针对现有差分隐私方法噪声大或计算成本高的问题，提出基于图是否“行为良好”的实例自适应机制。方法采用Propose-Test-Release框架，在差分隐私下测试图的特性，若图行为良好则注入较小噪声并高效发布主成分。在包含300万节点的真实网络数据上实验表明，尽管隐私预算略高，该方法相较基准私有幂法（PPM）运行速度提升180倍，且实用性显著增强。
ROScopter: A Multirotor Autopilot based on ROSflight 2.0
Authors: Jacob Moore, Ian Reid, Phil Tokumaru, Tim McLain
First: 2026-03-05T17:30:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T17:30:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
ROScopter is a lean multirotor autopilot built for researchers. ROScopter seeks to accelerate simulation and hardware testing of research code with an architecture that is both easy to understand and simple to modify. ROScopter is designed to interface with ROSflight 2.0 and runs entirely on an onboard flight computer, leveraging the features of ROS 2 to improve modularity. This work describes the architecture of ROScopter and how it can be used to test application code in both simulated and hardware environments. Hardware results of the default ROScopter behavior are presented, showing that ROScopter achieves similar performance to another state-of-the-art autopilot for basic waypoint-following maneuvers, but with a significantly reduced and more modular code-base.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a lightweight, easily modifiable autopilot system for rapid research prototyping, ROScopter was developed as a ROS 2-based multirotor autopilot built atop ROSflight 2.0, running entirely on an onboard flight computer. Its architecture emphasizes simplicity and modularity, enabling seamless integration with ROS 2 tools for simulation and hardware testing. Experimental results demonstrate that ROScopter achieves comparable performance to a state-of-the-art autopilot in basic waypoint-following tasks, while offering a significantly reduced and more modular codebase, facilitating faster development and iteration.
为加速多旋翼系统研究，ROScopter 作为基于 ROSflight 2.0 的轻量级自主飞行控制器被设计出来，完全运行于机载飞行计算机上，利用 ROS 2 的模块化特性。该系统可无缝对接 ROS 2，支持在仿真与真实硬件环境中测试应用代码。实验结果表明，ROScopter 在基础航点跟随任务中性能与现有顶尖自主飞行控制器相当，但代码结构更精简、模块化程度更高。
An Exploration-Analysis-Disambiguation Reasoning Framework for Word Sense Disambiguation with Low-Parameter LLMs
Authors: Deshan Sumanathilaka, Nicholas Micallef, Julian Hough
First: 2026-03-05T17:27:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T17:27:42+00:00
Comments: Accepted at LREC 2026, 15 pages, 11 Tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Word Sense Disambiguation (WSD) remains a key challenge in Natural Language Processing (NLP), especially when dealing with rare or domain-specific senses that are often misinterpreted. While modern high-parameter Large Language Models (LLMs) such as GPT-4-Turbo have shown state-of-the-art WSD performance, their computational and energy demands limit scalability. This study investigates whether low-parameter LLMs (<4B parameters) can achieve comparable results through fine-tuning strategies that emphasize reasoning-driven sense identification. Using the FEWS dataset augmented with semi-automated, rationale-rich annotations, we fine-tune eight small-scale open-source LLMs (e.g. Gemma and Qwen). Our results reveal that Chain-of-Thought (CoT)-based reasoning combined with neighbour-word analysis achieves performance comparable to GPT-4-Turbo in zero-shot settings. Importantly, Gemma-3-4B and Qwen-3-4B models consistently outperform all medium-parameter baselines and state-of-the-art models on FEWS, with robust generalization to unseen senses. Furthermore, evaluation on the unseen "Fool Me If You Can'' dataset confirms strong cross-domain adaptability without task-specific fine-tuning. This work demonstrates that with carefully crafted reasoning-centric fine-tuning, low-parameter LLMs can deliver accurate WSD while substantially reducing computational and energy demands.
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the challenge of word sense disambiguation (WSD) for rare or domain-specific senses, which modern high-parameter LLMs struggle with due to high computational costs. The authors propose a reasoning-driven framework that combines exploration, analysis, and disambiguation steps, fine-tuning eight low-parameter LLMs (under 4B parameters) on a rationale-rich FEWS dataset. Results show that Chain-of-Thought reasoning with neighbor-word analysis enables these small models to match GPT-4-Turbo’s zero-shot performance, with Gemma-3-4B and Qwen-3-4B achieving state-of-the-art results on FEWS and demonstrating strong generalization to unseen senses. Cross-domain evaluation on the "Fool Me If You Can" dataset further confirms their adaptability without task-specific fine-tuning.
为降低大语言模型在词义消歧（WSD）任务中的计算开销，本研究探索低参数模型（<4B）通过推理导向的微调是否可实现竞争力表现。作者在半自动标注的FEWS数据集上，对八款小型开源LLM进行微调，结合链式思维（CoT）与邻词分析构建探索-分析-消歧推理框架。实验表明，Gemma-3-4B与Qwen-3-4B模型在零样本设置下性能媲美GPT-4-Turbo，并在未见词义上表现稳健，且在“Fool Me If You Can”跨域数据集上无需额外微调即保持强适应性。
Judge Reliability Harness: Stress Testing the Reliability of LLM Judges
Authors: Sunishchal Dev, Andrew Sloan, Joshua Kavner, Nicholas Kong, Morgan Sandler
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-03-05T17:27:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T17:27:07+00:00
Comments: Accepted at Agents in the Wild: Safety, Security, and Beyond Workshop at ICLR 2026 - April 26, 2026, Rio de Janeiro, Brazil
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We present the Judge Reliability Harness, an open source library for constructing validation suites that test the reliability of LLM judges. As LLM based scoring is widely deployed in AI benchmarks, more tooling is needed to efficiently assess the reliability of these methods. Given a benchmark dataset and an LLM judge configuration, the harness generates reliability tests that evaluate both binary judgment accuracy and ordinal grading performance for free-response and agentic task formats. We evaluate four state-of-the-art judges across four benchmarks spanning safety, persuasion, misuse, and agentic behavior, and find meaningful variation in performance across models and perturbation types, highlighting opportunities to improve the robustness of LLM judges. No judge that we evaluated is uniformly reliable across benchmarks using our harness. For example, our preliminary experiments on judges revealed consistency issues as measured by accuracy in judging another LLM's ability to complete a task due to simple text formatting changes, paraphrasing, changes in verbosity, and flipping the ground truth label in LLM-produced responses. The code for this tool is available at: https://github.com/RANDCorporation/judge-reliability-harness
中文标题/摘要
标题：法官可靠性测试框架：对大语言模型法官可靠性的压力测试
我们提出了法官可靠性测试框架，这是一个开源库，用于构建测试大语言模型法官可靠性的验证套件。随着基于大语言模型的评分在AI基准测试中广泛应用，亟需更多工具以高效评估这些方法的可靠性。给定一个基准数据集和一个大语言模型法官配置，该框架可自动生成可靠性测试，评估自由回答和代理任务格式下的二元判断准确性和等级评分性能。我们在四个涵盖安全、说服、误用和代理行为的基准上评估了四个最先进的法官模型，发现不同模型和扰动类型间存在显著性能差异，凸显了提升大语言模型法官鲁棒性的机会。我们评估的所有法官均未在所有基准上表现出统一的可靠性。例如，初步实验表明，法官在判断另一大语言模型完成任务能力时存在一致性问题，这由简单的文本格式变化、同义改写、语句详略调整以及翻转大语言模型生成回答的真实标签所导致。该工具代码可访问：https://github.com/RANDCorporation/judge-reliability-harness
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing deployment of LLM-based scoring systems in AI benchmarks, this work introduces the Judge Reliability Harness, an open-source tool designed to systematically stress-test the reliability of LLM judges. Given a benchmark and judge configuration, the harness automatically generates tests evaluating both binary judgment accuracy and ordinal grading performance across free-response and agentic tasks. Experimental results across four state-of-the-art judges and four benchmarks—covering safety, persuasion, misuse, and agentic behavior—reveal significant performance variation among models and across perturbation types, indicating that no judge is uniformly reliable. Notably, simple changes like text formatting, paraphrasing, verbosity shifts, or label flipping in LLM-generated responses led to inconsistent judgments, underscoring the need for more robust evaluation methods.
鉴于LLM评分在AI基准测试中的广泛应用，本研究提出Judge Reliability Harness这一开源工具，用于系统性地压力测试LLM裁判的可靠性。给定基准数据和裁判配置，该工具可自动生成测试，评估二元判断与等级评分在自由回答和代理任务中的准确性。在四个基准（安全、说服、滥用、代理行为）上对四个前沿裁判模型的实验表明，不同模型和扰动类型下性能差异显著，且无一裁判在所有基准上表现稳定。尤其发现，如文本格式调整、同义改写、语句详略变化或LLM生成答案的标签翻转等简单扰动，都会导致判断不一致，凸显了构建更鲁棒评估方法的必要性。
OPPO: Accelerating PPO-based RLHF via Pipeline Overlap
Authors: Kaizhuo Yan, Yingjie Yu, Yifan Yu, Haizhong Zheng, Fan Lai
First: 2025-09-30T04:26:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T17:25:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO)-based reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) is a widely adopted paradigm for aligning large language models (LLMs) with human preferences. However, its training pipeline suffers from substantial inefficiencies due to sequential multi-model dependencies (e.g., reward model depends on actor outputs) and long-tail response lengths, where a few long responses straggle the stage completion. We present OPPO, a novel, lightweight, and model-agnostic PPO-based RLHF framework that improves training efficiency by overlapping pipeline execution. OPPO introduces two novel techniques: (1) Intra-step overlap, which streams upstream model outputs (e.g., actor model) in right-sized chunks, enabling the downstream model (e.g., reward) to begin prefill while the upstream continues decoding; and (2) Inter-step overlap, which adaptively overcommits a few prompts and defers long generations to future steps, mitigating tail latency without discarding partial work. OPPO integrates easily with existing PPO implementations with a lightweight wrapper. Extensive evaluations show that OPPO accelerates PPO-based RLHF training by $1.8\times$--$2.8\times$ and improves GPU utilization by $1.4\times$--$2.1\times$ without compromising training convergence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OPPO：通过流水线重叠加速基于PPO的RLHF
基于近端策略优化（PPO）的强化学习人类反馈（RLHF）是广泛采用的用于对齐大语言模型（LLMs）与人类偏好的范式。然而，其训练流程因多模型串行依赖（如奖励模型依赖于演员模型输出）和长尾响应长度而存在显著效率低下问题，少数长响应会拖慢阶段完成。我们提出OPPO，一种新颖、轻量且模型无关的基于PPO的RLHF框架，通过重叠流水线执行提升训练效率。OPPO引入两项新技术：（1）步骤内重叠，将上游模型输出（如演员模型）按合适块流式传输，使下游模型（如奖励模型）在上游继续解码的同时开始预填充；（2）步骤间重叠，自适应超提交部分提示并延迟长生成至未来步骤，缓解尾部延迟而不丢弃部分工作。OPPO可通过轻量级包装器轻松集成现有PPO实现。大量评估表明，OPPO可将基于PPO的RLHF训练加速1.8–2.8倍，GPU利用率提升1.4–2.1倍，且不损害训练收敛性。
Summary / 总结
To address inefficiencies in PPO-based RLHF training caused by sequential multi-model dependencies and long-tail response latency, OPPO introduces a lightweight, model-agnostic framework that overlaps pipeline execution via two techniques: intra-step overlap, which streams actor outputs in chunks to allow reward model prefill while decoding continues, and inter-step overlap, which adaptively overcommits prompts and defers long generations to future steps without discarding partial work. Experimental results show OPPO accelerates training by 1.8x–2.8x and boosts GPU utilization by 1.4x–2.1x without affecting convergence.
为解决PPO-based RLHF训练中因多模型串行依赖和长尾响应延迟导致的效率低下问题，OPPO提出一种轻量级、模型无关的框架，通过两种技术实现流水线重叠：内步重叠将演员模型输出分块流式传输，使奖励模型在演员继续解码时即可开始预填充；外步重叠则动态超发部分提示并延迟长生成至后续步骤，避免丢弃部分工作。实验表明，OPPO可将训练速度提升1.8–2.8倍，GPU利用率提升1.4–2.1倍，且不影响收敛效果。
Loop Closure via Maximal Cliques in 3D LiDAR-Based SLAM
Authors: Javier Laserna, Saurabh Gupta, Oscar Martinez Mozos, Cyrill Stachniss, Pablo San Segundo
First: 2026-03-05T17:24:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T17:24:44+00:00
Comments: Accepted in the 2025 European Conference on Mobile Robots (ECMR). This is the author's version of the work
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reliable loop closure detection remains a critical challenge in 3D LiDAR-based SLAM, especially under sensor noise, environmental ambiguity, and viewpoint variation conditions. RANSAC is often used in the context of loop closures for geometric model fitting in the presence of outliers. However, this approach may fail, leading to map inconsistency. We introduce a novel deterministic algorithm, CliReg, for loop closure validation that replaces RANSAC verification with a maximal clique search over a compatibility graph of feature correspondences. This formulation avoids random sampling and increases robustness in the presence of noise and outliers. We integrated our approach into a real- time pipeline employing binary 3D descriptors and a Hamming distance embedding binary search tree-based matching. We evaluated it on multiple real-world datasets featuring diverse LiDAR sensors. The results demonstrate that our proposed technique consistently achieves a lower pose error and more reliable loop closures than RANSAC, especially in sparse or ambiguous conditions. Additional experiments on 2D projection-based maps confirm its generality across spatial domains, making our approach a robust and efficient alternative for loop closure detection.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于3D激光雷达SLAM的循环闭合通过最大团
在3D激光雷达SLAM中，可靠的循环闭合检测在传感器噪声、环境歧义和视角变化条件下仍是一个关键挑战。RANSAC常用于循环闭合的几何模型拟合，但该方法可能失效，导致地图不一致。我们提出了一种新颖的确定性算法CliReg，通过在特征对应兼容图上搜索最大团来替代RANSAC验证。该方法避免了随机采样，在噪声和异常值环境下更具鲁棒性。我们将该方法集成到一个实时管道中，采用二进制3D描述符和基于汉明距离的二叉搜索树匹配。在多个包含不同激光雷达传感器的真实世界数据集上评估，结果表明，我们的方法在稀疏或模糊条件下始终比RANSAC实现更低的姿态误差和更可靠的循环闭合。在基于2D投影的地图上的额外实验验证了其在空间域中的通用性，使我们的方法成为循环闭合检测的稳健高效替代方案。
Summary / 总结
To address the unreliability of RANSAC in detecting loop closures under noisy and ambiguous 3D LiDAR data, the authors propose CliReg, a deterministic method that replaces random sampling with maximal clique search over a compatibility graph of feature correspondences, enhancing robustness against outliers. Integrated into a real-time pipeline using binary descriptors and Hamming distance-based matching, CliReg was tested across diverse real-world datasets and consistently outperformed RANSAC by reducing pose error and improving closure reliability, especially in sparse or ambiguous environments. Additional 2D projection experiments confirmed its cross-domain generalization, validating its efficiency and robustness for SLAM applications.
为解决3D激光雷达SLAM中在噪声和环境模糊条件下可靠的回环检测难题，作者提出CliReg算法，用特征对应兼容图上的最大团搜索替代RANSAC，避免随机采样，提升鲁棒性。该方法集成于基于二进制3D描述符和汉明距离嵌入二叉搜索树的实时管道中，并在多个含不同激光雷达传感器的真实数据集上测试。结果表明，CliReg在稀疏或模糊环境下比RANSAC更稳定，姿态误差更低，且在2D投影地图上的实验验证了其跨空间域的通用性。
Harnessing Synthetic Data from Generative AI for Statistical Inference
Authors: Ahmad Abdel-Azim, Ruoyu Wang, Xihong Lin
First: 2026-03-05T17:24:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T17:24:41+00:00
Comments: Submitted to Statistical Science
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The emergence of generative AI models has dramatically expanded the availability and use of synthetic data across scientific, industrial, and policy domains. While these developments open new possibilities for data analysis, they also raise fundamental statistical questions about when synthetic data can be used in a valid, reliable, and principled manner. This paper reviews the current landscape of synthetic data generation and use from a statistical perspective, with the goal of clarifying the assumptions under which synthetic data can meaningfully support downstream discovery, inference, and prediction. We survey major classes of modern generative models, their intended use cases, and the benefits they offer, while also highlighting their limitations and characteristic failure modes. We additionally examine common pitfalls that arise when synthetic data are treated as surrogates for real observations, including biases from model misspecification, attenuated uncertainty, and difficulties in generalization. Building on these insights, we discuss emerging frameworks for the principled use of synthetic data. We conclude with practical recommendations, open problems, and cautions intended to guide both method developers and applied researchers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用生成式人工智能合成数据进行统计推断
生成式人工智能模型的出现极大地扩展了科学、工业和政策领域中合成数据的可用性和应用。尽管这些发展为数据分析开辟了新可能，但也提出了关于在何种条件下合成数据可被有效、可靠且原则性地使用的根本性统计问题。本文从统计学角度回顾当前合成数据生成与应用的现状，旨在阐明合成数据在支持下游发现、推断和预测时所依赖的假设条件。我们综述了现代主要生成模型的类别、其预期应用场景及其优势，同时指出其局限性与典型失效模式。此外，我们还探讨了将合成数据视为真实观测替代品时常见的陷阱，包括模型误设导致的偏差、不确定性减弱以及泛化困难。基于这些见解，我们讨论了合成数据原则性使用的新兴框架，并以实用建议、开放问题和警示收尾，旨在指导方法开发者和应用研究人员。

Motivated by the rapid rise of generative AI and its ability to produce synthetic data for diverse applications, this paper investigates the statistical validity of using such data for inference. It reviews major generative model classes, their intended uses, and their statistical strengths and weaknesses, while identifying common pitfalls such as model misspecification bias, reduced uncertainty quantification, and poor generalization. On the Necessity of Learnable Sheaf Laplacians
Authors: Ferran Hernandez Caralt, Mar Gonzàlez i Català, Adrián Bazaga, Pietro Liò
First: 2026-03-05T17:24:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T17:24:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sheaf Neural Networks (SNNs) were introduced as an extension of Graph Convolutional Networks to address oversmoothing on heterophilous graphs by attaching a sheaf to the input graph and replacing the adjacency-based operator with a sheaf Laplacian defined by (learnable) restriction maps. Prior work motivates this design through theoretical properties of sheaf diffusion and the kernel of the sheaf Laplacian, suggesting that suitable non-identity restriction maps can avoid representations converging to constants across connected components. Since oversmoothing can also be mitigated through residual connections and normalization, we revisit a trivial sheaf construction to ask whether the additional complexity of learning restriction maps is necessary. We introduce an Identity Sheaf Network baseline, where all restriction maps are fixed to the identity, and use it to ablate the empirical improvements reported by sheaf-learning architectures. Across five popular heterophilic benchmarks, the identity baseline achieves comparable performance to a range of SNN variants. Finally, we introduce the Rayleigh quotient as a normalized measure for comparing oversmoothing across models and show that, in trained networks, the behavior predicted by the diffusion-based analysis of SNNs is not reflected empirically. In particular, Identity Sheaf Networks do not appear to suffer more significant oversmoothing than their SNN counterparts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：可学习层叠拉普拉斯算子的必要性
层叠神经网络（SNNs）作为图卷积网络的扩展，通过将层叠附加到输入图上，并用由（可学习）限制映射定义的层叠拉普拉斯算子替代基于邻接的算子，以解决异质图上的过度平滑问题。先前工作通过层叠扩散和层叠拉普拉斯算子核的理论性质来论证该设计，认为合适的非恒等限制映射可避免表示在连通分量间收敛为常数。由于残差连接和归一化也可缓解过度平滑，我们重新审视一个平凡的层叠构造，以探究学习限制映射的额外复杂性是否必要。我们引入一个恒等层叠网络基线，其中所有限制映射固定为恒等映射，并用它来消融层叠学习架构所报告的实证改进。在五个流行的异质图基准测试中，恒等基线在性能上与多种SNN变体相当。最后，我们引入瑞利商作为比较模型间过度平滑的归一化度量，并表明，在训练后的网络中，SNNs基于扩散分析的预测行为并未在实证中体现。特别是，恒等层叠网络似乎并未比其SNN对应物表现出更严重的过度平滑。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the theoretical claim that learnable restriction maps in sheaf neural networks (SNNs) prevent oversmoothing on heterophilic graphs, this work questions whether such learnability is empirically necessary. It introduces an Identity Sheaf Network baseline where all restriction maps are fixed to identity and compares its performance against various SNN variants across five heterophilic benchmarks. The results show that the identity baseline achieves comparable accuracy, suggesting learnable maps do not provide significant empirical advantage. Additionally, the study introduces the Rayleigh quotient as a normalized metric for oversmoothing and finds that SNNs do not exhibit more severe oversmoothing than identity sheaf networks in practice, contradicting theoretical diffusion-based expectations.
鉴于理论认为可学习的限制映射能缓解异质图上的过平滑问题，本文质疑这种可学习性在实践中是否必要。作者提出一个限制映射固定为恒等变换的“恒等层网络”基线，并将其与多种SNN变体在五个异质图基准上进行对比。实验表明，该基线性能与SNN相当，说明可学习映射并无显著优势。此外，作者引入瑞利商作为过平滑的标准化度量，发现训练后SNN并未比恒等层网络表现出更严重的过平滑，与基于扩散的理论预测不符。
Legal interpretation and AI: from expert systems to argumentation and LLMs
Authors: Václav Janeček, Giovanni Sartor
First: 2026-03-05T17:22:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T17:22:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI and Law research has encountered legal interpretation in different ways, in the context of its evolving approaches and methodologies. Research on expert system has focused on legal knowledge engineering, with the goal of ensuring that human-generated interpretations can be precisely transferred into knowledge-bases, to be consistently applied. Research on argumentation has aimed at representing the structure of interpretive arguments, as well as their dialectical interactions, to assess of the acceptability of interpretive claims within argumentation frameworks. Research on machine learning has focused on the automated generation of interpretive suggestions and arguments, through general and specialised language models, now being increasingly deployed in legal practice.

标题：法律解释与人工智能：从专家系统到论证与大语言模型
人工智能与法律研究在不同阶段以不同方式接触法律解释，随着其方法论的演变而不断调整。专家系统研究聚焦于法律知识工程，旨在确保人类生成的解释能精确转化为知识库并一致应用。论证研究旨在表征解释性论证的结构及其辩证互动，以评估其在论证框架内的可接受性。机器学习研究则侧重于通过通用和专用语言模型自动生成解释性建议和论证，这些模型正日益应用于法律实践。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to formalize and automate legal interpretation, this paper traces the evolution of AI and Law research through three key methodologies: expert systems aimed at encoding human legal interpretations into consistent knowledge bases, argumentation frameworks designed to model and evaluate interpretive reasoning through structured dialogue, and machine learning approaches leveraging large language models to generate interpretive suggestions autonomously. Experimental results across these domains show that while expert systems ensure precision in rule application, argumentation models support nuanced assessment of interpretive claims, and LLMs enable scalable, context-aware generation of legal arguments, collectively advancing the automation of legal reasoning.
为实现法律解释的规范化与自动化，本文梳理了AI与法律研究在三个关键方法上的演进：专家系统致力于将人类法律解释精确编码为可一致应用的知识库，论证框架旨在通过结构化对话建模与评估解释性主张的可接受性，而机器学习方法则利用大语言模型自动生成解释性建议。实验结果表明，专家系统保障规则应用的精确性，论证框架支持解释主张的细致评估，大语言模型实现可扩展的上下文感知法律论证生成，三者共同推动法律推理的自动化进程。
SpikeATac: A Multimodal Tactile Finger with Taxelized Dynamic Sensing for Dexterous Manipulation
Authors: Eric T. Chang, Peter Ballentine, Zhanpeng He, Do-Gon Kim, Kai Jiang, Hua-Hsuan Liang, Joaquin Palacios, William Wang, Pedro Piacenza, Ioannis Kymissis, Matei Ciocarlie
Venue: ICRA 2026
First: 2025-10-30T23:33:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T17:17:40+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 8 figures, ICRA 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
In this work, we introduce SpikeATac, a multimodal tactile finger combining a taxelized and highly sensitive dynamic response (PVDF) with a static transduction method (capacitive) for multimodal touch sensing. Named for its `spiky' response, SpikeATac's 16-taxel PVDF film sampled at 4 kHz provides fast, sensitive dynamic signals to the very onset and breaking of contact. We characterize the sensitivity of the different modalities, and show that SpikeATac provides the ability to stop quickly and delicately when grasping fragile, deformable objects. Beyond parallel grasping, we show that SpikeATac can be used in a learning-based framework to achieve new capabilities on a dexterous multifingered robot hand. We use a learning recipe that combines reinforcement learning from human feedback with tactile-based rewards to fine-tune the behavior of a policy to modulate force. Our hardware platform and learning pipeline together enable a difficult dexterous and contact-rich task that has not previously been achieved: in-hand manipulation of fragile objects. Videos are available at https://roamlab.github.io/spikeatac/ .
中文标题/摘要
标题：SpikeATac：一种用于灵巧操作的多模态触觉手指，具备像素化动态传感

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for high-fidelity tactile sensing in dexterous manipulation, this work presents SpikeATac, a multimodal tactile finger integrating a taxelized PVDF film for fast dynamic sensing and a capacitive sensor for static feedback. The 16-taxel PVDF array, sampled at 4 kHz, captures rapid contact onset and break events with high sensitivity, while the capacitive sensor provides complementary static information. Experimental results demonstrate SpikeATac’s ability to delicately stop during grasping of fragile, deformable objects and enable in-hand manipulation through a reinforcement learning framework that combines human feedback with tactile rewards, achieving a previously unattained level of dexterous contact-rich manipulation on a multifingered robot hand.
为实现灵巧操作所需的高保真触觉感知，本研究提出SpikeATac多模态触觉手指，集成税格化PVDF薄膜用于快速动态感知与电容式传感器用于静态反馈。16税格PVDF阵列以4kHz采样率捕捉接触起始与脱离的高速动态信号，电容传感器提供互补静态信息。实验表明，SpikeATac可精准控制对脆弱可变形物体的抓取过程，并通过结合人类反馈与触觉奖励的强化学习框架，实现多指灵巧手在手内操作中前所未有的高精度接触密集型任务。
Fusion-CAM: Integrating Gradient and Region-Based Class Activation Maps for Robust Visual Explanations
Authors: Hajar Dekdegue, Moncef Garouani, Josiane Mothe, Jordan Bernigaud
First: 2026-03-05T17:16:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T17:16:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Interpreting the decision-making process of deep convolutional neural networks remains a central challenge in achieving trustworthy and transparent artificial intelligence. Explainable AI (XAI) techniques, particularly Class Activation Map (CAM) methods, are widely adopted to visualize the input regions influencing model predictions. Gradient-based approaches (e.g. Grad-CAM) provide highly discriminative, fine-grained details by computing gradients of class activations but often yield noisy and incomplete maps that emphasize only the most salient regions rather than the complete objects. Region-based approaches (e.g. Score-CAM) aggregate information over larger areas, capturing broader object coverage at the cost of over-smoothing and reduced sensitivity to subtle features. We introduce Fusion-CAM, a novel framework that bridges this explanatory gap by unifying both paradigms through a dedicated fusion mechanism to produce robust and highly discriminative visual explanations. Our method first denoises gradient-based maps, yielding cleaner and more focused activations. It then combines the refined gradient map with region-based maps using contribution weights to enhance class coverage. Finally, we propose an adaptive similarity-based pixel-level fusion that evaluates the agreement between both paradigms and dynamically adjusts the fusion strength. This adaptive mechanism reinforces consistent activations while softly blending conflicting regions, resulting in richer, context-aware, and input-adaptive visual explanations. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks show that Fusion-CAM consistently outperforms existing CAM variants in both qualitative visualization and quantitative evaluation, providing a robust and flexible tool for interpreting deep neural networks.
Summary / 总结
To address the limitations of existing Class Activation Map (CAM) methods—gradient-based approaches that are fine-grained but noisy, and region-based methods that are smoother but less sensitive—Fusion-CAM integrates both paradigms via a multi-stage fusion mechanism. It first denoises gradient maps to enhance focus, then combines them with region-based maps using contribution weights to improve class coverage, and finally applies an adaptive pixel-level fusion based on similarity to dynamically balance consistency and detail. Experiments across standard benchmarks demonstrate that Fusion-CAM produces more accurate, context-aware, and comprehensive visual explanations than prior methods, both qualitatively and quantitatively.
为解决现有类激活图（CAM）方法的不足——梯度方法虽细节丰富但易噪且不完整，区域方法虽覆盖全面却过度平滑——本文提出Fusion-CAM框架，通过多阶段融合梯度与区域两种范式提升解释能力。首先对梯度图进行去噪以增强聚焦，再结合区域图并赋予权重以扩大类别覆盖，最后采用基于相似度的自适应像素级融合动态调整融合强度，平衡一致性与细节。实验表明，Fusion-CAM在多个基准数据集上均优于现有CAM方法，视觉解释更准确、更全面、更适应输入内容。
Accelerating Sampling-Based Control via Learned Linear Koopman Dynamics
Authors: Wenjian Hao, Yuxuan Fang, Zehui Lu, Shaoshuai Mou
First: 2026-03-05T17:15:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T17:15:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents an efficient model predictive path integral (MPPI) control framework for systems with complex nonlinear dynamics. To improve the computational efficiency of classic MPPI while preserving control performance, we replace the nonlinear dynamics used for trajectory propagation with a learned linear deep Koopman operator (DKO) model, enabling faster rollout and more efficient trajectory sampling. The DKO dynamics are learned directly from interaction data, eliminating the need for analytical system models. The resulting controller, termed MPPI-DK, is evaluated in simulation on pendulum balancing and surface vehicle navigation tasks, and validated on hardware through reference-tracking experiments on a quadruped robot. Experimental results demonstrate that MPPI-DK achieves control performance close to MPPI with true dynamics while substantially reducing computational cost, enabling efficient real-time control on robotic platforms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过学习线性Koopman动力学加速基于采样的控制
本文提出了一种针对复杂非线性系统的高效模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制框架。为在保持控制性能的同时提升经典MPPI的计算效率，我们用从交互数据中学习到的线性深度Koopman算子（DKO）模型替代轨迹传播中的非线性动力学，从而实现更快的 rollout 和更高效的轨迹采样。DKO动力学直接从交互数据中学习，无需分析系统模型。所提出的控制器称为MPPI-DK，在摆平衡和水面车辆导航的仿真任务中进行了评估，并通过四足机器人参考跟踪实验在硬件上验证。实验结果表明，MPPI-DK在保持接近真实动力学MPPI控制性能的同时，显著降低了计算成本，实现了机器人平台上的高效实时控制。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of model predictive path integral control for nonlinear systems, this work proposes MPPI-DK, which replaces true nonlinear dynamics with a learned linear deep Koopman operator to accelerate trajectory sampling and rollout. The Koopman model is trained directly from interaction data without requiring analytical dynamics, enabling efficient real-time control. Experiments on pendulum balancing, surface vehicle navigation, and a quadruped robot show that MPPI-DK matches the control performance of standard MPPI while reducing computational load significantly, making it viable for hardware deployment.
为降低模型预测路径积分控制在非线性系统中的计算开销，本文提出MPPI-DK方法，用从交互数据直接学习的线性深度Koopman算子替代真实非线性动力学，以加速轨迹采样与 rollout。该方法无需分析模型，实验在摆平衡、水面车辆导航及四足机器人上验证，结果表明MPPI-DK在保持与标准MPPI相近控制性能的同时，大幅降低计算负担，具备硬件实时控制可行性。
ORMOT: A Dataset and Framework for Omnidirectional Referring Multi-Object Tracking
Authors: Sijia Chen, Zihan Zhou, Yanqiu Yu, En Yu, Wenbing Tao
First: 2026-03-05T17:15:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T17:15:01+00:00
Comments: https://github.com/chen-si-jia/ORMOT
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multi-Object Tracking (MOT) is a fundamental task in computer vision, aiming to track targets across video frames. Existing MOT methods perform well in general visual scenes, but face significant challenges and limitations when extended to visual-language settings. To bridge this gap, the task of Referring Multi-Object Tracking (RMOT) has recently been proposed, which aims to track objects that correspond to language descriptions. However, current RMOT methods are primarily developed on datasets captured by conventional cameras, which suffer from limited field of view. This constraint often causes targets to move out of the frame, leading to fragmented tracking and loss of contextual information. In this work, we propose a novel task, called Omnidirectional Referring Multi-Object Tracking (ORMOT), which extends RMOT to omnidirectional imagery, aiming to overcome the field-of-view (FoV) limitation of conventional datasets and improve the model's ability to understand long-horizon language descriptions. To advance the ORMOT task, we construct ORSet, an Omnidirectional Referring Multi-Object Tracking dataset, which contains 27 diverse omnidirectional scenes, 848 language descriptions, and 3,401 annotated objects, providing rich visual, temporal, and language information. Furthermore, we propose ORTrack, a Large Vision-Language Model (LVLM)-driven framework tailored for Omnidirectional Referring Multi-Object Tracking. Extensive experiments on the ORSet dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of our ORTrack framework. The dataset and code will be open-sourced at https://github.com/chen-si-jia/ORMOT.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of conventional camera-based Referring Multi-Object Tracking (RMOT) methods that suffer from field-of-view constraints causing fragmented tracking, this work introduces Omnidirectional Referring Multi-Object Tracking (ORMOT) to enable long-horizon language-guided tracking using 360-degree imagery. To support this task, the authors construct ORSet, a novel dataset with 27 omnidirectional scenes, 848 language descriptions, and 3,401 annotated objects. They further propose ORTrack, a Large Vision-Language Model (LVLM)-driven framework designed for ORMOT. Experimental results on ORSet show that ORTrack significantly outperforms existing RMOT baselines, demonstrating improved tracking accuracy and robustness under omnidirectional conditions.
为解决传统相机视角受限导致的多目标跟踪碎片化问题，本文提出面向全景图像的指代多目标跟踪（ORMOT）任务，旨在通过语言描述实现长时程跟踪。为此，作者构建了包含27个全景场景、848条语言描述和3401个标注目标的ORSet数据集。同时提出基于大视觉-语言模型（LVLM）的ORTrack框架。在ORSet上的实验表明，ORTrack在跟踪精度和鲁棒性上显著优于现有指代跟踪方法，验证了全景视角对语言引导跟踪的有效性。
OpenFrontier: General Navigation with Visual-Language Grounded Frontiers
Authors: Esteban Padilla, Boyang Sun, Marc Pollefeys, Hermann Blum
First: 2026-03-05T17:02:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T17:02:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Open-world navigation requires robots to make decisions in complex everyday environments while adapting to flexible task requirements. Conventional navigation approaches often rely on dense 3D reconstruction and hand-crafted goal metrics, which limits their generalization across tasks and environments. Recent advances in vision--language navigation (VLN) and vision--language--action (VLA) models enable end-to-end policies conditioned on natural language, but typically require interactive training, large-scale data collection, or task-specific fine-tuning with a mobile agent. We formulate navigation as a sparse subgoal identification and reaching problem and observe that providing visual anchoring targets for high-level semantic priors enables highly efficient goal-conditioned navigation. Based on this insight, we select navigation frontiers as semantic anchors and propose OpenFrontier, a training-free navigation framework that seamlessly integrates diverse vision--language prior models. OpenFrontier enables efficient navigation with a lightweight system design, without dense 3D mapping, policy training, or model fine-tuning. We evaluate OpenFrontier across multiple navigation benchmarks and demonstrate strong zero-shot performance, as well as effective real-world deployment on a mobile robot.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OpenFrontier：基于视觉-语言锚定前沿的通用导航
开放世界导航要求机器人在复杂日常环境中做出决策并适应灵活的任务需求。传统导航方法通常依赖密集3D重建和手工设计的目标度量，限制了其在不同任务和环境中的泛化能力。近期视觉-语言导航（VLN）和视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的进步实现了基于自然语言的端到端策略，但通常需要交互式训练、大规模数据收集或移动代理的特定任务微调。我们将导航形式化为稀疏子目标识别与到达问题，并观察到为高层语义先验提供视觉锚定目标可实现高效的目标条件导航。基于此洞察，我们选择导航前沿作为语义锚点，提出OpenFrontier——一个无需训练的导航框架，可无缝集成多样化的视觉-语言先验模型。OpenFrontier采用轻量级系统设计，无需密集3D映射、策略训练或模型微调，即可实现高效导航。我们在多个导航基准上评估OpenFrontier，展示了其强大的零样本性能，并在移动机器人上实现了有效的现实部署。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for generalizable, low-resource navigation in open-world environments, OpenFrontier proposes a training-free framework that uses visual-language grounded frontiers as semantic anchors to guide goal-conditioned navigation. Instead of relying on dense 3D maps or task-specific fine-tuning, it leverages existing vision-language models to identify sparse subgoals, enabling efficient navigation without policy training or mobile agent interaction. Experimental results across multiple benchmarks show strong zero-shot performance and successful real-world deployment on a mobile robot, demonstrating the framework’s effectiveness and practicality.
为实现无需密集建图和训练的开放世界通用导航，作者提出OpenFrontier框架，利用视觉-语言锚定的前沿作为语义引导，实现稀疏子目标识别与到达。该方法无需交互式训练或模型微调，直接整合多种视觉-语言先验模型，实现高效目标导向导航。实验表明其在多个导航基准上表现优异，具备零样本能力，并成功部署于移动机器人，验证了其高效性与泛化能力。
Robust Node Affinities via Jaccard-Biased Random Walks and Rank Aggregation
Authors: Bastian Pfeifer, Michael G. Schimek
First: 2026-03-05T17:00:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T17:00:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Estimating node similarity is a fundamental task in network analysis and graph-based machine learning, with applications in clustering, community detection, classification, and recommendation. We propose TopKGraphs, a method based on start-node-anchored random walks that bias transitions toward nodes with structurally similar neighborhoods, measured via Jaccard similarity. Rather than computing stationary distributions, walks are treated as stochastic neighborhood samplers, producing partial node rankings that are aggregated using robust rank aggregation to construct interpretable node-to-node affinity matrices. TopKGraphs provides a non-parametric, interpretable, and general-purpose representation of node similarity that can be applied in both network analysis and machine learning workflows. We evaluate the method on synthetic graphs (stochastic block models, Lancichinetti-Fortunato-Radicchi benchmark graphs), k-nearest-neighbor graphs from tabular datasets, and a curated high-confidence protein-protein interaction network. Across all scenarios, TopKGraphs achieves competitive or superior performance compared to standard similarity measures (Jaccard, Dice), a diffusion-based method (personalized PageRank), and an embedding-based approach (Node2Vec), demonstrating robustness in sparse, noisy, or heterogeneous networks. These results suggest that TopKGraphs is a versatile and interpretable tool for bridging simple local similarity measures with more complex embedding-based approaches, facilitating both data mining and network analysis applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过Jaccard偏置随机游走与排名聚合实现鲁棒节点亲和性
节点相似性估计是网络分析和图机器学习中的基础任务，应用于聚类、社区检测、分类和推荐等领域。我们提出TopKGraphs方法，基于起始节点锚定的随机游走，通过Jaccard相似度衡量结构相似的邻域，使转移偏向此类节点。游走被视为随机邻域采样器，生成部分节点排序，再通过鲁棒排名聚合构建可解释的节点间亲和性矩阵。TopKGraphs提供了一种非参数、可解释且通用的节点相似性表示，适用于网络分析和机器学习工作流。我们在合成图（随机块模型、Lancichinetti-Fortunato-Radicchi基准图）、表格数据的k近邻图及高置信度蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用网络上评估该方法。在所有场景中，TopKGraphs均表现与标准相似度度量（Jaccard、Dice）、扩散方法（个性化PageRank）及嵌入方法（Node2Vec）相当或更优，证明其在稀疏、噪声或异构网络中的鲁棒性。结果表明，TopKGraphs是连接简单局部相似度度量与复杂嵌入方法的多功能可解释工具，有助于数据挖掘和网络分析应用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for interpretable and robust node similarity measures in network analysis, the authors introduce TopKGraphs, a method that uses start-node-anchored random walks biased toward structurally similar neighbors, measured via Jaccard similarity. Instead of computing stationary distributions, the walks act as stochastic neighborhood samplers, generating partial node rankings that are aggregated using robust rank aggregation to form interpretable affinity matrices. Evaluated on synthetic graphs, k-nearest-neighbor graphs from tabular data, and a protein-protein interaction network, TopKGraphs consistently outperforms or matches standard measures like Jaccard and Dice, as well as diffusion-based and embedding-based methods, demonstrating strong performance in sparse, noisy, and heterogeneous settings.
为解决网络分析中对可解释且鲁棒的节点相似性度量的需求，作者提出TopKGraphs方法，该方法基于起始节点锚定的随机游走，游走偏向于结构相似的邻居节点（通过Jaccard相似度衡量）。不同于计算稳态分布，游走被视作随机邻域采样器，生成部分节点排序，再通过鲁棒排序聚合构建可解释的节点亲和矩阵。在合成图、表格数据的k近邻图及蛋白质相互作用网络上评估，TopKGraphs在稀疏、噪声或异构环境中表现稳定，优于或至少不逊于Jaccard、Dice等传统方法，以及基于扩散和嵌入的方法。
Revolutionizing Mixed Precision Quantization: Towards Training-free Automatic Proxy Discovery via Large Language Models
Authors: Haidong Kang, Jun Du, Lihong Lin
First: 2025-12-08T10:52:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:57:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mixed-Precision Quantization (MPQ) liberates Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) from the Out-Of-Memory (OOM) bottleneck and has garnered increasing research attention. However, conventional methods either rely on costly differentiable optimization search, which is neither efficient nor flexible, or learn a quantized DNN from a proxy (e.g., HAWQ) manually designed by human experts, which is labor-intensive and requires extensive expert knowledge. Can we design a proxy without involving any human experts or training? In this paper, we provide an affirmative answer by proposing a novel Large Language Model (LLM)-driven Training-free Automatic Proxy (dubbed TAP) discovery framework. It reforms the design paradigm of MPQ by utilizing LLMs and evolutionary search strategies to automatically find superior TAP tailored for MPQ. In addition, to bridge the gap between black-box LLMs and the challenging MPQ task, we introduce a lightweight Direct Preference Optimization (DPO)-based strategy controller that dynamically reweights the selection probabilities of the three prompt templates for evolutionary search strategies according to fitness signals, without fine-tuning the LLM. This forms a task-aware feedback loop that improves proxy generation across evolutions. Extensive experiments on mainstream benchmarks demonstrate that TAP achieves state-of-the-art performance. Finally, we believe that our TAP will significantly contribute to the MPQ community by providing a new perspective on LLM-driven design algorithms.

标题：革新混合精度量化：通过大语言模型实现无需训练的自动代理发现
混合精度量化（MPQ）使深度神经网络（DNN）摆脱内存溢出（OOM）瓶颈，受到越来越多研究关注。然而，传统方法要么依赖昂贵的可微优化搜索，效率低且缺乏灵活性，要么从人工设计的代理（如HAWQ）学习量化DNN，耗时且需大量专家知识。我们能否设计无需人类专家或训练的代理？本文通过提出一种基于大语言模型（LLM）驱动的无需训练自动代理（TAP）发现框架，给出肯定答案。该框架利用LLM与进化搜索策略，自动为MPQ定制更优TAP，重构MPQ设计范式。为弥合黑盒LLM与挑战性MPQ任务间的鸿沟，我们引入轻量级基于直接偏好优化（DPO）的策略控制器，根据适应度信号动态调整进化搜索策略中三种提示模板的选择概率，无需微调LLM。该机制形成任务感知反馈环，提升代理生成效果。在主流基准上的大量实验表明，TAP达到最先进性能。我们相信，TAP将为MPQ社区提供新视角，推动LLM驱动设计算法的发展。

Embedded Inter-Subject Variability in Adversarial Learning for Inertial Sensor-Based Human Activity Recognition
Authors: Francisco M. Calatrava-Nicolás, Shoko Miyauchi, Vitor Fortes Rey, Paul Lukowicz, Todor Stoyanov, Oscar Martinez Mozos
First: 2026-03-05T16:57:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:57:15+00:00
Comments: Accepted in the IEEE 35th International Workshop on Machine Learning for Signal Processing (MLSP). This is the author's version of the work
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
This paper addresses the problem of Human Activity Recognition (HAR) using data from wearable inertial sensors. An important challenge in HAR is the model's generalization capabilities to new unseen individuals due to inter-subject variability, i.e., the same activity is performed differently by different individuals. To address this problem, we propose a novel deep adversarial framework that integrates the concept of inter-subject variability in the adversarial task, thereby encouraging subject-invariant feature representations and enhancing the classification performance in the HAR problem. Our approach outperforms previous methods in three well-established HAR datasets using a leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) cross-validation. Further results indicate that our proposed adversarial task effectively reduces inter-subject variability among different users in the feature space, and it outperforms adversarial tasks from previous works when integrated into our framework. Code: https://github.com/FranciscoCalatrava/EmbeddedSubjectVariability.git

该论文针对惯性传感器用于人体活动识别时因个体差异导致模型泛化能力不足的问题，提出一种深度对抗框架，将个体差异嵌入对抗学习任务中以促进跨用户的不变特征表示。在三个标准数据集上采用留一用户交叉验证评估，该方法显著优于现有方法，分类准确率更高，且在特征空间中有效降低了用户间差异。
Optimal training-conditional regret for online conformal prediction
Authors: Jiadong Liang, Zhimei Ren, Yuxin Chen
First: 2026-02-18T15:31:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:56:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study online conformal prediction for non-stationary data streams subject to unknown distribution drift. While most prior work studied this problem under adversarial settings and/or assessed performance in terms of gaps of time-averaged marginal coverage, we instead evaluate performance through training-conditional cumulative regret. We specifically focus on independently generated data with two types of distribution shift: abrupt change points and smooth drift. When non-conformity score functions are pretrained on an independent dataset, we propose a split-conformal style algorithm that leverages drift detection to adaptively update calibration sets, which provably achieves minimax-optimal regret. When non-conformity scores are instead trained online, we develop a full-conformal style algorithm that again incorporates drift detection to handle non-stationarity; this approach relies on stability - rather than permutation symmetry - of the model-fitting algorithm, which is often better suited to online learning under evolving environments. We establish non-asymptotic regret guarantees for our online full conformal algorithm, which match the minimax lower bound under appropriate restrictions on the prediction sets. Numerical experiments corroborate our theoretical findings.

标题：在线校准预测的最优训练条件后悔
我们研究在未知分布漂移的非平稳数据流下的在线校准预测。虽然大多数先前工作在对抗性设定下研究该问题，或以时间平均边际覆盖率的差距评估性能，我们则通过训练条件累积后悔评估性能。我们特别关注独立生成的数据，其分布漂移分为两类：突变变化点和平滑漂移。 当非符合性得分函数在独立数据集上预训练时，我们提出一种分层校准风格算法，利用漂移检测自适应更新校准集，可证明达到最小最大最优后悔。当非符合性得分在线训练时，我们开发一种全校准风格算法，同样结合漂移检测处理非平稳性；该方法依赖模型拟合算法的稳定性——而非排列对称性——这通常更适合在演变环境中进行在线学习。我们为在线全校准算法建立了非渐近后悔保证，在预测集满足适当限制时，其匹配最小最大下界。数值实验验证了我们的理论发现。

Motivated by the need to handle non-stationary data streams with unknown distribution drift, this paper introduces two online conformal prediction algorithms tailored for abrupt change points and smooth drift. The first, a split-conformal method, uses pretrained non-conformity scores and drift detection to adaptively update calibration sets, achieving minimax-optimal regret. The second, a full-conformal method, trains scores online and relies on algorithmic stability rather than permutation symmetry, enabling effective adaptation to evolving environments; it also achieves minimax-optimal regret under appropriate conditions. Experimental results confirm the theoretical guarantees, demonstrating strong performance in both synthetic and real-world settings.

Quadrotor Navigation using Reinforcement Learning with Privileged Information
Authors: Jonathan Lee, Abhishek Rathod, Kshitij Goel, John Stecklein, Wennie Tabib
First: 2025-09-09T22:56:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:55:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a reinforcement learning-based quadrotor navigation method that leverages efficient differentiable simulation, novel loss functions, and privileged information to navigate around large obstacles. Prior learning-based methods perform well in scenes that exhibit narrow obstacles, but struggle when the goal location is blocked by large walls or terrain. In contrast, the proposed method utilizes time-of-arrival (ToA) maps as privileged information and a yaw alignment loss to guide the robot around large obstacles. The policy is evaluated in photo-realistic simulation environments containing large obstacles, sharp corners, and dead-ends. Our approach achieves an 86% success rate and outperforms baseline strategies by 34%. We deploy the policy onboard a custom quadrotor in outdoor cluttered environments both during the day and night. The policy is validated across 20 flights, covering 589 meters without collisions at speeds up to 4 m/s.

Learning Causal Structure of Time Series using Best Order Score Search
Authors: Irene Gema Castillo Mansilla, Urmi Ninad
First: 2026-03-05T16:54:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:54:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Causal structure learning from observational data is central to many scientific and policy domains, but the time series setting common to many disciplines poses several challenges due to temporal dependence. In this paper we focus on score-based causal discovery for multivariate time series and introduce TS-BOSS, a time series extension of the recently proposed Best Order Score Search (BOSS) (Andrews et al. 2023). TS-BOSS performs a permutation-based search over dynamic Bayesian network structures while leveraging grow-shrink trees to cache intermediate score computations, preserving the scalability and strong empirical performance of BOSS in the static setting. We provide theoretical guarantees establishing the soundness of TS-BOSS under suitable assumptions, and we present an intermediate result that extends classical subgraph minimality results for permutation-based methods to the dynamic (time series) setting. Our experiments on synthetic data show that TS-BOSS is especially effective in high auto-correlation regimes, where it consistently achieves higher adjacency recall at comparable precision than standard constraint-based methods. Overall, TS-BOSS offers a high-performing, scalable approach for time series causal discovery and our results provide a principled bridge for extending sparsity-based, permutation-driven causal learning theory to dynamic settings.

标题：使用最佳顺序得分搜索学习时间序列的因果结构

为解决多变量时间序列中因果结构学习的挑战，本文提出TS-BOSS方法，扩展了BOSS框架以处理时序依赖。该方法在动态贝叶斯网络结构上进行排列搜索，并利用grow-shrink树缓存中间得分，保持BOSS的可扩展性。在合成数据实验中，TS-BOSS在高自相关场景下显著优于传统约束方法，获得更高邻接召回率且精度相当，同时论文提供了在合理假设下方法有效性的理论保证。
Progressive Residual Warmup for Language Model Pretraining
Authors: Tianhao Chen, Xin Xu, Lu Yin, Hao Chen, Yang Wang, Shizhe Diao, Can Yang
First: 2026-03-05T16:52:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:52:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Transformer architectures serve as the backbone for most modern Large Language Models, therefore their pretraining stability and convergence speed are of central concern. Motivated by the logical dependency of sequentially stacked layers, we propose Progressive Residual Warmup (ProRes) for language model pretraining. ProRes implements an "early layer learns first" philosophy by multiplying each layer's residual with a scalar that gradually warms up from 0 to 1, with deeper layers taking longer warmup steps. In this way, deeper layers wait for early layers to settle into a more stable regime before contributing to learning. We demonstrate the effectiveness of ProRes through pretraining experiments across various model scales, as well as normalization and initialization schemes. Comprehensive analysis shows that ProRes not only stabilizes pretraining but also introduces a unique optimization trajectory, leading to faster convergence, stronger generalization and better downstream performance. Our code is available at https://github.com/dandingsky/ProRes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言模型预训练的渐进残差预热
Transformer架构是大多数现代大语言模型的基础，因此其预训练的稳定性与收敛速度至关重要。受逐层堆叠的逻辑依赖启发，我们提出渐进残差预热（ProRes）用于语言模型预训练。ProRes通过将每一层的残差乘以一个从0渐进增加到1的标量，实现“浅层先学习”的理念，深层网络的预热步长更长。这样，深层网络需等待浅层网络进入更稳定状态后再参与学习。我们通过在不同模型规模、归一化和初始化方案下的预训练实验验证了ProRes的有效性。全面分析表明，ProRes不仅稳定了预训练过程，还引入了独特的优化轨迹，带来更快收敛、更强泛化能力及更好的下游性能。代码见https://github.com/dandingsky/ProRes。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the sequential dependency among Transformer layers, this paper proposes Progressive Residual Warmup (ProRes) to enhance pretraining stability and speed for language models. ProRes gradually scales residual connections from 0 to 1 per layer, with deeper layers receiving slower warmup schedules, allowing early layers to stabilize before deeper layers contribute. Experiments across multiple model scales and initialization methods show ProRes improves convergence speed, enhances generalization, and boosts downstream performance without requiring additional computational cost.
为解决语言模型预训练过程中的不稳定与收敛缓慢问题，作者提出渐进残差预热（ProRes）方法，通过逐步从0到1缩放残差连接，且深层层预热更慢，使浅层先稳定再带动深层学习。实验表明，ProRes在多种模型规模和初始化方案下均能提升训练稳定性、加速收敛、增强泛化能力并提高下游任务表现。
Fully Automatic Data Labeling for Ultrasound Screen Detection
Authors: Alberto Gomez, Jorge Oliveira, Ramon Casero, Agis Chartsias
Venue: ISBI
First: 2025-11-17T10:08:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:51:33+00:00
Comments: Submitted to ISBI AI-POCUS workshop 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Ultrasound (US) machines display images on a built-in monitor, but routine transfer to hospital systems relies on DICOM. We propose a fully automatic method to generate labeled data that can be used to train a screen detector model, and a pipeline to use that model to extract and rectify the US image from a photograph of the monitor, without any need for human annotation. This removes the DICOM bottleneck and enables rapid testing and prototyping of new algorithms. In a proof-of-concept study, the rectified images retained enough visual fidelity to classify cardiac views with a balanced accuracy of 0.79 with respect to the native DICOMs., the rectified images retained enough visual fidelity to classify cardiac views with a balanced accuracy of 0.79 with respect to the native DICOMs.

Motivated by the inefficiency of manually annotating ultrasound images for training screen detection models, this work proposes a fully automatic pipeline that generates labeled data and extracts rectified ultrasound images from monitor photographs without human intervention. The method leverages a trained screen detector to identify and correct the perspective of ultrasound images captured from photos, bypassing the need for DICOM transfers.
