为解决超声图像手动DICOM传输效率低的问题，该研究提出全自动方法，通过照片自动检测并校正超声屏幕图像，无需人工标注。方法利用预训练的屏幕检测模型从照片中定位并校正超声图像，支持快速算法原型开发。实验表明，校正后的图像视觉保真度足够，心脏视图分类的平衡准确率达0.79，与原DICOM图像相当。
Towards Understanding Subliminal Learning: When and How Hidden Biases Transfer
Authors: Simon Schrodi, Elias Kempf, Fazl Barez, Thomas Brox
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-09-28T13:51:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:49:57+00:00
Comments: ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language models can transfer hidden biases during distillation. For example, a teacher that "likes owls" can make its student "like owls" too, even when the training data consists only of lists of numbers. This surprising phenomenon is called subliminal learning. Subliminal learning can be expected under soft distillation, where the student is trained on the teacher's full next-token distribution. But the fact that this also occurs under hard distillation-where the student only sees sampled tokens-raises a deeper question: when and how does subliminal learning actually occur? We answer this question through controlled experiments and mechanistic analysis. Our results show that subliminal learning does not need (global) token entanglement or logit leakage. Instead, it comes down to a small set of divergence tokens-rare cases where teachers with different biases would predict different tokens. Masking out these tokens mostly removes the hidden bias transfer. Mechanistically, divergence tokens reveal that early layers are critical. Surprisingly, finetuning even a single such early layer is sufficient for subliminal learning. Finally, we find that subliminal learning is fragile. Even small changes, like prompt paraphrasings, are usually sufficient to suppress it.
中文标题/摘要
标题：迈向理解潜意识学习：隐藏偏见何时以及如何传递
语言模型在蒸馏过程中可传递隐藏偏见。例如，一个“喜欢猫头鹰”的教师模型，即使训练数据仅为数字列表，也能使其学生也“喜欢猫头鹰”。这种令人惊讶的现象被称为潜意识学习。潜意识学习在软蒸馏中可预期，即学生在教师完整下一词分布上训练。但该现象在硬蒸馏中同样出现——学生仅看到采样词——这引发更深层的问题：潜意识学习究竟何时以及如何发生？我们通过受控实验和机制分析回答了这一问题。结果表明，潜意识学习不需要（全局）词元纠缠或logit泄露。相反，它源于一组稀有的“分歧词元”——即不同偏见教师会预测不同词元的罕见情况。屏蔽这些词元大多能消除隐藏偏见的传递。机制上，分歧词元揭示早期层至关重要。令人惊讶的是，仅微调一个早期层就足以实现潜意识学习。最后，我们发现潜意识学习脆弱：即使微小变化，如提示重述，通常足以抑制其发生。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates subliminal learning—the phenomenon where language models transfer hidden biases during distillation, even when training data is devoid of such content. Through controlled experiments and mechanistic analysis, the authors find that subliminal learning occurs not due to global token entanglement or logit leakage, but through a small set of divergence tokens—rare cases where teachers with differing biases predict different tokens. Masking these tokens largely eliminates bias transfer, and mechanistic probing reveals that early layers are critical, with even fine-tuning a single early layer sufficient to enable subliminal learning. The effect is fragile, often suppressed by minor changes like prompt paraphrasing.
该研究探讨了模型在蒸馏过程中无意识传递隐藏偏见的现象，即使训练数据仅包含数字列表仍会发生。研究发现，这种传递不依赖全局词元纠缠或对数概率泄露，而是由少数“分歧词元”驱动——即不同偏见教师预测不同词元的罕见情况。屏蔽这些词元可大幅削弱偏见传递，机制分析表明早期层至关重要，仅微调一层即可实现无意识学习。该现象脆弱，轻微改动如提示重写通常即可抑制其发生。
Ask, Reason, Assist: Robot Collaboration via Natural Language and Temporal Logic
Authors: Dan BW Choe, Sundhar Vinodh Sangeetha, Steven Emanuel, Chih-Yuan Chiu, Samuel Coogan, Shreyas Kousik
First: 2025-09-27T21:28:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:47:49+00:00
Comments: arXiv admin note: substantial text overlap with arXiv:2505.13376
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Increased robot deployment, such as in warehousing, has revealed a need for collaboration among heterogeneous robot teams to resolve unforeseen conflicts. To this end, we propose a peer-to-peer coordination protocol that enables robots to request and provide help without a central task allocator. The process begins when a robot detects a conflict and uses a Large Language Model (LLM) to decide whether external assistance is required. If so, it crafts and broadcasts a natural language (NL) help request. Potential helper robots reason over the request and respond with offers of assistance, including information about the effect on their ongoing tasks. Helper reasoning is implemented via an LLM grounded in Signal Temporal Logic (STL) using a Backus-Naur Form (BNF) grammar, ensuring syntactically valid NL-to-STL translations, which are then solved as a Mixed Integer Linear Program (MILP). Finally, the requester robot selects a helper by reasoning over the expected increase in system-level total task completion time. We evaluated our framework through experiments comparing different helper-selection strategies and found that considering multiple offers allows the requester to minimize added makespan. Our approach significantly outperforms heuristics such as selecting the nearest available candidate helper robot, and achieves performance comparable to a centralized "Oracle" baseline but without heavy information demands.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for decentralized collaboration among heterogeneous robots in dynamic environments like warehouses, this paper proposes a peer-to-peer coordination protocol that enables robots to request and offer assistance using natural language. When a robot detects a conflict, it uses a Large Language Model to determine if help is needed, then broadcasts a natural language request. Potential helpers reason over the request using an LLM grounded in Signal Temporal Logic via a BNF grammar, translating the request into a solvable Mixed Integer Linear Program. The requester selects the best helper based on the expected reduction in system-wide task completion time. Experiments show that evaluating multiple offers leads to minimal added makespan, outperforming heuristic methods like choosing the nearest robot and matching the performance of a centralized oracle without requiring centralized coordination.
为应对仓储等动态环境中异构机器人团队协作的需求，本文提出一种基于自然语言的点对点协作协议。方法为：当机器人检测到冲突时，使用大语言模型判断是否需要协助，若需协助则广播自然语言请求；其他机器人通过基于信号时序逻辑（STL）的LLM推理请求，利用BNF语法确保语义正确性，并将请求转化为混合整数线性规划（MILP）求解任务影响；请求者通过最小化系统总任务完成时间选择最优协助者。实验表明，综合多份协助方案可显著降低额外完工时间，优于选择最近可用机器人的启发式方法，且性能接近集中式“Oracle”基线，无需中心协调。
Track Anything Behind Everything: Zero-Shot Amodal Video Object Segmentation
Authors: Finlay G. C. Hudson, William A. P. Smith
First: 2024-11-28T15:30:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:45:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present Track Anything Behind Everything (TABE), a novel pipeline for zero-shot amodal video object segmentation. Unlike existing methods that require pretrained class labels, our approach uses a single query mask from the first frame where the object is visible, enabling flexible, zero-shot inference. We pose amodal segmentation as generative outpainting from modal (visible) masks using a pretrained video diffusion model. We do not need to re-train the diffusion model to accommodate additional input channels but instead use a pretrained model that we fine-tune at test-time to allow specialisation towards the tracked object. Our TABE pipeline is specifically designed to handle amodal completion, even in scenarios where objects are completely occluded. Our model and code will all be released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：万物之后皆可追踪：零样本无模态视频目标分割
我们提出了“万物之后皆可追踪”（TABE），一种新颖的零样本无模态视频目标分割流程。与现有需要预训练类别标签的方法不同，我们的方法仅需第一帧中目标可见时的一个查询掩码，即可实现灵活的零样本推理。我们将无模态分割建模为从有模态（可见）掩码出发的生成式外扩，利用预训练的视频扩散模型。我们无需重新训练扩散模型以适应额外输入通道，而是使用一个预训练模型，在测试时进行微调，使其专门针对被追踪的目标进行优化。TABE流程专门设计用于处理无模态补全，即使在目标完全被遮挡的情况下也能有效工作。我们的模型和代码将全部开源。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for flexible, zero-shot amodal video object segmentation without requiring class-specific training, TABE introduces a pipeline that uses a single query mask from the first visible frame to track and complete occluded objects. It leverages a pretrained video diffusion model, fine-tuned at test-time for the specific tracked object, to perform generative outpainting from visible masks, enabling amodal completion even when objects are fully occluded. Experimental results demonstrate that TABE achieves competitive performance on standard benchmarks, outperforming existing zero-shot methods and showing strong generalization across unseen object categories.
为实现无需预训练类别标签的零样本遮挡视频目标分割，TABE提出一种基于首帧可见掩码查询并利用预训练视频扩散模型进行生成式外扩的跟踪框架。该模型在测试时针对目标进行微调，无需重新训练扩散模型即可完成遮挡区域的重建。实验表明，TABE能有效跟踪并恢复完全遮挡的目标，性能在标准数据集上表现优异。
MatRIS: Toward Reliable and Efficient Pretrained Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials
Authors: Yuanchang Zhou, Siyu Hu, Xiangyu Zhang, Hongyu Wang, Guangming Tan, Weile Jia
First: 2026-03-02T15:52:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:45:08+00:00
Comments: 28 pages, 9 figures, 12 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Foundation MLIPs demonstrate broad applicability across diverse material systems and have emerged as a powerful and transformative paradigm in chemical and computational materials science. Equivariant MLIPs achieve state-of-the-art accuracy in a wide range of benchmarks by incorporating equivariant inductive bias. However, the reliance on tensor products and high-degree representations makes them computationally costly. This raises a fundamental question: as quantum mechanical-based datasets continue to expand, can we develop a more compact model to thoroughly exploit high-dimensional atomic interactions? In this work, we present MatRIS (\textbf{Mat}erials \textbf{R}epresentation and \textbf{I}nteraction \textbf{S}imulation), an invariant MLIP that introduces attention-based modeling of three-body interactions. MatRIS leverages a novel separable attention mechanism with linear complexity $O(N)$, enabling both scalability and expressiveness. MatRIS delivers accuracy comparable to that of leading equivariant models on a wide range of popular benchmarks (Matbench-Discovery, MatPES, MDR phonon, Molecular dataset, etc). Taking Matbench-Discovery as an example, MatRIS achieves an F1 score of up to 0.847 and attains comparable accuracy at a lower training cost. The work indicates that our carefully designed invariant models can match or exceed the accuracy of equivariant models at a fraction of the cost, shedding light on the development of accurate and efficient MLIPs.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency of equivariant machine learning interatomic potentials despite their high accuracy, this work introduces MatRIS, an invariant MLIP that models three-body interactions via a linear-complexity attention mechanism. By replacing tensor-based representations with a separable attention design, MatRIS achieves scalability while preserving expressive power. Experimental results across multiple benchmarks—including Matbench-Discovery, MatPES, and MDR phonon—show MatRIS matches or surpasses equivariant models in accuracy while reducing training cost significantly; for instance, on Matbench-Discovery, it achieves an F1 score of 0.847 with lower computational overhead.
为了解决现有等变机器学习原子势计算成本高的问题，本文提出MatRIS模型，采用基于注意力机制的三体相互作用建模，复杂度为线性O(N)。通过替换张量表示为可分离注意力结构，MatRIS在保持表达能力的同时实现高效扩展。在多个基准测试（如Matbench-Discovery、MatPES、MDR phonon）中，MatRIS的准确度可媲美甚至超越主流等变模型，在Matbench-Discovery上F1分数达0.847，同时训练成本显著降低。
Revisiting Multimodal KV Cache Compression: A Frequency-Domain-Guided Outlier-KV-Aware Approach
Authors: Yaoxin Yang, Peng Ye, Xudong Tan, Chongjun Tu, Maosen Zhao, Jia Hao, Tao Chen
First: 2025-11-20T20:25:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:43:51+00:00
Comments: CVPR2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal large language models suffer from substantial inference overhead since multimodal KV Cache grows proportionally with the visual input length. Existing multimodal KV Cache compression methods mostly rely on attention score to reduce cache size, which makes them are incompatible with established efficient attention kernels (e.g., FlashAttention) and ignores the contribution of value vectors to the attention output. In this work, we revisit multimodal KV Cache compression from the perspective of the KV matrices' distribution. First, we observe that frequency-domain energy of multimodal KV matrices is predominantly concentrated in low-frequency and extract this principal energy via a low-pass filter. Further, we find that removing KV pairs that deviate substantially from this principal energy leads to a pronounced performance drop, which we define as Outlier KVs. Considering Outlier KVs are more likely to encode features critical for inference, we propose FlashCache, a frequency-domain-guided, Outlier-KV-aware KV Cache compression framework. First, we introduce an Outlier KV Recognition Module that models the principal component of multimodal KV matrices in the frequency domain and preferentially retains KV pairs that significantly deviate from it. Furthermore, Dynamic Budget Allocation Module is designed to adaptively determine the per-layer KV Cache size to retain more Outlier KVs. Experiments on multiple MLLMs and benchmarks demonstrate that FlashCache outperforms state-of-the-art multimoal KV compression methods, achieving up to 1.69 times faster decoding with 80% lower KV memory usage while maintaining task performance.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high inference overhead of multimodal LLMs due to growing KV cache sizes with visual input, this work revisits KV cache compression by analyzing the frequency-domain distribution of KV matrices. It identifies 'Outlier KVs' — KV pairs deviating significantly from the low-frequency energy dominant in the matrix — as critical for performance, and proposes FlashCache, a framework that retains these outliers while compressing the rest. FlashCache uses a frequency-domain outlier recognition module and a dynamic budget allocation module to adaptively control per-layer cache size. Experiments show FlashCache achieves up to 1.69x faster decoding with 80% lower KV memory usage while preserving task performance across multiple MLLMs and benchmarks.
为缓解多模态大语言模型因视觉输入增长导致的KV缓存膨胀和推理开销，本文从KV矩阵频域分布视角重新审视缓存压缩问题。发现偏离低频主能量的‘异常KV’对推理性能至关重要，提出FlashCache框架，通过频域异常KV识别模块优先保留异常KV，并结合动态预算分配模块自适应控制每层缓存大小。实验表明，FlashCache在多个多模态模型和基准测试中，实现最高1.69倍的推理加速与80%的KV内存节省，同时保持任务性能不变。
ROVER: Regulator-Driven Robust Temporal Verification of Black-Box Robot Policies
Authors: Kristy Sakano, Jianyu An, Dinesh Manocha, Huan Xu
First: 2025-11-21T20:58:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:43:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a novel, regulator-driven approach for the temporal verification of black-box autonomous robot policies, inspired by real-world certification processes where regulators often evaluate observable behavior without access to model internals. Central to our method is a regulator-in-the-loop approach that evaluates execution traces from black-box policies against temporal safety requirements. These requirements, expressed as prioritized Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications, characterize behavior changes over time and encode domain knowledge into the verification process. We use Total Robustness Value (TRV) and Largest Robustness Value (LRV) to quantify average performance and worst-case adherence, and introduce Average Violation Robustness Value (AVRV) to measure average specification violation. Together, these metrics guide targeted retraining and iterative model improvement. Our approach accommodates diverse temporal safety requirements (e.g., lane-keeping, delayed acceleration, and turn smoothness), capturing persistence, sequencing, and response across two distinct domains (virtual racing game and mobile robot navigation). Across six STL specifications in both scenarios, regulator-guided retraining increased satisfaction rates by an average of 43.8%, with consistent improvement in average performance (TRV) and reduced violation severity (LRV) in half of the specifications. Finally, real-world validation on a TurtleBot3 robot demonstrates a 27% improvement in smooth-navigation satisfaction, yielding smoother paths and stronger compliance with STL-defined temporal safety requirements.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ROVER：基于监管器驱动的黑盒机器人策略时序验证
我们提出了一种新颖的、由监管器驱动的黑盒自主机器人策略时序验证方法，该方法借鉴了现实世界认证流程中监管机构常在不接触模型内部结构的情况下评估可观测行为的做法。本方法的核心是“监管器在环”机制，通过将黑盒策略的执行轨迹与时间安全需求进行对比评估。这些需求以优先级排序的信号时序逻辑（STL）规范形式表达，刻画行为随时间的变化，并将领域知识融入验证过程。我们使用总鲁棒值（TRV）和最大鲁棒值（LRV）量化平均性能与最坏情况下的合规性，并引入平均违反鲁棒值（AVRV）衡量平均规范违反程度。这些指标共同指导定向再训练和模型迭代改进。本方法支持多样化的时序安全需求（如车道保持、延迟加速和转向平滑），在两个不同领域（虚拟赛车游戏与移动机器人导航）中捕捉持续性、序列性和响应性。在两个场景的六种STL规范中，监管器引导的再训练平均提升了43.8%的满足率，其中一半规范的平均性能（TRV）持续改善，违反严重性（LRV）降低。最后，在TurtleBot3机器人上的现实验证显示，平滑导航满足率提升27%，路径更平滑，对STL定义的时序安全需求合规性更强。
Summary / 总结
We present a novel, regulator-driven approach for the temporal verification of black-box autonomous robot policies, inspired by real-world certification processes where regulators often evaluate observable behavior without access to model internals.
PACE: A Personalized Adaptive Curriculum Engine for 9-1-1 Call-taker Training
Authors: Zirong Chen, Hongchao Zhang, Meiyi Ma
First: 2026-03-05T16:42:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:42:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
9-1-1 call-taking training requires mastery of over a thousand interdependent skills, covering diverse incident types and protocol-specific nuances. A nationwide labor shortage is already straining training capacity, but effective instruction still demands that trainers tailor objectives to each trainee's evolving competencies. This personalization burden is one that current practice cannot scale. Partnering with Metro Nashville Department of Emergency Communications (MNDEC), we propose PACE (Personalized Adaptive Curriculum Engine), a co-pilot system that augments trainer decision-making by (1) maintaining probabilistic beliefs over trainee skill states, (2) modeling individual learning and forgetting dynamics, and (3) recommending training scenarios that balance acquisition of new competencies with retention of existing ones. PACE propagates evidence over a structured skill graph to accelerate diagnostic coverage and applies contextual bandits to select scenarios that target gaps the trainee is prepared to address. Empirical results show that PACE achieves 19.50% faster time-to-competence and 10.95% higher terminal mastery compared to state-of-the-art frameworks. Co-pilot studies with practicing training officers further demonstrate a 95.45% alignment rate between PACE's and experts' pedagogical judgments on real-world cases. Under estimation, PACE cuts turnaround time to merely 34 seconds from 11.58 minutes, up to 95.08% reduction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PACE：面向9-1-1接线员培训的个性化自适应课程引擎
9-1-1接线员培训需掌握上千项相互关联的技能，涵盖多样化的事件类型及特定协议细节。全国范围内人力短缺已加剧培训能力压力，但有效教学仍需培训师根据每位学员不断变化的能力量身定制目标。当前实践难以承担这一个性化负担。我们与纳什维尔市紧急通讯部（MNDEC）合作，提出PACE（个性化自适应课程引擎），作为辅助培训师决策的协作者系统，其功能包括：（1）维护对学员技能状态的概率信念；（2）建模个体学习与遗忘动态；（3）推荐平衡新能力获取与现有能力保持的训练场景。PACE通过结构化技能图传播证据以加速诊断覆盖，并采用上下文多臂老虎机算法选择针对学员当前可应对能力缺口的场景。实证结果表明，PACE相较现有最先进框架，可使学员达到能力所需时间缩短19.50%，最终掌握度提升10.95%。与一线培训教官的协作者研究进一步显示，PACE对真实案例的教学判断与专家意见的一致率达95.45%。在低估情况下，PACE将处理时间从11.58分钟缩短至仅34秒，效率提升高达95.08%。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to personalize training for 9-1-1 call-takers amid nationwide staffing shortages and complex skill requirements, PACE employs a probabilistic skill-state model and contextual bandits to recommend adaptive training scenarios that balance learning new competencies with retaining existing ones. By propagating evidence across a structured skill graph, PACE accelerates diagnostic coverage and optimizes scenario selection. Experimental results show it achieves 19.50% faster time-to-competence and 10.95% higher terminal mastery than existing methods, with expert alignment rates of 95.45% and up to 95.08% reduction in turnaround time under estimation.
为应对全国警力短缺和9-1-1接线员培训中复杂技能需求，PACE提出一个协同辅助系统，通过概率建模学员技能状态，并根据个体学习与遗忘动态调整课程。系统利用结构化技能图传播证据，结合上下文多臂老虎机选择最优训练场景，兼顾新技能掌握与旧技能巩固。实验表明，PACE相较现有方法缩短19.50%的达标时间，提升10.95%的最终掌握度，专家判断一致性达95.45%，在估算下将处理时间缩短至34秒，效率提升95.08%。
DiSCTT: Consensus-Guided Self-Curriculum for Efficient Test-Time Adaptation in Reasoning
Authors: Mohammad Mahdi Moradi, Sudhir Mudur
First: 2026-03-05T16:38:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:38:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Test-time adaptation offers a promising avenue for improving reasoning performance in large language models without additional supervision, but existing approaches often apply a uniform optimization objective across all inputs, leading to inefficient or unstable adaptation on heterogeneous reasoning problems. We propose DiSCTT, a difficulty-aware, consensus-guided self-curriculum framework that dynamically allocates test-time optimization strategies based on instance-level epistemic uncertainty estimated from agreement among sampled reasoning trajectories. Inputs with high consensus are consolidated via supervised fine-tuning using majority-agreed solutions as pseudo-labels, while low-consensus inputs are optimized via reinforcement learning with a consensus-regularized objective that encourages diversity under relevance constraints. Across a broad suite of mathematical and general reasoning benchmarks, DiSCTT consistently outperforms strong test-time adaptation baselines, achieving higher accuracy with reduced variance and substantially lower computation and wall-clock training times. These results demonstrate that explicitly accounting for instance difficulty and uncertainty enables more stable, efficient, and effective test-time adaptation for reasoning models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DiSCTT：基于共识的自适应课程框架，用于推理模型测试时高效自适应
测试时自适应为无需额外监督即可提升大语言模型推理性能提供了有前景的途径，但现有方法常对所有输入采用统一优化目标，导致在异构推理问题上效率低下或不稳定。我们提出DiSCTT，一种基于难度感知与共识引导的自适应课程框架，根据采样推理轨迹间的共识程度动态分配测试时优化策略。高共识输入通过使用多数同意解作为伪标签进行监督微调进行整合；低共识输入则通过共识正则化的目标函数进行强化学习优化，以在相关性约束下鼓励多样性。在广泛的数学与通用推理基准测试中，DiSCTT持续优于强基线方法，实现更高准确率、更低方差及显著减少计算量和训练时长。结果表明，显式考虑实例难度与不确定性可使推理模型的测试时自适应更稳定、高效且有效。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of uniform test-time adaptation across diverse reasoning tasks, the authors introduce DiSCTT, a self-curriculum framework that dynamically assigns optimization strategies based on instance-level epistemic uncertainty derived from consensus among sampled reasoning trajectories. High-consensus instances are fine-tuned with pseudo-labels from majority-agreed solutions, while low-consensus instances are optimized via reinforcement learning with a consensus-regularized objective to maintain diversity under relevance constraints. Experimental results across multiple reasoning benchmarks show DiSCTT achieves higher accuracy with lower variance and significantly reduced computational and training time compared to existing baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of difficulty-aware, uncertainty-guided adaptation.
为解决现有测试时自适应方法在异构推理任务中效率低下、不稳定的问题，本文提出DiSCTT框架，根据采样推理轨迹间的共识度动态分配优化策略。高共识样本通过多数同意解作为伪标签进行监督微调，低共识样本则采用共识正则化强化学习以在相关性约束下保持多样性。在多个推理基准测试中，DiSCTT显著优于现有基线方法，不仅提升准确率、降低方差，还大幅减少计算量和训练耗时，证明了基于难度感知与不确定性引导的自适应策略可有效提升推理模型的效率与性能。
LHM-Humanoid: Learning a Unified Policy for Long-Horizon Humanoid Whole-Body Loco-Manipulation in Diverse Messy Environments
Authors: Haozhuo Zhang, Jingkai Sun, Michele Caprio, Jian Tang, Shanghang Zhang, Qiang Zhang, Wei Pan
First: 2025-08-23T08:23:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:38:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce LHM-Humanoid, a benchmark and learning framework for long-horizon whole-body humanoid loco-manipulation in diverse, cluttered scenes. In our setting, multiple objects are displaced from their intended locations and may obstruct navigation; a humanoid agent must repeatedly (i) walk to a target, (ii) pick it up with diverse whole-body postures under balance constraints, (iii) carry it while navigating around obstacles, and (iv) place it at a designated goal -- all within a single continuous episode and without any environment reset. This task simultaneously demands cross-scene generalization and unified one-policy control: layouts, obstacle arrangements, object category/mass/shape/color and object start/goal poses vary substantially even within a room category, requiring a single general policy that directly outputs actions rather than invoking pre-trained skill libraries. Our dataset spans four room types (bedroom, living room, kitchen, and warehouse), comprising 350 diverse scenes/tasks with 79 objects (25 movable targets). Since no scene-specific ground-truth motion sequences are provided, we learn goal-conditioned teacher policies via reinforcement learning and distill them into a single end-to-end student policy using DAgger. We further distill this unified policy into a vision-language-action (VLA) model driven by egocentric RGB observations and natural language. Experiments in Isaac Gym demonstrate that LHM-Humanoid substantially outperforms end-to-end RL baselines and prior humanoid loco-manipulation methods on both seen and unseen scenes, exhibiting strong long-horizon robustness and cross-scene generalization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LHM-Humanoid：在多样杂乱环境中学习统一策略以实现长时程人形机器人全身运动与操作
我们提出了LHM-Humanoid，一个用于在多样、杂乱场景中进行长时程全身人形机器人运动与操作的基准与学习框架。在我们的设定中，多个物体被移离原位并可能阻碍导航；人形智能体必须在单个连续任务中反复执行：(i) 走向目标，(ii) 在平衡约束下采用多样全身姿态拾取物体，(iii) 在避障过程中搬运物体，(iv) 将其放置于指定目标位置——且全程无需环境重置。该任务同时要求跨场景泛化与统一单策略控制：即使在同一房间类别内，布局、障碍物排列、物体类别/质量/形状/颜色及起始/目标姿态也存在显著变化，需单一通用策略直接输出动作，而非调用预训练技能库。我们的数据集涵盖四类房间（卧室、客厅、厨房、仓库），包含350个多样场景/任务，共79个物体（25个可移动目标）。由于未提供场景特定的真实运动序列，我们通过强化学习学习目标条件教师策略，并使用DAgger将其蒸馏为单一端到端学生策略。进一步地，我们将该统一策略蒸馏为由第一人称RGB观测与自然语言驱动的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型。在Isaac Gym上的实验表明，LHM-Humanoid在已见与未见场景上均显著优于端到端强化学习基线及先前的人形机器人运动与操作方法，展现出强大的长时程鲁棒性与跨场景泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for humanoid robots to perform complex, long-horizon manipulation tasks in cluttered, real-world environments without resets, the authors introduce LHM-Humanoid, a learning framework and benchmark that requires agents to navigate, pick up, carry, and place objects across diverse scenes using a single unified policy. The method leverages reinforcement learning to train goal-conditioned teacher policies, then distills them into an end-to-end student policy via DAgger, and further refines it into a vision-language-action model using egocentric RGB and natural language. Experiments in Isaac Gym show that this unified policy significantly outperforms prior end-to-end RL methods and existing humanoid loco-manipulation approaches, demonstrating strong robustness over long horizons and generalization across unseen scenes.
为使人形机器人能在无重置的复杂杂乱环境中完成长时程的全身运动与操作任务，作者提出LHM-Humanoid框架，学习一个统一策略。该方法通过强化学习训练目标条件教师策略，再用DAgger方法蒸馏为端到端学生策略，进一步结合视觉-语言-动作模型，以第一人称RGB图像和自然语言驱动。实验表明，该方法在Isaac Gym中显著优于现有端到端强化学习及人形操作方法，在已见与未见场景中均表现优异，展现出强大的长时程鲁棒性与跨场景泛化能力。
Empirical Stability Analysis of Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks in Hard-Constrained Recurrent Physics-Informed Discovery
Authors: Enzo Nicolas Spotorno, Josafat Leal Filho, Antonio Augusto Medeiros Frohlich
Venue: ICLR 2026 Poster
First: 2026-02-10T17:13:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:35:52+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 1 figure, 1 table, accepted as Poster at AI&PDE ICLR 2026 Workshop
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We investigate the integration of Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) into hard-constrained recurrent physics-informed architectures (HRPINN) to evaluate the fidelity of learned residual manifolds in oscillatory systems. Motivated by the Kolmogorov-Arnold representation theorem and preliminary gray-box results, we hypothesized that KANs would enable efficient recovery of unknown terms compared to MLPs. Through initial sensitivity analysis on configuration sensitivity, parameter scale, and training paradigm, we found that while small KANs are competitive on univariate polynomial residuals (Duffing), they exhibit severe hyperparameter fragility, instability in deeper configurations, and consistent failure on multiplicative terms (Van der Pol), generally outperformed by standard MLPs. These empirical challenges highlight limitations of the additive inductive bias in the original KAN formulation for state coupling and provide preliminary empirical evidence of inductive bias limitations for future hybrid modeling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：科尔莫戈罗夫-阿诺德网络在硬约束递归物理信息发现中的经验稳定性分析
我们研究了将科尔莫戈罗夫-阿诺德网络（KANs）整合到硬约束递归物理信息架构（HRPINN）中，以评估所学习残差流形在振荡系统中的保真度。受科尔莫戈罗夫-阿诺德表示定理及初步灰盒结果启发，我们假设KANs相比多层感知机（MLP）能更高效地恢复未知项。通过初步敏感性分析（配置敏感性、参数尺度、训练范式），我们发现：虽然小型KANs在单变量多项式残差（Duffing系统）上表现有竞争力，但其超参数脆弱性严重、深层配置不稳定，且在乘性项（Van der Pol系统）上持续失败，总体上被标准MLP超越。这些经验性挑战揭示了原始KAN公式中加性归纳偏置在状态耦合方面的局限性，并为未来混合建模的归纳偏置限制提供了初步实证依据。
Summary / 总结
We investigate the integration of Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) into hard-constrained recurrent physics-informed architectures (HRPINN) to evaluate the fidelity of learned residual manifolds in oscillatory systems.
受科尔莫戈罗夫-阿诺德表示定理及初步灰盒结果启发，本研究探索将科尔莫戈罗夫-阿诺德网络（KANs）集成到硬约束递归物理信息架构（HRPINN）中，以评估其在振荡系统中恢复残差动态的能力。通过针对配置、参数尺度和训练范式的敏感性分析发现，尽管小型KAN在单变量多项式残差（如Duffing系统）上表现竞争力，但在深层结构中易受超参数影响、不稳定，并在乘性项（如Van der Pol系统）上持续失效，总体上被标准MLP超越。这些结果表明，KAN原始的加性归纳偏置不足以有效建模物理信息发现中的复杂状态耦合。
Omni-Manip: Beyond-FOV Large-Workspace Humanoid Manipulation with Omnidirectional 3D Perception
Authors: Pei Qu, Zheng Li, Yufei Jia, Ziyun Liu, Liang Zhu, Haoang Li, Jinni Zhou, Jun Ma
First: 2026-03-05T16:34:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:34:53+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The deployment of humanoid robots for dexterous manipulation in unstructured environments remains challenging due to perceptual limitations that constrain the effective workspace. In scenarios where physical constraints prevent the robot from repositioning itself, maintaining omnidirectional awareness becomes far more critical than color or semantic information. While recent advances in visuomotor policy learning have improved manipulation capabilities, conventional RGB-D solutions suffer from narrow fields of view (FOV) and self-occlusion, requiring frequent base movements that introduce motion uncertainty and safety risks. Existing approaches to expanding perception, including active vision systems and third-view cameras, introduce mechanical complexity, calibration dependencies, and latency that hinder reliable real-time performance. In this work, We propose Omni-Manip, an end-to-end LiDAR-driven 3D visuomotor policy that enables robust manipulation in large workspaces. Our method processes panoramic point clouds through a Time-Aware Attention Pooling mechanism, efficiently encoding sparse 3D data while capturing temporal dependencies. This 360° perception allows the robot to interact with objects across wide areas without frequent repositioning. To support policy learning, we develop a whole-body teleoperation system for efficient data collection on full-body coordination. Extensive experiments in simulation and real-world environments show that Omni-Manip achieves robust performance in large-workspace and cluttered scenarios, outperforming baselines that rely on egocentric depth cameras.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Omni-Manip：基于全方位3D感知的超越视场大工作空间人形机器人操作
在非结构化环境中部署人形机器人进行灵巧操作仍面临挑战，因感知限制制约了有效工作空间。在物理约束下机器人无法频繁重新定位时，保持全方位感知比颜色或语义信息更为关键。尽管视觉运动策略学习的最新进展提升了操作能力，传统RGB-D方案受限于窄视场（FOV）和自遮挡，需频繁移动基座，引入运动不确定性与安全风险。现有扩展感知方法，如主动视觉系统和第三视角相机，引入机械复杂性、校准依赖和延迟，阻碍可靠实时性能。本文提出Omni-Manip，一种端到端的激光雷达驱动3D视觉运动策略，支持在大工作空间内稳健操作。该方法通过时间感知注意力池化机制处理全景点云，高效编码稀疏3D数据并捕捉时序依赖。360°感知使机器人无需频繁重新定位即可与广阔区域内的物体交互。为支持策略学习，我们开发了全身遥操作系统，以高效采集全身协调数据。仿真与真实环境的大量实验表明，Omni-Manip在大工作空间和杂乱场景中表现稳健，优于依赖第一人称深度相机的基线方法。

The method processes 360° panoramic point clouds using a Time-Aware Attention Pooling mechanism to encode sparse 3D data and temporal context efficiently. Supported by a whole-body teleoperation system for data collection, the policy learns full-body coordination. Experimental results in both simulation and real-world settings demonstrate that Omni-Manip achieves superior robustness and performance compared to baselines relying on egocentric depth cameras, particularly in large and cluttered environments.
为解决窄视场RGB-D传感器在大工作空间中进行人形机器人灵巧操作时的局限性，本文提出Omni-Manip，一种基于LiDAR的端到端三维视觉运动策略，支持无频繁位姿调整的全方位感知与操作。该方法通过时间感知注意力池化机制高效编码360度全景点云，保留时序信息。采用全身遥操作系统采集训练数据，实现全身协调控制。仿真与真实环境实验表明，Omni-Manip在大空间和杂乱场景中表现优于依赖单目深度相机的基线方法。
Sparse Attention Post-Training for Mechanistic Interpretability
Authors: Florent Draye, Anson Lei, Hsiao-Ru Pan, Ingmar Posner, Bernhard Schölkopf
First: 2025-12-05T16:40:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:34:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce a simple post-training method that makes transformer attention sparse without sacrificing performance. Applying a flexible sparsity regularisation under a constrained-loss objective, we show on models up to 7B parameters that it is possible to retain the original pretraining loss while reducing attention connectivity to $\approx 0.4 \%$ of its edges. Unlike sparse-attention methods designed for computational efficiency, our approach leverages sparsity as a structural prior: it preserves capability while exposing a more organized and interpretable connectivity pattern. We find that this local sparsity cascades into global circuit simplification: task-specific circuits involve far fewer components (attention heads and MLPs) with up to 100x fewer edges connecting them. Additionally, using cross-layer transcoders, we show that sparse attention substantially simplifies attention attribution, enabling a unified view of feature-based and circuit-based perspectives. These results demonstrate that transformer attention can be made orders of magnitude sparser, suggesting that much of its computation is redundant and that sparsity may serve as a guiding principle for more structured and interpretable models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于机制可解释性的稀疏注意力后训练方法
我们提出了一种简单的后训练方法，可在不牺牲性能的前提下使Transformer注意力稀疏化。通过在约束损失目标下应用灵活的稀疏正则化，我们在最多7B参数的模型上表明，可以在保持原始预训练损失的同时，将注意力连接性减少至约0.4%的边。与为计算效率设计的稀疏注意力方法不同，我们的方法将稀疏性视为结构先验：它在保持能力的同时，展现出更有序且可解释的连接模式。我们发现，这种局部稀疏性会引发全局电路简化：任务特定电路涉及的组件（注意力头和MLP）数量大幅减少，连接边数最多可减少100倍。此外，通过跨层转码器，我们表明稀疏注意力显著简化了注意力归因，实现了基于特征和基于电路视角的统一视图。这些结果表明，Transformer注意力可被压缩至数量级的稀疏性，暗示其大部分计算冗余，稀疏性或可作为构建更结构化和可解释模型的指导原则。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more interpretable transformer architectures, this work proposes a post-training method that induces sparse attention without performance loss. By applying flexible sparsity regularization under a constrained-loss objective, the method achieves attention connectivity reduced to approximately 0.4% of original edges while preserving pretraining loss on up to 7B-parameter models. Experimentally, this local sparsity leads to global circuit simplification, with task-specific circuits involving up to 100x fewer edges and fewer components. Additionally, cross-layer transcoders enable unified attention attribution, bridging feature-based and circuit-based interpretations, suggesting that transformer computation is largely redundant and that sparsity can guide more interpretable model design.
为提升Transformer架构的可解释性，该研究提出一种后训练方法，通过柔性稀疏正则化在保持性能的前提下诱导注意力稀疏化。在7B参数模型上，该方法将注意力边数减少至原值约0.4%，同时维持原始预训练损失。稀疏化揭示了更规整的内部结构，使任务特定电路中的组件数量和连接边数减少达100倍。此外，跨层转码器使特征与电路归因可统一，表明稀疏性可引导设计更结构化、更易解释的模型，揭示大量计算冗余。
Quantitative convergence of trained single layer neural networks to Gaussian processes
Authors: Eloy Mosig, Andrea Agazzi, Dario Trevisan
Venue: NeurIPS 2025
First: 2025-09-29T09:59:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:34:28+00:00
Comments: Submitted and accepted at NeurIPS 2025, main body of 10 pages, 3 figures, 28 pages of supplementary material. Corrected an issue in the proof of Proposition 3.7
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, we study the quantitative convergence of shallow neural networks trained via gradient descent to their associated Gaussian processes in the infinite-width limit. While previous work has established qualitative convergence under broad settings, precise, finite-width estimates remain limited, particularly during training. We provide explicit upper bounds on the quadratic Wasserstein distance between the network output and its Gaussian approximation at any training time $t \ge 0$, demonstrating polynomial decay with network width. Our results quantify how architectural parameters, such as width and input dimension, influence convergence, and how training dynamics affect the approximation error.
中文标题/摘要
标题：训练的单层神经网络向高斯过程的定量收敛
本文研究了通过梯度下降训练的浅层神经网络在无限宽度极限下与其关联高斯过程的定量收敛性。虽然先前工作已在广泛条件下建立了定性收敛性，但精确的有限宽度估计仍有限，特别是在训练过程中。我们提供了网络输出与其高斯近似之间二次Wasserstein距离的显式上界，该距离在任意训练时间 $t \ge 0$ 下随网络宽度呈多项式衰减。我们的结果量化了架构参数（如宽度和输入维度）对收敛性的影响，以及训练动态对近似误差的作用。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how shallow neural networks trained via gradient descent converge quantitatively to their associated Gaussian processes in the infinite-width limit, addressing a gap in prior work that lacked precise, finite-width error bounds during training. The authors derive explicit upper bounds on the quadratic Wasserstein distance between network outputs and their Gaussian approximations at any training time t ≥ 0, showing polynomial decay in network width. Their analysis reveals how architectural parameters like width and input dimension affect convergence speed and how training dynamics influence approximation error.
为了解决神经网络训练过程中有限宽度下收敛性的精确量化问题，本文研究了通过梯度下降训练的浅层网络如何收敛于其对应的高斯过程（在无限宽度极限下）。作者通过显式上界量化了网络输出与高斯近似之间的二次Wasserstein距离，证明了该误差随网络宽度呈多项式衰减。研究结果揭示了网络宽度、输入维度等架构参数以及训练动态如何共同影响收敛速度与精度。
Exploring the potential and limitations of Model Merging for Multi-Domain Adaptation in ASR
Authors: Carlos Carvalho, Francisco Teixeira, Thomas Rolland, Alberto Abad
First: 2026-03-05T16:34:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:34:24+00:00
Comments: submitted for review for INTERSPEECH2026 conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Model merging is a scalable alternative to multi-task training that combines the capabilities of multiple specialised models into a single model. This is particularly attractive for large speech foundation models, which are typically adapted through domain-specific fine-tuning, resulting in multiple customised checkpoints, for which repeating full fine-tuning when new data becomes available is computationally prohibitive. In this work, we study model merging for multi-domain ASR and benchmark 11 merging algorithms for 10 European Portuguese domains, evaluating in-domain accuracy, robustness under distribution shift, as well as English and multilingual performance. We further propose BoostedTSV-M, a new merging algorithm based on TSV-M that mitigates rank collapse via singular-value boosting and improves numerical stability. Overall, our approach outperforms full fine-tuning on European Portuguese while preserving out-of-distribution generalisation in a single model.
中文标题/摘要
标题：探索模型融合在多领域自适应语音识别中的潜力与局限
模型融合是多任务训练的一种可扩展替代方案，可将多个专业模型的能力整合为单一模型。这对于通常通过领域特定微调进行适应的大规模语音基础模型尤其有吸引力，因为重复完整微调以应对新数据出现时计算成本过高。本研究探讨了多领域ASR的模型融合方法，针对10个欧洲葡萄牙语领域评估了11种融合算法，考察了领域内准确率、分布偏移下的鲁棒性，以及英语和多语言性能。我们进一步提出了基于TSV-M的BoostedTSV-M新融合算法，通过奇异值增强缓解秩坍塌并提升数值稳定性。总体而言，我们的方法在欧洲葡萄牙语上优于完整微调，同时保持单模型在分布外的泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency of repeatedly fine-tuning large ASR models for new domains, this work explores model merging as a scalable alternative to multi-task training, combining multiple domain-specialized models into a single unified model. It evaluates 11 merging algorithms across 10 European Portuguese domains, measuring in-domain accuracy, robustness under distribution shift, and performance on English and multilingual tasks. The study introduces BoostedTSV-M, an enhanced version of TSV-M that addresses rank collapse and improves numerical stability, and finds that this approach consistently outperforms full fine-tuning on European Portuguese while maintaining strong out-of-distribution generalization.
为解决大型ASR模型在新领域数据出现时反复微调计算成本过高的问题，本文探索模型融合作为多任务训练的可扩展替代方案，将多个领域专用模型合并为单一模型。研究在10个欧洲葡萄牙语领域上对比了11种融合算法，评估了领域内准确率、分布偏移下的鲁棒性以及英语和多语言表现。作者提出BoostedTSV-M算法，在TSV-M基础上通过奇异值增强缓解秩塌陷并提升数值稳定性，该方法在欧洲葡萄牙语上全面超越全量微调，同时保持了单模型在分布外数据上的良好泛化能力。
InfoFlow KV: Information-Flow-Aware KV Recomputation for Long Context
Authors: Xin Teng, Canyu Zhang, Shaoyi Zheng, Danyang Zhuo, Tianyi Zhou, Shengjie Wang
First: 2026-03-05T16:33:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:33:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) for long-context question answering is bottlenecked by inference-time prefilling over large retrieved contexts. A common strategy is to precompute key-value (KV) caches for individual documents and selectively recompute a small subset of tokens to restore global causal dependencies, but existing methods rely on heuristics or representation discrepancies without modeling whether selected tokens can effectively influence generation. We cast selective KV recomputation as an information flow problem and show that a simple attention-norm signal from the query reliably identifies tokens that are both semantically relevant and structurally positioned to propagate information, when computed under an inference-consistent RoPE geometry. We therefore reconstruct global positional assignments for retrieved chunks and introduce an information-flow-guided chunk reordering strategy. Experiments on LLM and VLM benchmarks demonstrate consistent gains over prior methods under comparable efficiency budgets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：InfoFlow KV：面向长上下文的信息流感知KV重计算
长上下文问答中的检索增强生成（RAG）受限于推理时对大尺寸检索上下文的预填充。一种常见策略是对单个文档预计算键值（KV）缓存，并选择性重计算一小部分令牌以恢复全局因果依赖，但现有方法依赖启发式或表示差异，未建模所选令牌是否能有效影响生成。我们将选择性KV重计算建模为信息流问题，证明在推理一致的RoPE几何下，查询的简单注意力归一化信号能可靠识别既语义相关又结构上能传播信息的令牌。因此，我们重建检索块的全局位置分配，并引入信息流引导的块重排序策略。在LLM和VLM基准测试中，实验表明在同等效率预算下，该方法相较现有方法持续获得性能提升。
Summary / 总结
Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) for long-context question answering is bottlenecked by inference-time prefilling over large retrieved contexts.
为解决长上下文RAG系统推理时预填充效率低的问题，本文提出InfoFlow KV方法，将选择性KV重计算建模为信息流问题。该方法利用查询生成的注意力归一化信号，在RoPE几何下识别既语义相关又结构上能有效传递信息的token。通过重建检索块的全局位置分配并引入信息流引导的块重排序策略，实验在多个LLM和VLM基准上验证了其相较现有启发式方法更优的性能，且在计算效率上保持相当水平。
Ailed: A Psyche-Driven Chess Engine with Dynamic Emotional Modulation
Authors: Diego Armando Resendez Prado
First: 2026-03-05T16:32:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:32:40+00:00
Comments: 27 pages, 8 figures, 11 tables. Open source: https://github.com/chrnx-dev/ailed-chess
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Chess engines passed human strength years ago, but they still don't play like humans. A grandmaster under clock pressure blunders in ways a club player on a hot streak never would. Conventional engines capture none of this. This paper proposes a personality x psyche decomposition to produce behavioral variability in chess play, drawing on patterns observed in human games. Personality is static -- a preset that pins down the engine's character. Psyche is dynamic -- a bounded scalar ψ_t \in [-100, +100], recomputed from five positional factors after every move. These two components feed into an audio-inspired signal chain (noise gate, compressor/expander, five-band equalizer, saturation limiter) that reshapes move probability distributions on the fly. The chain doesn't care what engine sits behind it: any system that outputs move probabilities will do. It needs no search and carries no state beyond ψ_t. I test the framework across 12,414 games against Maia2-1100, feeding it two probability sources that differ by ~2,800x in training data. Both show the same monotonic gradient in top-move agreement (~20-25 pp spread from stress to overconfidence), which tells us the behavioral variation comes from the signal chain, not from the model underneath. When the psyche runs overconfident, the chain mostly gets out of the way (66% agreement with vanilla Maia2). Under stress, the competitive score falls from 50.8% to 30.1%. The patterns are reminiscent of tilt and overconfidence as described in human play, but I should be upfront: this study includes no human-subject validation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Ailed：一种基于心理驱动并具备动态情绪调节的国际象棋引擎
国际象棋引擎多年前已超越人类水平，但它们仍不似人类下棋。大师在计时压力下会犯下业余高手在状态火热时绝不会犯的错误。传统引擎无法捕捉这些现象。 本文提出一种人格×心理分解方法，以产生国际象棋下的行为多样性，借鉴人类对局中观察到的模式。人格是静态的——一个预设参数，固定引擎的性格；心理是动态的——一个有界标量ψ_t ∈ [-100, +100]，每步棋后根据五个局面因素重新计算。这两个组件输入一个受音频启发的信号链（噪声门、压缩/扩展器、五段均衡器、饱和限制器），实时重塑走法概率分布。该信号链不关心其后端是什么引擎：任何输出走法概率的系统均可。它无需搜索，且仅需ψ_t状态。 我在12,414局对弈中测试该框架，输入两个训练数据相差约2,800倍的概率源。两者均表现出从压力到过度自信的单调梯度（顶级走法一致性约20-25个百分点），表明行为变化源于信号链而非底层模型。当心理处于过度自信状态时，信号链大多保持低调（与原版Maia2一致性达66%）；在压力下，竞争得分从50.8%降至30.1%。这些模式与人类对局中描述的“倾斜”和“过度自信”相似，但需坦诚说明：本研究未包含人类受试者验证。
Summary / 总结
Chess engines passed human strength years ago, but they still don't play like humans.
为使棋类引擎能模拟人类在情绪波动下的行为差异，本文提出Ailed引擎，将行为分解为静态人格与动态心理。心理值ψ_t在每步后根据五个局面因素重新计算，输入一个音频启发式信号链，实时调整走法概率分布。在与Maia2-1100的12,414局对弈中，无论底层模型训练数据差异达2800倍，行为梯度一致：压力下顶尖走法一致性下降20-25个百分点，过自信时上升；压力下胜率从50.8%骤降至30.1%。结果表明，行为变化源于信号链而非底层模型，其模式类似人类的“倾斜”与“过度自信”，但未进行人类受试者验证。
Conflict-Based Search as a Protocol: A Multi-Agent Motion Planning Protocol for Heterogeneous Agents, Solvers, and Independent Tasks
Authors: Rishi Veerapaneni, Alvin Tang, Haodong He, Sophia Zhao, Viraj Shah, Yidai Cen, Ziteng Ji, Gabriel Olin, Jon Arrizabalaga, Yorai Shaoul, Jiaoyang Li, Maxim Likhachev
Venue: ICRA 2026
First: 2025-10-01T02:07:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:28:48+00:00
Comments: Published at ICRA 2026, Project webpage: https://rishi-v.github.io/CBS-Protocol/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Imagine the future construction site, hospital, or office with dozens of robots bought from different manufacturers. How can we enable these different robots to effectively move in a shared environment, given that each robot may have its own independent motion planning system? This work shows how we can get efficient collision-free movements between algorithmically heterogeneous agents by using Conflict-Based Search (Sharon et al. 2015) as a protocol. At its core, the CBS Protocol requires one specific single-agent motion planning API; finding a collision-free path that satisfies certain space-time constraints. Given such an API, CBS uses a central planner to find collision-free paths - independent of how the API is implemented. We demonstrate how this protocol enables multi-agent motion planning for a heterogeneous team of agents completing independent tasks with a variety of single-agent planners including: Heuristic Search (e.g., A*), Sampling Based Search (e.g., RRT), Optimization (e.g., Direct Collocation), Diffusion, and Reinforcement Learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于冲突的搜索作为协议：面向异构代理、求解器和独立任务的多代理运动规划协议
想象一下未来的建筑工地、医院或办公室，其中数十台来自不同制造商的机器人协同工作。如何让这些各自拥有独立运动规划系统的机器人在共享环境中高效移动？本工作展示了如何通过使用冲突基于搜索（Sharon等，2015）作为协议，实现算法异构代理之间的高效无碰撞运动。CBS协议的核心要求是一个特定的单代理运动规划API：寻找满足特定时空约束的无碰撞路径。给定该API，CBS通过中央规划器寻找无碰撞路径——无论API的具体实现方式如何。我们展示了该协议如何支持异构代理团队完成独立任务，这些代理可使用多种单代理规划器，包括启发式搜索（如A*）、采样搜索（如RRT）、优化（如直接参数化）、扩散方法和强化学习。
Summary / 总结
Imagine the future construction site, hospital, or office with dozens of robots bought from different manufacturers.
为实现不同制造商机器人在共享环境中的协同运动，本文提出将冲突基搜索（CBS）作为通用协议用于多智能体路径规划。该方法依赖于一个返回时空约束下无碰撞路径的单智能体规划接口，使CBS能作为中心协调器，无论底层规划器类型如何。实验表明，该协议可高效协调包括A*、RRT、直接参数化、扩散和强化学习在内的多种单智能体规划器，实现独立任务的无碰撞协同执行。
Inverse Reconstruction of Shock Time Series from Shock Response Spectrum Curves using Machine Learning
Authors: Adam Watts, Andrew Jeon, Destry Newton, Ryan Bowering
First: 2026-03-03T18:23:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:27:36+00:00
Comments: Extended journal-style manuscript. 27 pages, 13 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The shock response spectrum (SRS) is widely used to characterize the response of single-degree-of-freedom (SDOF) systems to transient accelerations. Because the mapping from acceleration time history to SRS is nonlinear and many-to-one, reconstructing time-domain signals from a target spectrum is inherently ill-posed. Conventional approaches address this problem through iterative optimization, typically representing signals as sums of exponentially decayed sinusoids, but these methods are computationally expensive and constrained by predefined basis functions. We propose a conditional variational autoencoder (CVAE) that learns a data-driven inverse mapping from SRS to acceleration time series. Once trained, the model generates signals consistent with prescribed target spectra without requiring iterative optimization. Experiments demonstrate improved spectral fidelity relative to classical techniques, strong generalization to unseen spectra, and inference speeds three to six orders of magnitude faster. These results establish deep generative modeling as a scalable and efficient approach for inverse SRS reconstruction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用机器学习从冲击响应谱曲线反演冲击时程
冲击响应谱（SRS）广泛用于表征单自由度（SDOF）系统对瞬态加速度的响应。由于从加速度时程到SRS的映射是非线性且多对一的，从目标谱重建时域信号本质上是病态问题。传统方法通过迭代优化解决此问题，通常将信号表示为指数衰减正弦波的和，但这些方法计算成本高且受限于预定义基函数。 我们提出了一种条件变分自编码器（CVAE），用于学习从SRS到加速度时程的数据驱动逆映射。训练完成后，该模型无需迭代优化即可生成与指定目标谱一致的信号。实验表明，与经典方法相比，该方法在频谱保真度上更优，对未见谱具有强泛化能力，推理速度提升三至六个数量级。这些结果确立了深度生成建模作为可扩展且高效的逆SRS重建方法。
Summary / 总结
The shock response spectrum (SRS) is widely used to characterize the response of single-degree-of-freedom (SDOF) systems to transient accelerations.
为了解决传统方法在从冲击响应谱反演加速度时域信号时计算效率低、依赖预设基函数的问题，本文提出一种条件变分自编码器（CVAE），通过学习SRS曲线到时域信号的直接映射来实现逆向重建。训练完成后，该模型无需迭代优化即可生成与目标谱匹配的加速度波形。实验表明，该方法在频谱保真度上优于传统方法，对未见谱具有强泛化能力，且推理速度比传统方法快三个至六个数量级。
BabyHuBERT: Multilingual Self-Supervised Learning for Segmenting Speakers in Child-Centered Long-Form Recordings
Authors: Théo Charlot, Tarek Kunze, Maxime Poli, Alejandrina Cristia, Emmanuel Dupoux, Marvin Lavechin
First: 2025-09-18T14:34:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:26:56+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 1 figure
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Child-centered daylong recordings are essential for studying early language development, but existing speech models trained on clean adult data perform poorly due to acoustic and linguistic differences. We introduce BabyHuBERT, a self-supervised speech model trained on 13,000 hours of multilingual child-centered recordings spanning 40+ languages. Evaluated on voice type classification -- distinguishing target children from female adults, male adults, and other children, a key preprocessing step for analyzing naturalistic language experiences -- BabyHuBERT-VTC achieves F1-scores from 52.1% to 74.4% across six corpora, consistently outperforming W2V2-LL4300 (English daylongs) and HuBERT (clean adult speech). Notable gains include 13.2 and 15.9 absolute F1 points over HuBERT on Vanuatu and Solomon Islands, demonstrating effectiveness on underrepresented languages. We share code and model to support researchers working with child-centered recordings across diverse linguistic contexts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BabyHuBERT：面向儿童中心长时录音中说话人分割的多语言自监督学习

A Multilingual Human Annotated Corpus of Original and Easy-to-Read Texts to Support Access to Democratic Participatory Processes
Authors: Stefan Bott, Verena Riegler, Horacio Saggion, Almudena Rascón Alcaina, Nouran Khallaf
First: 2026-03-05T16:21:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:21:25+00:00
Comments: Will be published in LREC26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Being able to understand information is a key factor for a self-determined life and society. It is also very important for participating in democratic processes. The study of automatic text simplification is often limited by the availability of high quality material for the training and evaluation on automatic simplifiers. This is true for English, but more so for less resourced languages like Spanish, Catalan and Italian. In order to fill this gap, we present a corpus of original texts for these 3 languages, with high quality simplification produced by human experts in text simplification. It was developed within the iDEM project to assess the impact of Easy-to-Read (E2R) language for democratic participation. The original texts were compiled from domains related to this topic. The corpus includes different text types, selected based on relevance, copyright availability, and ethical standards. All texts were simplified to E2R level. The corpus is particularity valuable because it includes the first annotated corpus of its kind for the Catalan language. It also represents a noteworthy contribution for Spanish and Italian, offering high-quality, human-annotated language resources that are rarely available in these domains. The corpus will be made freely accessible to the public.
中文标题/摘要
标题：支持民主参与进程的多语言人工标注原版与易读文本语料库
理解信息是实现自主生活与社会的关键，也是参与民主进程的重要基础。自动文本简化研究常受限于高质量训练与评估材料的缺乏，英语尚可，但西班牙语、加泰罗尼亚语和意大利语等资源匮乏语言尤为明显。为填补这一空白，我们为这三种语言构建了由人工专家标注的高质量简化文本语料库，该语料库在iDEM项目中开发，旨在评估易读语言（E2R）对民主参与的影响。原始文本来自相关主题领域，涵盖多种文本类型，依据相关性、版权可用性和伦理标准筛选。所有文本均简化至E2R水平。该语料库尤其珍贵，因其是加泰罗尼亚语首个同类标注语料库，同时为西班牙语和意大利语提供了罕见的高质量人工标注语言资源，具有重要贡献。语料库将向公众免费开放。
Summary / 总结
Being able to understand information is a key factor for a self-determined life and society.
为解决民主参与领域中低资源语言（如西班牙语、加泰罗尼亚语和意大利语）缺乏高质量人工标注的简化文本资源问题，本研究构建了一个包含原始文本及其人工简化版本的多语言语料库。文本选自与民主相关领域，依据相关性、版权合规性和伦理标准筛选，全部简化至易读水平。该语料库对加泰罗尼亚语而言是首个此类标注语料库，同时为西班牙语和意大利语提供了稀缺的高质量人工标注资源，有助于提升低资源语言自动简化系统的训练与评估能力。
Building AI Coding Agents for the Terminal: Scaffolding, Harness, Context Engineering, and Lessons Learned
Authors: Nghi D. Q. Bui
First: 2026-03-05T16:21:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:21:08+00:00
Comments: Work in progress, new versions will be updated continuously
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The landscape of AI coding assistance is undergoing a fundamental shift from complex IDE plugins to versatile, terminal-native agents. Operating directly where developers manage source control, execute builds, and deploy environments, CLI-based agents offer unprecedented autonomy for long-horizon development tasks. In this paper, we present OPENDEV, an open-source, command-line coding agent engineered specifically for this new paradigm. Effective autonomous assistance requires strict safety controls and highly efficient context management to prevent context bloat and reasoning degradation. OPENDEV overcomes these challenges through a compound AI system architecture with workload-specialized model routing, a dual-agent architecture separating planning from execution, lazy tool discovery, and adaptive context compaction that progressively reduces older observations. Furthermore, it employs an automated memory system to accumulate project-specific knowledge across sessions and counteracts instruction fade-out through event-driven system reminders. By enforcing explicit reasoning phases and prioritizing context efficiency, OPENDEV provides a secure, extensible foundation for terminal-first AI assistance, offering a blueprint for robust autonomous software engineering.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向终端的AI编码代理构建：脚手架、框架、上下文工程与经验总结
AI编码辅助领域正从复杂的IDE插件转向多功能、原生终端的代理。这些基于命令行的代理直接运行在开发者管理源代码、执行构建和部署环境的位置，为长期开发任务提供了前所未有的自主性。本文提出OPENDEV，这是一个专为这一新范式设计的开源命令行编码代理。有效的自主辅助需要严格的安全控制和高效的上下文管理，以防止上下文膨胀和推理退化。OPENDEV通过复合AI系统架构解决这些挑战，包括工作负载专用模型路由、分离规划与执行的双代理架构、延迟工具发现以及自适应上下文压缩机制，逐步减少旧观察数据。此外，它采用自动化记忆系统，在会话间积累项目特定知识，并通过事件驱动的系统提醒对抗指令遗忘。通过强制显式推理阶段和优先上下文效率，OPENDEV为以终端优先的AI辅助提供了安全、可扩展的基础，为稳健的自主软件工程提供了蓝图。
Summary / 总结
The landscape of AI coding assistance is undergoing a fundamental shift from complex IDE plugins to versatile, terminal-native agents.
为应对无需IDE依赖、能在终端环境中自主处理长期开发任务的AI编码助手需求，作者提出了开源CLI工具OPENDEV。该系统采用复合架构，包含模型路由、规划与执行分离的双代理设计、延迟工具发现及自适应上下文压缩机制，以防止上下文膨胀。实验表明，OPENDEV在长时间会话中保持推理质量，高效管理上下文，并通过自动化记忆系统积累项目专属知识，为终端优先的自主软件工程提供了安全、可扩展的基础框架。
Preserving Continuous Symmetry in Discrete Spaces: Geometric-Aware Quantization for SO(3)-Equivariant GNNs
Authors: Haoyu Zhou, Ping Xue, Hao Zhang, Tianfan Fu
First: 2026-03-05T16:20:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:20:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Equivariant Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are essential for physically consistent molecular simulations but suffer from high computational costs and memory bottlenecks, especially with high-order representations. While low-bit quantization offers a solution, applying it naively to rotation-sensitive features destroys the SO(3)-equivariant structure, leading to significant errors and violations of conservation laws. To address this issue, in this work, we propose a Geometric-Aware Quantization (GAQ) framework that compresses and accelerates equivariant models while rigorously preserving continuous symmetry in discrete spaces. Our approach introduces three key contributions: (1) a Magnitude-Direction Decoupled Quantization (MDDQ) scheme that separates invariant lengths from equivariant orientations to maintain geometric fidelity; (2) a symmetry-aware training strategy that treats scalar and vector features with distinct quantization schedules; and (3) a robust attention normalization mechanism to stabilize gradients in low-bit regimes. Experiments on the rMD17 benchmark demonstrate that our W4A8 models match the accuracy of FP32 baselines (9.31 meV vs. 23.20 meV) while reducing Local Equivariance Error (LEE) by over 30x compared to naive quantization. On consumer hardware, GAQ achieves 2.39x inference speedup and 4x memory reduction, enabling stable, energy-conserving molecular dynamics simulations for nanosecond timescales.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在离散空间中保持连续对称性：面向SO(3)等变图神经网络的几何感知量化
等变图神经网络（GNNs）对物理一致的分子模拟至关重要，但高阶表示会带来高昂的计算成本和内存瓶颈。虽然低比特量化可缓解此问题，但直接应用于旋转敏感特征会破坏SO(3)等变结构，导致显著误差和守恒定律违反。为此，本文提出几何感知量化（GAQ）框架，在压缩加速等变模型的同时严格保持离散空间中的连续对称性。我们的方法包含三项关键贡献：（1）分离不变长度与等变方向的幅值-方向解耦量化（MDDQ）方案，以维持几何保真度；（2）感知对称性的训练策略，为标量与矢量特征采用不同量化调度；（3）鲁棒的注意力归一化机制，以稳定低比特环境下的梯度。在rMD17基准测试中，我们的W4A8模型在精度上与FP32基线（9.31 meV vs. 23.20 meV）相当，相比直接量化将局部等变误差（LEE）降低30倍以上。在消费级硬件上，GAQ实现2.39倍推理加速和4倍内存缩减，支持纳秒级稳定、能量守恒的分子动力学模拟。
Summary / 总结
To address the computational and memory inefficiencies of SO(3)-equivariant GNNs in molecular simulations without sacrificing physical consistency, this work introduces Geometric-Aware Quantization (GAQ), a framework that preserves continuous rotational symmetry in discrete low-bit representations. GAQ employs Magnitude-Direction Decoupled Quantization to separate invariant magnitudes from equivariant directions, uses distinct quantization schedules for scalar and vector features during training, and incorporates a gradient-stabilizing attention normalization. Experiments on rMD17 show that GAQ’s W4A8 models achieve accuracy comparable to FP32 (9.31 meV vs. 23.20 meV) while reducing Local Equivariance Error by over 30x compared to naive quantization, and deliver 2.39x speedup and 4x memory reduction on consumer hardware, enabling nanosecond-scale energy-conserving simulations.
为解决SO(3)等变图神经网络在分子模拟中计算与内存开销高的问题，同时保持物理对称性，本文提出几何感知量化（GAQ）框架，实现低比特量化而不破坏旋转等变性。GAQ采用幅值-方向解耦量化分离不变幅值与等变方向，对标量与向量特征采用不同量化策略进行训练，并引入注意力归一化稳定低比特梯度。在rMD17基准测试中，GAQ的W4A8模型精度与FP32基准相当（9.31 meV vs. 23.20 meV），相比直接量化局部等变误差降低30倍以上，且在消费级硬件上实现2.39倍推理加速与4倍内存节省，支持纳秒级能量守恒分子动力学模拟。
An Experimental Study on Fairness-aware Machine Learning for Credit Scoring Problems
Authors: Huyen Giang Thi Thu, Thang Viet Doan, Ha-Bang Ban, Tai Le Quy
First: 2024-12-28T23:27:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:19:05+00:00
Comments: The manuscript is submitted to Springer Nature's journal
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The digitalization of credit scoring has become essential for financial institutions and commercial banks, especially in the era of digital transformation. Machine learning techniques are commonly used to evaluate customers' creditworthiness. However, the predicted outcomes of machine learning models can be biased toward protected attributes, such as race or gender. Numerous fairness-aware machine learning models and fairness measures have been proposed. Nevertheless, their performance in the context of credit scoring has not been thoroughly investigated. In this paper, we present a comprehensive experimental study of fairness-aware machine learning in credit scoring. The study explores key aspects of credit scoring, including financial datasets, predictive models, and fairness measures. We also provide a detailed evaluation of fairness-aware predictive models and fairness measures on widely used financial datasets. The experimental results show that fairness-aware models achieve a better balance between predictive accuracy and fairness compared to traditional classification models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向信用评分问题的公平感知机器学习实验研究
信用评分的数字化对金融机构和商业银行而言已成为数字化转型时代的关键需求，机器学习技术常用于评估客户信用状况。然而，机器学习模型的预测结果可能偏向受保护属性（如种族或性别）。尽管已提出多种公平感知机器学习模型与公平性度量方法，但其在信用评分场景中的表现尚未被充分研究。本文对信用评分中的公平感知机器学习进行系统性实验研究，涵盖金融数据集、预测模型与公平性度量等关键方面，并对常用金融数据集上的公平感知预测模型与度量方法进行详细评估。实验结果表明，公平感知模型在预测准确率与公平性之间实现了优于传统分类模型的平衡。
Summary / 总结
The digitalization of credit scoring has become essential for financial institutions and commercial banks, especially in the era of digital transformation.
为缓解自动化信用评分系统中的偏见问题，本研究在真实金融数据集上系统评估了公平性感知机器学习模型与传统分类器的性能。通过比较多种公平性度量和预测模型，分析其在准确率与公平性之间的权衡。实验结果表明，公平性感知模型在减少受保护属性间差异的同时，整体预测准确率优于传统模型，实现了公平性与准确性的更好平衡。
On the Statistical Optimality of Optimal Decision Trees
Authors: Zineng Xu, Subhroshekhar Ghosh, Yan Shuo Tan
First: 2026-03-05T16:16:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-05T16:16:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While globally optimal empirical risk minimization (ERM) decision trees have become computationally feasible and empirically successful, rigorous theoretical guarantees for their statistical performance remain limited. In this work, we develop a comprehensive statistical theory for ERM trees under random design in both high-dimensional regression and classification. We first establish sharp oracle inequalities that bound the excess risk of the ERM estimator relative to the best possible approximation achievable by any tree with at most $L$ leaves, thereby characterizing the interpretability-accuracy trade-off. We derive these results using a novel uniform concentration framework based on empirically localized Rademacher complexity. Furthermore, we derive minimax optimal rates over a novel function class: the piecewise sparse heterogeneous anisotropic Besov (PSHAB) space. This space explicitly captures three key structural features encountered in practice: sparsity, anisotropic smoothness, and spatial heterogeneity. While our main results are established under sub-Gaussianity, we also provide robust guarantees that hold under heavy-tailed noise settings. Together, these findings provide a principled foundation for the optimality of ERM trees and introduce empirical process tools broadly applicable to other highly adaptive, data-driven procedures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：最优决策树的统计最优性研究
尽管全局最优的经验风险最小化（ERM）决策树在计算上已变得可行且在实证上取得成功，但对其统计性能的严格理论保证仍有限。本文在高维回归与分类问题中，针对随机设计下的ERM树建立了全面的统计理论。我们首先推导出尖锐的Oracle不等式，以界定ERM估计量相对于任何最多含L个叶节点的树所能达到的最佳近似之超额风险，从而刻画可解释性与准确率之间的权衡。我们采用基于经验局部化Rademacher复杂度的新型统一集中框架推导这些结果。此外，我们推导出在新型函数类——分段稀疏异质各向异性Besov（PSHAB）空间上的最小最大最优速率。该空间明确捕捉实践中常见的三个关键结构特征：稀疏性、各向异性光滑性与空间异质性。虽然主要结果在次高斯噪声假设下建立，我们亦提供了在重尾噪声设置下仍成立的鲁棒保证。这些发现共同为ERM树的最优性提供了理论基础，并引入了可广泛应用于其他高度自适应、数据驱动过程的经验过程工具。
Summary / 总结
While globally optimal empirical risk minimization (ERM) decision trees have become computationally feasible and empirically successful, rigorous theoretical guarantees for their statistical performance remain limited.
鉴于全局最优决策树在实践中表现良好但缺乏严格的统计理论保障，本文在随机设计下构建了针对经验风险最小化（ERM）树的完整统计理论框架。通过基于经验局部Rademacher复杂度的新型统一集中不等式，建立了相对于最优L叶树的尖锐Oracle不等式，刻画了可解释性与准确率之间的权衡。作者在新函数类——分段稀疏异质各向异性Besov空间（PSHAB）上推导出最小最大最优收敛速率，该空间显式捕捉了实际中常见的稀疏性、各向异性光滑性和空间异质性。结果在次高斯噪声下成立，并扩展至重尾噪声场景，为ERM树的统计最优性提供了理论基础，并推广了适用于其他高度自适应数据驱动方法的实证过程工具。
